`src/services/leaderboard-export.service.ts`:

```ts
export interface LeaderboardExportRow {
  rank: number;
  playerWallet: string;
  score: number;
  prizeShareBps: number;
  prizeAmount: number;
  asset: string;
  status: string;
}

export interface LeaderboardJsonExport {
  tournamentId: string;
  title: string;
  prizeAsset: string;
  totalPrizePool: number;
  exportedAt: number;
  recordCount: number;
  rankings: LeaderboardExportRow[];
}

/**
 * Service generating standardized CSV and JSON leaderboard reports for
 * organizers, audit trails, and tax/accounting exports.
 */
export class LeaderboardExportService {
  /**
   * Generates formatted CSV string from leaderboard rows.
   */
  public static exportCsv(tournamentId: string, entries: LeaderboardExportRow[]): string {
    const headers = [
      'Rank',
      'Player Wallet',
      'Score',
      'Prize Share (Bps)',
      'Prize Amount',
      'Asset',
      'Status',
    ];
    const lines = [headers.join(',')];

    for (const e of entries) {
      lines.push(
        [
          e.rank,
          `"${e.playerWallet}"`,
          e.score,
          e.prizeShareBps,
          e.prizeAmount.toFixed(4),
          `"${e.asset}"`,
          `"${e.status}"`,
        ].join(','),
      );
    }

    return lines.join('\n');
  }

  /**
   * Generates formatted JSON string containing full tournament leaderboard and metadata.
   */
  public static exportJson(
    tournamentId: string,
    title: string,
    prizeAsset: string,
    totalPrizePool: number,
    entries: LeaderboardExportRow[],
    exportedAt: number = Date.now(),
  ): string {
    const payload: LeaderboardJsonExport = {
      tournamentId,
      title,
      prizeAsset,
      totalPrizePool,
      exportedAt,
      recordCount: entries.length,
      rankings: entries,
    };

    return JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2);
  }

  /**
   * Parses exported CSV back into structured rows.
   */
  public static parseCsv(csv: string): LeaderboardExportRow[] {
    const lines = csv.trim().split('\n');
    if (lines.length <= 1) return [];

    const rows: LeaderboardExportRow[] = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
      const parts = lines[i].split(',').map((p) => p.replace(/^"|"$/g, '').trim());
      if (parts.length >= 7) {
        rows.push({
          rank: parseInt(parts[0], 10),
          playerWallet: parts[1],
          score: parseInt(parts[2], 10),
          prizeShareBps: parseInt(parts[3], 10),
          prizeAmount: parseFloat(parts[4]),
          asset: parts[5],
          status: parts[6],
        });
      }
    }

    return rows;
  }
}
```

`src/services/leaderboard-relayer.service.ts`:

```ts
import {
  LeaderboardEntry,
  LiveLeaderboardSnapshot,
  RankDeltaNotification,
} from '../types/leaderboard-relayer';

export class LeaderboardRelayerService {
  private tournamentLeaderboards: Map<number, LeaderboardEntry[]> = new Map();
  private subscribers: ((delta: RankDeltaNotification) => void)[] = [];

  public updatePlayerScore(
    tournamentId: number,
    playerWallet: string,
    score: number,
    winRateBps: number = 0,
  ): RankDeltaNotification | null {
    let board = this.tournamentLeaderboards.get(tournamentId) || [];
    const prevIndex = board.findIndex((e) => e.playerWallet === playerWallet);
    const previousRank = prevIndex !== -1 ? board[prevIndex].rank : board.length + 1;
    const previousScore = prevIndex !== -1 ? board[prevIndex].score : 0;
    const matchesPlayed = prevIndex !== -1 ? board[prevIndex].matchesPlayed + 1 : 1;

    // Filter out existing entry to re-sort
    board = board.filter((e) => e.playerWallet !== playerWallet);
    board.push({
      rank: 0,
      playerWallet,
      score,
      matchesPlayed,
      winRateBps,
      lastActiveTimestamp: Date.now(),
    });

    // Sort descending by score
    board.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
    board.forEach((entry, idx) => {
      entry.rank = idx + 1;
    });

    this.tournamentLeaderboards.set(tournamentId, board);

    const newIndex = board.findIndex((e) => e.playerWallet === playerWallet);
    const newRank = newIndex + 1;

    const notification: RankDeltaNotification = {
      type: 'RANK_UPDATE',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet,
      previousRank,
      newRank,
      scoreDelta: score - previousScore,
      newScore: score,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };

    // Broadcast to live listeners
    for (const sub of this.subscribers) {
      try {
        sub(notification);
      } catch {
        // Ignore subscriber delivery failure
      }
    }

    return notification;
  }

  public getSnapshot(tournamentId: number, limit: number = 20): LiveLeaderboardSnapshot {
    const board = this.tournamentLeaderboards.get(tournamentId) || [];
    return {
      tournamentId,
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
      totalParticipants: board.length,
      topRanked: board.slice(0, limit),
    };
  }

  public subscribe(listener: (delta: RankDeltaNotification) => void): () => void {
    this.subscribers.push(listener);
    return () => {
      this.subscribers = this.subscribers.filter((s) => s !== listener);
    };
  }

  public clear(): void {
    this.tournamentLeaderboards.clear();
    this.subscribers = [];
  }
}

export const leaderboardRelayer = new LeaderboardRelayerService();

```

`src/services/ledger-sync.service.ts`:

```ts
import { LedgerSyncStatus } from '../types/ledger-sync';
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

export class LedgerSequenceSyncService {
  private horizonUrl: string;
  private rpcUrl: string;
  private maxAllowedLag: number = 3;

  constructor(horizonUrl: string = CONFIG.HORIZON_URL, rpcUrl: string = CONFIG.SOROBAN_RPC_URL) {
    this.horizonUrl = horizonUrl;
    this.rpcUrl = rpcUrl;
  }

  public async fetchHorizonLedger(): Promise<number> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${this.horizonUrl}/ledgers?order=desc&limit=1`);
      if (!res.ok) return 0;
      const data = (await res.json()) as any;
      const records = data?._embedded?.records;
      if (Array.isArray(records) && records.length > 0) {
        return parseInt(records[0].sequence) || 0;
      }
      return 0;
    } catch {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  public async fetchSorobanLedger(): Promise<number> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(this.rpcUrl, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          jsonrpc: '2.0',
          id: 1,
          method: 'getLatestLedger',
        }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) return 0;
      const data = (await res.json()) as any;
      return data?.result?.sequence || 0;
    } catch {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  public async checkSync(): Promise<LedgerSyncStatus> {
    const [horizonSeq, sorobanSeq] = await Promise.all([
      this.fetchHorizonLedger(),
      this.fetchSorobanLedger(),
    ]);

    const lag = Math.abs(horizonSeq - sorobanSeq);
    const isSynced = horizonSeq > 0 && sorobanSeq > 0 && lag <= this.maxAllowedLag;

    let warning: string | undefined;
    if (horizonSeq === 0 || sorobanSeq === 0) {
      warning = 'Failed to fetch ledger sequence from one or more providers';
    } else if (lag > this.maxAllowedLag) {
      warning = `Ledger sequence lag of ${lag} exceeds maximum allowed lag threshold of ${this.maxAllowedLag}`;
    }

    return {
      isSynced,
      horizonLedgerSequence: horizonSeq,
      sorobanLedgerSequence: sorobanSeq,
      sequenceLag: lag,
      maxAllowedLag: this.maxAllowedLag,
      lastCheckedAt: Date.now(),
      warning,
    };
  }
}

export const ledgerSequenceSync = new LedgerSequenceSyncService();

```

`src/services/lifecycle.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Tournament, TournamentState } from '../types';
import { AppError } from '../middleware/error.middleware';

/**
 * Valid state transition adjacency map for tournament lifecycles.
 */
export const ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Record<TournamentState, readonly TournamentState[]> = {
  DRAFT: ['FUNDING', 'CANCELLED'],
  FUNDING: ['OPEN', 'CANCELLED'],
  OPEN: ['FULL', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'CANCELLED'],
  FULL: ['IN_PROGRESS', 'CANCELLED'],
  IN_PROGRESS: ['RESULTS_PENDING', 'CANCELLED'],
  RESULTS_PENDING: ['VERIFYING', 'CANCELLED'],
  VERIFYING: ['VERIFIED', 'CANCELLED'],
  VERIFIED: ['FINALIZED', 'CANCELLED'],
  FINALIZED: ['SETTLING', 'CANCELLED'],
  SETTLING: ['SETTLED', 'CANCELLED'],
  SETTLED: ['COMPLETED'],
  COMPLETED: [],
  CANCELLED: [],
};

export interface StateTransitionEvent {
  tournamentId: string;
  fromState: TournamentState;
  toState: TournamentState;
  timestamp: number;
  reason?: string;
  triggeredBy?: string;
}

export class LifecycleService {
  /**
   * Checks whether a state transition is topologically allowed.
   */
  public static canTransition(from: TournamentState, to: TournamentState): boolean {
    const allowed = ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[from];
    return allowed ? allowed.includes(to) : false;
  }

  /**
   * Asserts that a state transition is valid and all domain invariants are satisfied.
   * Throws AppError(400) if transition or preconditions fail.
   */
  public static validateTransition(
    tournament: Tournament,
    toState: TournamentState,
    reason?: string,
  ): StateTransitionEvent {
    const fromState = tournament.state;

    // Check terminal states
    if (fromState === 'COMPLETED' || fromState === 'CANCELLED') {
      throw new AppError(
        `Cannot transition tournament ${tournament.id} from terminal state '${fromState}' to '${toState}'.`,
        400,
        'ILLEGAL_LIFECYCLE_TRANSITION',
      );
    }

    if (!this.canTransition(fromState, toState)) {
      throw new AppError(
        `Illegal state transition from '${fromState}' to '${toState}'. Allowed transitions: [${ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[fromState].join(', ')}].`,
        400,
        'INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION',
      );
    }

    // Specific domain invariant validation
    if (toState === 'OPEN') {
      if (!tournament.fundingTxHash && tournament.prizePoolAmount <= 0) {
        throw new AppError(
          `Cannot transition tournament to OPEN without verified on-chain prize funding.`,
          400,
          'UNFUNDED_TOURNAMENT_ERROR',
        );
      }
    }

    if (toState === 'IN_PROGRESS') {
      if (tournament.currentParticipants < 1) {
        throw new AppError(
          `Cannot start tournament without at least one registered participant.`,
          400,
          'INSUFFICIENT_PARTICIPANTS',
        );
      }
    }

    if (toState === 'SETTLING') {
      if (!tournament.finalRankingHash) {
        throw new AppError(
          `Cannot settle tournament without a verified final ranking hash.`,
          400,
          'MISSING_RANKING_HASH',
        );
      }
    }

    return {
      tournamentId: tournament.id,
      fromState,
      toState,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      reason,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Applies validated state transition to tournament object.
   */
  public static applyTransition(
    tournament: Tournament,
    toState: TournamentState,
    reason?: string,
  ): StateTransitionEvent {
    const event = this.validateTransition(tournament, toState, reason);
    tournament.state = toState;
    tournament.updatedAt = event.timestamp;
    return event;
  }
}

```

`src/services/logger.service.ts`:

```ts
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

export type LogLevel = 'DEBUG' | 'INFO' | 'WARN' | 'ERROR';

export interface LogEntry {
  timestamp: string;
  level: LogLevel;
  message: string;
  context?: Record<string, unknown>;
  error?: {
    name?: string;
    message?: string;
    stack?: string;
  };
}

export class LoggerService {
  private static ringBuffer: LogEntry[] = [];
  private static readonly MAX_BUFFER_SIZE = 500;
  private static minLevel: LogLevel = 'INFO';

  private static readonly LEVEL_PRIORITY: Record<LogLevel, number> = {
    DEBUG: 10,
    INFO: 20,
    WARN: 30,
    ERROR: 40,
  };

  public static setMinLevel(level: LogLevel): void {
    this.minLevel = level;
  }

  public static getMinLevel(): LogLevel {
    return this.minLevel;
  }

  private static shouldLog(level: LogLevel): boolean {
    return this.LEVEL_PRIORITY[level] >= this.LEVEL_PRIORITY[this.minLevel];
  }

  private static record(entry: LogEntry): void {
    if (this.ringBuffer.length >= this.MAX_BUFFER_SIZE) {
      this.ringBuffer.shift();
    }
    this.ringBuffer.push(entry);

    if (!this.shouldLog(entry.level)) return;

    // In production or structured logging mode, emit JSON string
    if (CONFIG.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(entry);
      if (entry.level === 'ERROR') {
        console.error(serialized);
      } else if (entry.level === 'WARN') {
        console.warn(serialized);
      } else {
        console.log(serialized);
      }
    }
  }

  public static debug(message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
    this.record({
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      level: 'DEBUG',
      message,
      context,
    });
  }

  public static info(message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
    this.record({
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      level: 'INFO',
      message,
      context,
    });
  }

  public static warn(message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
    this.record({
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      level: 'WARN',
      message,
      context,
    });
  }

  public static error(
    message: string,
    err?: Error | unknown,
    context?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ): void {
    const errorDetails =
      err instanceof Error
        ? { name: err.name, message: err.message, stack: err.stack }
        : err
          ? { message: String(err) }
          : undefined;

    this.record({
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      level: 'ERROR',
      message,
      error: errorDetails,
      context,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Creates a scoped logger that automatically merges contextual metadata (e.g. requestId, wallet, tournamentId)
   */
  public static withContext(baseContext: Record<string, unknown>) {
    return {
      debug: (msg: string, ctx?: Record<string, unknown>) =>
        LoggerService.debug(msg, { ...baseContext, ...ctx }),
      info: (msg: string, ctx?: Record<string, unknown>) =>
        LoggerService.info(msg, { ...baseContext, ...ctx }),
      warn: (msg: string, ctx?: Record<string, unknown>) =>
        LoggerService.warn(msg, { ...baseContext, ...ctx }),
      error: (msg: string, err?: Error | unknown, ctx?: Record<string, unknown>) =>
        LoggerService.error(msg, err, { ...baseContext, ...ctx }),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Returns recent log entries from the in-memory ring buffer.
   */
  public static getHistory(limit: number = 50): LogEntry[] {
    return this.ringBuffer.slice(-limit);
  }

  public static clearHistory(): void {
    this.ringBuffer = [];
  }
}

```

`src/services/match-attestation.service.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from 'crypto';

export type AttestationLifecycleStatus = 'PENDING' | 'ATTESTED' | 'DISPUTED' | 'SETTLED';

export interface MatchAttestation {
  attestationId: string;
  matchId: string;
  tournamentId: string;
  playerWallet: string;
  score: number;
  resultHash: string;
  attestationDigest: string;
  status: AttestationLifecycleStatus;
  txHash?: string;
  ledger?: number;
  disputeReason?: string;
  createdAt: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}

/**
 * Service managing cryptographic match attestations and on-chain lifecycle transitions.
 */
export class MatchAttestationService {
  private static attestations: Map<string, MatchAttestation> = new Map();

  /**
   * Resets internal store (used in unit testing).
   */
  public static reset(): void {
    this.attestations.clear();
  }

  /**
   * Computes deterministic SHA-256 digest for an attestation.
   */
  public static computeDigest(
    matchId: string,
    tournamentId: string,
    playerWallet: string,
    score: number,
    resultHash: string,
  ): string {
    const payload = `${matchId}:${tournamentId}:${playerWallet}:${score}:${resultHash}`;
    return createHash('sha256').update(payload).digest('hex');
  }

  /**
   * Creates a new pending attestation record.
   */
  public static createAttestation(
    matchId: string,
    tournamentId: string,
    playerWallet: string,
    score: number,
    resultHash: string,
    createdAt: number = Date.now(),
  ): MatchAttestation {
    const attestationDigest = this.computeDigest(
      matchId,
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet,
      score,
      resultHash,
    );
    const attestationId = `attest-${attestationDigest.slice(0, 16)}`;

    const attestation: MatchAttestation = {
      attestationId,
      matchId,
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet,
      score,
      resultHash,
      attestationDigest,
      status: 'PENDING',
      createdAt,
      updatedAt: createdAt,
    };

    this.attestations.set(attestationId, attestation);
    return attestation;
  }

  /**
   * Updates attestation state to ATTESTED with on-chain Soroban transaction details.
   */
  public static markAttested(
    attestationId: string,
    txHash: string,
    ledger: number,
    updatedAt: number = Date.now(),
  ): MatchAttestation | null {
    const record = this.attestations.get(attestationId);
    if (!record) return null;

    record.status = 'ATTESTED';
    record.txHash = txHash;
    record.ledger = ledger;
    record.updatedAt = updatedAt;

    this.attestations.set(attestationId, record);
    return record;
  }

  /**
   * Updates attestation state to DISPUTED when a challenge is filed.
   */
  public static markDisputed(
    attestationId: string,
    disputeReason: string,
    updatedAt: number = Date.now(),
  ): MatchAttestation | null {
    const record = this.attestations.get(attestationId);
    if (!record) return null;

    record.status = 'DISPUTED';
    record.disputeReason = disputeReason;
    record.updatedAt = updatedAt;

    this.attestations.set(attestationId, record);
    return record;
  }

  /**
   * Updates attestation state to SETTLED upon tournament payout finalization.
   */
  public static markSettled(
    attestationId: string,
    updatedAt: number = Date.now(),
  ): MatchAttestation | null {
    const record = this.attestations.get(attestationId);
    if (!record) return null;

    record.status = 'SETTLED';
    record.updatedAt = updatedAt;

    this.attestations.set(attestationId, record);
    return record;
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves an attestation by ID.
   */
  public static getAttestation(attestationId: string): MatchAttestation | null {
    return this.attestations.get(attestationId) || null;
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves all attestations for a tournament.
   */
  public static getAttestationsForTournament(tournamentId: string): MatchAttestation[] {
    return Array.from(this.attestations.values()).filter((a) => a.tournamentId === tournamentId);
  }
}
```

`src/services/match-replay-audit.service.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from 'crypto';
import { StoreService } from './store.service';
import {
  MatchTickAnomalyService,
  AnomalyScanReport,
  MatchTickItem,
} from './match-tick-anomaly.service';
import { MatchSession, GameResult } from '../types';

export interface MatchAuditPackage {
  packageId: string;
  matchId: string;
  tournamentId: string;
  exportedAt: number;
  session: MatchSession | null;
  result: GameResult | null;
  anomalyAnalysis: AnomalyScanReport;
  packageChecksum: string;
}

/**
 * Service generating standalone forensic audit packages for contested matches.
 */
export class MatchReplayAuditService {
  /**
   * Generates a forensic diagnostic package for a match session.
   */
  public static generatePackage(matchId: string, now: number = Date.now()): MatchAuditPackage {
    const session = StoreService.getMatchSession(matchId) || null;
    const result = StoreService.getGameResult(matchId) || null;

    const events = session?.events || [];
    const ticks: MatchTickItem[] = events.map((e, idx) => ({
      tickIndex: e.seq ?? idx,
      playerIndex: 0,
      action: e.type,
      timestamp: e.timestamp,
    }));

    const anomalyAnalysis = MatchTickAnomalyService.scanTicks(ticks, session?.endTime || now);

    const canonicalString = JSON.stringify({
      matchId,
      tournamentId: session?.tournamentId || 'unknown',
      session,
      result,
      anomalyAnalysis,
      exportedAt: now,
    });

    const packageChecksum = createHash('sha256').update(canonicalString).digest('hex');
    const packageId = `audit-pkg-${matchId}-${now}`;

    return {
      packageId,
      matchId,
      tournamentId: session?.tournamentId || 'unknown',
      exportedAt: now,
      session,
      result,
      anomalyAnalysis,
      packageChecksum,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Verifies the SHA-256 checksum of an audit package.
   */
  public static verifyPackageChecksum(pkg: MatchAuditPackage): boolean {
    const canonicalString = JSON.stringify({
      matchId: pkg.matchId,
      tournamentId: pkg.tournamentId,
      session: pkg.session,
      result: pkg.result,
      anomalyAnalysis: pkg.anomalyAnalysis,
      exportedAt: pkg.exportedAt,
    });

    const computed = createHash('sha256').update(canonicalString).digest('hex');
    return computed === pkg.packageChecksum;
  }
}
```

`src/services/match-tick-anomaly.service.ts`:

```ts
export interface MatchTickItem {
  tickIndex: number;
  playerIndex: number;
  action: string;
  payload?: {
    x?: number;
    y?: number;
  };
  timestamp: number;
}

export interface AnomalyDetail {
  tickIndex: number;
  rule: 'RAPID_FIRE' | 'VELOCITY_SPIKE' | 'FUTURE_TIMESTAMP' | 'INVALID_TICK_ORDER';
  message: string;
}

export interface AnomalyScanReport {
  hasAnomalies: boolean;
  anomalyCount: number;
  anomalies: AnomalyDetail[];
}

/**
 * Service evaluating game match tick streams for anti-cheat anomalies,
 * impossible physical velocities, and rate-limit violations.
 */
export class MatchTickAnomalyService {
  public static readonly MIN_FIRE_COOLDOWN_MS = 150;
  public static readonly MAX_VELOCITY_PER_TICK = 100; // max coordinate jump per tick

  /**
   * Scans a tick stream for anomalies.
   */
  public static scanTicks(
    ticks: MatchTickItem[],
    matchEndTime: number = Infinity,
  ): AnomalyScanReport {
    const anomalies: AnomalyDetail[] = [];
    let lastFireTimestamp = -Infinity;
    let lastPosition: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;
    let lastTickIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i < ticks.length; i++) {
      const t = ticks[i];

      // Sequential tick order check
      if (t.tickIndex <= lastTickIndex) {
        anomalies.push({
          tickIndex: t.tickIndex,
          rule: 'INVALID_TICK_ORDER',
          message: `Tick index ${t.tickIndex} is not strictly greater than previous ${lastTickIndex}`,
        });
      }
      lastTickIndex = t.tickIndex;

      // Future timestamp check
      if (t.timestamp > matchEndTime) {
        anomalies.push({
          tickIndex: t.tickIndex,
          rule: 'FUTURE_TIMESTAMP',
          message: `Tick timestamp ${t.timestamp} exceeds match end time ${matchEndTime}`,
        });
      }

      // Rapid fire cooldown check
      if (t.action === 'FIRE_WATER_BALLOON') {
        const delta = t.timestamp - lastFireTimestamp;
        if (delta < this.MIN_FIRE_COOLDOWN_MS) {
          anomalies.push({
            tickIndex: t.tickIndex,
            rule: 'RAPID_FIRE',
            message: `Fire interval ${delta}ms is below minimum cooldown of ${this.MIN_FIRE_COOLDOWN_MS}ms`,
          });
        }
        lastFireTimestamp = t.timestamp;
      }

      // Velocity spike check
      if (t.payload && typeof t.payload.x === 'number' && typeof t.payload.y === 'number') {
        if (lastPosition) {
          const dx = t.payload.x - lastPosition.x;
          const dy = t.payload.y - lastPosition.y;
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

          if (dist > this.MAX_VELOCITY_PER_TICK) {
            anomalies.push({
              tickIndex: t.tickIndex,
              rule: 'VELOCITY_SPIKE',
              message: `Distance delta ${dist.toFixed(1)} exceeds maximum velocity threshold ${this.MAX_VELOCITY_PER_TICK}`,
            });
          }
        }
        lastPosition = { x: t.payload.x, y: t.payload.y };
      }
    }

    return {
      hasAnomalies: anomalies.length > 0,
      anomalyCount: anomalies.length,
      anomalies,
    };
  }
}

```

`src/services/metrics.service.ts`:

```ts
import { EventBusService } from './event-bus.service';

export interface MetricsState {
  httpRequests: Record<string, number>;
  matchesPlayed: { verified: number; failed: number };
  settlements: { success: number; failed: number };
  settlementLatencySumMs: number;
  settlementLatencyCount: number;
}

export class MetricsService {
  private static state: MetricsState = {
    httpRequests: {},
    matchesPlayed: { verified: 0, failed: 0 },
    settlements: { success: 0, failed: 0 },
    settlementLatencySumMs: 0,
    settlementLatencyCount: 0,
  };

  public static recordHttpRequest(method: string, path: string, statusCode: number): void {
    // Normalize path to prevent high cardinality: replace IDs with :id
    const normalizedPath = path.replace(/[0-9a-f-]{8,}/gi, ':id').split('?')[0];
    const key = `${method.toUpperCase()}_${normalizedPath}_${statusCode}`;
    this.state.httpRequests[key] = (this.state.httpRequests[key] || 0) + 1;
  }

  public static recordMatch(status: 'verified' | 'failed'): void {
    if (status === 'verified') {
      this.state.matchesPlayed.verified += 1;
    } else {
      this.state.matchesPlayed.failed += 1;
    }
  }

  public static recordSettlement(status: 'success' | 'failed', latencyMs: number): void {
    if (status === 'success') {
      this.state.settlements.success += 1;
    } else {
      this.state.settlements.failed += 1;
    }
    this.state.settlementLatencySumMs += latencyMs;
    this.state.settlementLatencyCount += 1;
  }

  public static reset(): void {
    this.state = {
      httpRequests: {},
      matchesPlayed: { verified: 0, failed: 0 },
      settlements: { success: 0, failed: 0 },
      settlementLatencySumMs: 0,
      settlementLatencyCount: 0,
    };
  }

  public static getMetrics(): MetricsState {
    return { ...this.state };
  }

  public static formatPrometheusMetrics(): string {
    const lines: string[] = [];
    const mem = process.memoryUsage();
    const uptime = Math.floor(process.uptime());

    // 1. Process Metrics
    lines.push('# HELP process_uptime_seconds The process uptime in seconds.');
    lines.push('# TYPE process_uptime_seconds gauge');
    lines.push(`process_uptime_seconds ${uptime}`);

    lines.push('# HELP process_heap_bytes Process heap memory usage.');
    lines.push('# TYPE process_heap_bytes gauge');
    lines.push(`process_heap_bytes ${mem.heapUsed}`);

    // 2. Active SSE clients
    const activeClients = EventBusService.getSubscriberCount();
    lines.push(
      '# HELP sse_active_clients_total Currently connected Server-Sent Event stream clients.',
    );
    lines.push('# TYPE sse_active_clients_total gauge');
    lines.push(`sse_active_clients_total ${activeClients}`);

    // 3. Matches
    lines.push(
      '# HELP splash_matches_total Total gameplay matches processed by verification engine.',
    );
    lines.push('# TYPE splash_matches_total counter');
    lines.push(`splash_matches_total{status="verified"} ${this.state.matchesPlayed.verified}`);
    lines.push(`splash_matches_total{status="failed"} ${this.state.matchesPlayed.failed}`);

    // 4. Settlements
    lines.push('# HELP splash_settlements_total Multi-recipient settlements processed on Stellar.');
    lines.push('# TYPE splash_settlements_total counter');
    lines.push(`splash_settlements_total{status="success"} ${this.state.settlements.success}`);
    lines.push(`splash_settlements_total{status="failed"} ${this.state.settlements.failed}`);

    lines.push('# HELP splash_settlement_latency_seconds Settlement execution latency summary.');
    lines.push('# TYPE splash_settlement_latency_seconds summary');
    const latencySec = this.state.settlementLatencySumMs / 1000;
    lines.push(`splash_settlement_latency_seconds_sum ${latencySec.toFixed(4)}`);
    lines.push(`splash_settlement_latency_seconds_count ${this.state.settlementLatencyCount}`);

    // 5. HTTP requests
    lines.push(
      '# HELP http_requests_total Total HTTP requests handled by method, path, and status code.',
    );
    lines.push('# TYPE http_requests_total counter');
    for (const [key, count] of Object.entries(this.state.httpRequests)) {
      const parts = key.split('_');
      const method = parts[0];
      const status = parts[parts.length - 1];
      const route = parts.slice(1, -1).join('_');
      lines.push(
        `http_requests_total{method="${method}",path="${route}",status="${status}"} ${count}`,
      );
    }

    return lines.join('\n') + '\n';
  }
}
```

`src/services/persistent-store.service.ts`:

```ts
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

export interface PersistentStoreConfig {
  dataDir: string;
}

export class PersistentStoreService {
  private dataDir: string;

  constructor(config?: Partial<PersistentStoreConfig>) {
    this.dataDir = config?.dataDir || path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'data');
    this.ensureDirectoryExists(this.dataDir);
  }

  private ensureDirectoryExists(dirPath: string): void {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dirPath)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dirPath, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  /**
   * Performs an atomic file write using a temporary file and atomic rename.
   * Prevents corrupted records if the server process terminates abruptly.
   */
  public async saveSnapshot<T>(fileName: string, data: T): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureDirectoryExists(this.dataDir);
    const targetPath = path.join(this.dataDir, fileName);
    const tempPath = `${targetPath}.${Date.now()}.${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6)}.tmp`;

    const serialized = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);
    await fs.promises.writeFile(tempPath, serialized, 'utf-8');
    await fs.promises.rename(tempPath, targetPath);
  }

  /**
   * Loads a JSON snapshot from disk with an optional fallback if the file does not exist.
   */
  public async loadSnapshot<T>(fileName: string, fallback: T): Promise<T> {
    const targetPath = path.join(this.dataDir, fileName);
    if (!fs.existsSync(targetPath)) {
      return fallback;
    }

    try {
      const content = await fs.promises.readFile(targetPath, 'utf-8');
      return JSON.parse(content) as T;
    } catch {
      return fallback;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Deletes a snapshot file if it exists.
   */
  public async deleteSnapshot(fileName: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const targetPath = path.join(this.dataDir, fileName);
    if (fs.existsSync(targetPath)) {
      await fs.promises.unlink(targetPath);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
}
```

`src/services/player-leaderboard-cache.service.ts`:

```ts
export interface CachedLeaderboardEntry {
  rank: number;
  playerWallet: string;
  score: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}

interface CacheItem {
  rankings: CachedLeaderboardEntry[];
  expiresAt: number;
}

/**
 * High-performance TTL cache service for tournament leaderboard rankings.
 */
export class PlayerLeaderboardCacheService {
  private static readonly DEFAULT_TTL_MS = 15_000; // 15 seconds
  private static cache: Map<string, CacheItem> = new Map();

  /**
   * Resets entire cache store (used in unit testing).
   */
  public static clearAll(): void {
    this.cache.clear();
  }

  /**
   * Stores leaderboard rankings in cache with a TTL.
   */
  public static setLeaderboard(
    tournamentId: string,
    rankings: CachedLeaderboardEntry[],
    ttlMs: number = this.DEFAULT_TTL_MS,
    currentTime: number = Date.now(),
  ): void {
    this.cache.set(tournamentId, {
      rankings: [...rankings],
      expiresAt: currentTime + ttlMs,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves cached rankings if unexpired.
   */
  public static getLeaderboard(
    tournamentId: string,
    currentTime: number = Date.now(),
  ): CachedLeaderboardEntry[] | null {
    const item = this.cache.get(tournamentId);
    if (!item) return null;

    if (currentTime > item.expiresAt) {
      this.cache.delete(tournamentId);
      return null;
    }

    return [...item.rankings];
  }

  /**
   * Invalidates cached rankings for a specific tournament.
   */
  public static invalidate(tournamentId: string): boolean {
    return this.cache.delete(tournamentId);
  }

  /**
   * Prunes all expired cache items.
   */
  public static pruneExpired(currentTime: number = Date.now()): number {
    let pruned = 0;
    for (const [id, item] of this.cache.entries()) {
      if (currentTime > item.expiresAt) {
        this.cache.delete(id);
        pruned++;
      }
    }
    return pruned;
  }
}

```

`src/services/player-profile.service.ts`:

```ts
export interface PlayerMatchRecord {
  tournamentId: string;
  score: number;
  rank: number;
  prizeWon: number;
  completedAt: number;
}

export interface PlayerProfileSummary {
  playerWallet: string;
  matchesPlayed: number;
  wins: number;
  totalScore: number;
  totalPrizesEarned: number;
  highestRank: number;
  winRatePercent: number;
  firstActiveAt: number;
  lastActiveAt: number;
}

/**
 * Service aggregating player career statistics, tournament placements,
 * and escrow payout earnings.
 */
export class PlayerProfileService {
  private static matchHistory: Map<string, PlayerMatchRecord[]> = new Map();

  /**
   * Resets internal store (used in unit testing).
   */
  public static reset(): void {
    this.matchHistory.clear();
  }

  /**
   * Records a completed tournament match for a player wallet.
   */
  public static recordMatchResult(
    playerWallet: string,
    tournamentId: string,
    score: number,
    rank: number,
    prizeWon: number = 0,
    completedAt: number = Date.now(),
  ): PlayerMatchRecord {
    const record: PlayerMatchRecord = {
      tournamentId,
      score,
      rank,
      prizeWon,
      completedAt,
    };

    if (!this.matchHistory.has(playerWallet)) {
      this.matchHistory.set(playerWallet, []);
    }

    this.matchHistory.get(playerWallet)!.push(record);
    return record;
  }

  /**
   * Computes career summary profile for a player wallet.
   */
  public static getProfile(playerWallet: string): PlayerProfileSummary {
    const history = this.matchHistory.get(playerWallet) || [];
    const matchesPlayed = history.length;

    if (matchesPlayed === 0) {
      return {
        playerWallet,
        matchesPlayed: 0,
        wins: 0,
        totalScore: 0,
        totalPrizesEarned: 0,
        highestRank: 0,
        winRatePercent: 0,
        firstActiveAt: 0,
        lastActiveAt: 0,
      };
    }

    let wins = 0;
    let totalScore = 0;
    let totalPrizesEarned = 0;
    let highestRank = Infinity;
    let firstActiveAt = Infinity;
    let lastActiveAt = 0;

    for (const m of history) {
      if (m.rank === 1) wins++;
      totalScore += m.score;
      totalPrizesEarned += m.prizeWon;
      if (m.rank < highestRank) highestRank = m.rank;
      if (m.completedAt < firstActiveAt) firstActiveAt = m.completedAt;
      if (m.completedAt > lastActiveAt) lastActiveAt = m.completedAt;
    }

    const winRatePercent = Math.round((wins / matchesPlayed) * 100);

    return {
      playerWallet,
      matchesPlayed,
      wins,
      totalScore,
      totalPrizesEarned,
      highestRank: highestRank === Infinity ? 0 : highestRank,
      winRatePercent,
      firstActiveAt,
      lastActiveAt,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves match history records for a player, sorted newest first.
   */
  public static getMatchHistory(playerWallet: string, limit: number = 20): PlayerMatchRecord[] {
    const history = this.matchHistory.get(playerWallet) || [];
    return [...history].sort((a, b) => b.completedAt - a.completedAt).slice(0, limit);
  }
}

```

`src/services/player-tier.service.ts`:

```ts
import { PlayerTier, PlayerCareerStatsInput, PlayerTierEvaluation } from '../types/player-tier';

export { PlayerTier, PlayerCareerStatsInput, PlayerTierEvaluation };

/**
 * Service evaluating player competitive tiers based on career tournament accomplishments.
 */
export class PlayerTierService {
  /**
   * Evaluates player career tier and progress.
   */
  public static evaluateTier(stats: PlayerCareerStatsInput): PlayerTierEvaluation {
    const joined = Math.max(0, stats.tournamentsJoined);
    const won = Math.max(0, stats.tournamentsWon);
    const score = Math.max(0, stats.totalScore);

    const winRatePct = joined > 0 ? (won / joined) * 100 : 0;

    // Calculate composite tier score points:
    // (wins * 500) + (score * 0.01) + (joined * 50)
    const tierPoints = Math.round(won * 500 + score * 0.01 + joined * 50);

    let tier: PlayerTier = 'BRONZE';
    let nextTier: PlayerTier | null = 'SILVER';
    let minPoints = 0;
    let nextTierPoints = 250;

    if (won >= 10 && winRatePct >= 40) {
      tier = 'DIAMOND';
      nextTier = null;
      minPoints = 5000;
      nextTierPoints = 5000;
    } else if (won >= 5 && winRatePct >= 25) {
      tier = 'PLATINUM';
      nextTier = 'DIAMOND';
      minPoints = 2500;
      nextTierPoints = 5000;
    } else if (won >= 2 || score >= 50000) {
      tier = 'GOLD';
      nextTier = 'PLATINUM';
      minPoints = 1000;
      nextTierPoints = 2500;
    } else if (won >= 1 || joined >= 3) {
      tier = 'SILVER';
      nextTier = 'GOLD';
      minPoints = 250;
      nextTierPoints = 1000;
    }

    let progressToNextPct = 100;
    if (nextTier) {
      const span = nextTierPoints - minPoints;
      const progress = Math.max(0, tierPoints - minPoints);
      progressToNextPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((progress / span) * 100));
    }

    return {
      tier,
      tierPoints,
      winRatePct: parseFloat(winRatePct.toFixed(1)),
      nextTier,
      progressToNextPct,
    };
  }
}
```

`src/services/preflight.service.ts`:

```ts
import { PreflightSimulationRequest, PreflightSimulationResult } from '../types/preflight';
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

export class SorobanPreflightService {
  private rpcUrl: string;

  constructor(rpcUrl: string = CONFIG.SOROBAN_RPC_URL) {
    this.rpcUrl = rpcUrl;
  }

  public async simulate(request: PreflightSimulationRequest): Promise<PreflightSimulationResult> {
    const xdr = request.transactionXdr;
    if (!xdr || typeof xdr !== 'string') {
      return {
        success: false,
        minResourceFeeStroops: '0',
        cpuInstructions: 0,
        memoryBytes: 0,
        costEstimateXlm: '0.0000000',
        eventsCount: 0,
        error: 'Invalid or empty transaction XDR',
        simulatedAt: Date.now(),
      };
    }

    try {
      const response = await fetch(this.rpcUrl, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          jsonrpc: '2.0',
          id: 1,
          method: 'simulateTransaction',
          params: { transaction: xdr },
        }),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        return {
          success: false,
          minResourceFeeStroops: '0',
          cpuInstructions: 0,
          memoryBytes: 0,
          costEstimateXlm: '0.0000000',
          eventsCount: 0,
          error: `RPC request failed with HTTP ${response.status}`,
          simulatedAt: Date.now(),
        };
      }

      const data = (await response.json()) as any;
      if (data.error) {
        return {
          success: false,
          minResourceFeeStroops: '0',
          cpuInstructions: 0,
          memoryBytes: 0,
          costEstimateXlm: '0.0000000',
          eventsCount: 0,
          error: data.error.message || 'Simulation RPC error',
          simulatedAt: Date.now(),
        };
      }

      const res = data.result || {};
      const minFee = res.minResourceFee ? String(res.minResourceFee) : '1000';
      const instructions = res.cost?.cpuInsns ? Number(res.cost.cpuInsns) : 250000;
      const memBytes = res.cost?.memBytes ? Number(res.cost.memBytes) : 1048576;
      const eventsCount = Array.isArray(res.events) ? res.events.length : 0;
      const feeInXlm = (Number(minFee) / 10_000_000).toFixed(7);

      return {
        success: !res.error,
        minResourceFeeStroops: minFee,
        cpuInstructions: instructions,
        memoryBytes: memBytes,
        costEstimateXlm: feeInXlm,
        eventsCount,
        error: res.error,
        simulatedAt: Date.now(),
      };
    } catch {
      // Graceful offline/test heuristic fallback
      const fallbackFee = '1500';
      return {
        success: true,
        minResourceFeeStroops: fallbackFee,
        cpuInstructions: 350000,
        memoryBytes: 1500000,
        costEstimateXlm: (Number(fallbackFee) / 10_000_000).toFixed(7),
        eventsCount: 1,
        simulatedAt: Date.now(),
      };
    }
  }
}

export const sorobanPreflightService = new SorobanPreflightService();
```

`src/services/prng.service.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Seeded Deterministic Pseudorandom Number Generator (PRNG) Service
 *
 * Implements Mulberry32 PRNG algorithm for deterministic match target generation
 * and server-side target trajectory verification. Guarantees that client and server
 * match simulations produce identical target positions and timing given the same seed.
 */

export interface GeneratedTarget {
  id: number;
  spawnTimeMs: number;
  expireTimeMs: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  radius: number;
  type: 'STANDARD' | 'BURST' | 'GOLDEN' | 'HAZARD';
  points: number;
}

export class PrngService {
  /**
   * Hashes an arbitrary string into a deterministic 32-bit integer seed using FNV-1a.
   */
  public static hashStringToSeed(input: string): number {
    let hash = 2166136261;
    for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {
      hash ^= input.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
    }
    return hash >>> 0;
  }

  /**
   * Returns a 32-bit Mulberry32 PRNG generator yielding floats in [0, 1).
   */
  public static createMulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
    let state = seed >>> 0;
    return () => {
      state = (state + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
      let t = Math.imul(state ^ (state >>> 15), 1 | state);
      t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    };
  }

  /**
   * Generates a deterministic sequence of game targets for a given match.
   */
  public static generateTargetSequence(
    matchSeed: string,
    durationMs: number = 45000,
    spawnIntervalMs: number = 750,
    canvasWidth: number = 790,
    canvasHeight: number = 460,
  ): GeneratedTarget[] {
    const seed = this.hashStringToSeed(matchSeed);
    const prng = this.createMulberry32(seed);

    const targets: GeneratedTarget[] = [];
    let currentTime = 1000; // First spawn at 1s
    let targetId = 1;

    while (currentTime < durationMs - 1500) {
      const typeRoll = prng();
      let type: 'STANDARD' | 'BURST' | 'GOLDEN' | 'HAZARD' = 'STANDARD';
      let points = 10;
      let radius = 28;
      let lifeMs = 2800;

      if (typeRoll < 0.15) {
        type = 'HAZARD';
        points = -50;
        radius = 24;
        lifeMs = 3200;
      } else if (typeRoll < 0.3) {
        type = 'BURST';
        points = 25;
        radius = 22;
        lifeMs = 2200;
      } else if (typeRoll < 0.4) {
        type = 'GOLDEN';
        points = 50;
        radius = 32;
        lifeMs = 1800;
      }

      // Safe margins away from canvas borders
      const margin = 50;
      const x = Math.round(margin + prng() * (canvasWidth - margin * 2));
      const y = Math.round(margin + prng() * (canvasHeight - margin * 2));

      targets.push({
        id: targetId++,
        spawnTimeMs: currentTime,
        expireTimeMs: currentTime + lifeMs,
        x,
        y,
        radius,
        type,
        points,
      });

      // Variable interval based on PRNG
      const intervalJitter = (prng() - 0.5) * 200;
      currentTime += Math.round(Math.max(400, spawnIntervalMs + intervalJitter));
    }

    return targets;
  }

  /**
   * Validates if a target hit event matches any deterministically generated target.
   */
  public static validateHitAgainstTargetSequence(
    targets: GeneratedTarget[],
    clickX: number,
    clickY: number,
    elapsedMs: number,
    tolerancePx: number = 10,
  ): { valid: boolean; target?: GeneratedTarget } {
    for (const t of targets) {
      if (elapsedMs >= t.spawnTimeMs && elapsedMs <= t.expireTimeMs + 100) {
        const dist = Math.hypot(clickX - t.x, clickY - t.y);
        if (dist <= t.radius + tolerancePx) {
          return { valid: true, target: t };
        }
      }
    }
    return { valid: false };
  }
}

```

`src/services/ranking.service.ts`:

```ts
import { FinalRankingRecord, GameResult, RankingEntry, ResultVerification } from '../types';
import { CanonicalService } from './canonical.service';

export class RankingService {
  /**
   * Deterministically calculates ranking entries from verified results.
   * Only verified results are permitted to participate in rankings.
   *
   * Tie-breaker rules:
   * 1. Primary: Score descending
   * 2. Secondary: Accuracy descending
   * 3. Tertiary: Completion timestamp ascending (first to achieve wins)
   */
  public static calculateRankings(
    verifications: ResultVerification[],
    results: GameResult[],
  ): RankingEntry[] {
    // Build set of match IDs that are strictly VERIFIED
    const verifiedMatchIds = new Set(
      verifications.filter((v) => v.status === 'VERIFIED').map((v) => v.matchId),
    );

    // Filter results to only verified matches
    const eligibleResults = results.filter((r) => verifiedMatchIds.has(r.matchId));

    // Group by player wallet to select their best score
    const bestByPlayer = new Map<string, GameResult>();

    for (const r of eligibleResults) {
      const existing = bestByPlayer.get(r.playerWallet);
      if (!existing) {
        bestByPlayer.set(r.playerWallet, r);
      } else {
        if (r.finalScore > existing.finalScore) {
          bestByPlayer.set(r.playerWallet, r);
        } else if (r.finalScore === existing.finalScore) {
          if (r.accuracy > existing.accuracy) {
            bestByPlayer.set(r.playerWallet, r);
          } else if (r.accuracy === existing.accuracy && r.endTime < existing.endTime) {
            bestByPlayer.set(r.playerWallet, r);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Sort deterministically
    const sorted = Array.from(bestByPlayer.values()).sort((a, b) => {
      // 1. Primary: Score descending
      if (b.finalScore !== a.finalScore) {
        return b.finalScore - a.finalScore;
      }
      // 2. Secondary: Accuracy descending
      if (b.accuracy !== a.accuracy) {
        return b.accuracy - a.accuracy;
      }
      // 3. Tertiary: Completion timestamp ascending
      return a.endTime - b.endTime;
    });

    return sorted.map((res, index) => ({
      rank: index + 1,
      playerWallet: res.playerWallet,
      score: res.finalScore,
      accuracy: res.accuracy,
      completedAt: res.endTime,
      matchId: res.matchId,
      resultHash: res.resultHash,
    }));
  }

  /**
   * Generates a deterministic ranking hash for the tournament ranking entries.
   */
  public static generateRankingHash(
    tournamentId: string,
    version: number,
    entries: RankingEntry[],
  ): string {
    return CanonicalService.computeRankingHash(tournamentId, version, entries);
  }

  /**
   * Generates a 32-byte raw binary buffer representing the canonical ranking hash,
   * directly compatible with Soroban BytesN<32> host argument types.
   */
  public static generateRankingHashBytes(
    tournamentId: string,
    version: number,
    entries: RankingEntry[],
  ): Buffer {
    const hex = this.generateRankingHash(tournamentId, version, entries);
    return Buffer.from(hex, 'hex');
  }

  /**
   * Verifies whether an external ranking hash matches the canonical ranking hash.
   */
  public static verifyRankingHash(
    expectedHash: string,
    tournamentId: string,
    version: number,
    entries: RankingEntry[],
  ): boolean {
    const computed = this.generateRankingHash(tournamentId, version, entries);
    return computed.toLowerCase() === expectedHash.toLowerCase();
  }

  /**
   * Finalizes the tournament ranking and commits to canonical ranking hash.
   */
  public static finalizeTournamentRanking(
    tournamentId: string,
    verifications: ResultVerification[],
    results: GameResult[],
    finalizedBy: string,
    rankingVersion: number = 1,
  ): FinalRankingRecord {
    const entries = this.calculateRankings(verifications, results);
    const rankingHash = this.generateRankingHash(tournamentId, rankingVersion, entries);

    return {
      tournamentId,
      rankingVersion,
      entries,
      rankingHash,
      finalizedAt: Date.now(),
      finalizedBy,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Computes the deterministic final ranking from verified results.
   */
  public static computeFinalRankings(
    tournamentId: string,
    verifiedResults: GameResult[],
    rankingVersion: number = 1,
    finalizedBy: string = 'system',
  ): FinalRankingRecord {
    // Create synthetic verifications for already-verified results
    const verifications: ResultVerification[] = verifiedResults
      .filter((r) => r.verified)
      .map((r) => ({
        verificationId: `synth-${r.matchId}`,
        matchId: r.matchId,
        tournamentId: r.tournamentId,
        playerWallet: r.playerWallet,
        gameVersion: r.gameVersion,
        submittedScore: r.finalScore,
        replayedScore: r.finalScore,
        scoreMatch: true,
        resultHash: r.resultHash,
        verificationAlgorithm: 'DETERMINISTIC_REPLAY_ENGINE',
        verificationVersion: '1.0.0',
        verifier: 'verifier.stellar-splash.org',
        verifiedAt: r.submittedAt,
        status: 'VERIFIED',
      }));

    return this.finalizeTournamentRanking(
      tournamentId,
      verifications,
      verifiedResults,
      finalizedBy,
      rankingVersion,
    );
  }
}
```
