```ts
  // --- Tournaments ---
  public static getAllTournaments(): Tournament[] {
    return Array.from(this.tournaments.values()).sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);
  }

  public static getTournamentById(id: string): Tournament | undefined {
    return this.tournaments.get(id);
  }

  public static saveTournament(tournament: Tournament): void {
    tournament.updatedAt = Date.now();
    this.tournaments.set(tournament.id, tournament);
    this.logAudit('TOURNAMENT_SAVED', tournament.creatorWallet, { tournamentId: tournament.id });
  }

  // --- Participants ---
  public static getParticipants(tournamentId: string): TournamentParticipant[] {
    return this.participants.get(tournamentId) || [];
  }

  public static getParticipant(
    tournamentId: string,
    playerWallet: string,
  ): TournamentParticipant | undefined {
    const list = this.getParticipants(tournamentId);
    return list.find((p) => p.playerWallet === playerWallet);
  }

  public static addParticipant(participant: TournamentParticipant): void {
    const list = this.getParticipants(participant.tournamentId);
    list.push(participant);
    this.participants.set(participant.tournamentId, list);

    const t = this.getTournamentById(participant.tournamentId);
    if (t) {
      t.currentParticipants = list.length;
      if (t.maxParticipants > 0 && t.currentParticipants >= t.maxParticipants) {
        t.state = 'FULL';
      }
      this.saveTournament(t);
    }

    this.logAudit('PARTICIPANT_JOINED', participant.playerWallet, {
      tournamentId: participant.tournamentId,
    });
  }

  public static updateParticipantScore(
    tournamentId: string,
    playerWallet: string,
    score: number,
  ): void {
    const list = this.getParticipants(tournamentId);
    const p = list.find((item) => item.playerWallet === playerWallet);
    if (p) {
      p.matchesPlayed += 1;
      if (score > p.bestScore) {
        p.bestScore = score;
      }
    }
  }

  // --- Matches & Sessions ---
  public static createMatchSession(session: MatchSession): void {
    this.matches.set(session.matchId, session);
    this.logAudit('MATCH_STARTED', session.playerWallet, { matchId: session.matchId });
  }

  public static getMatchSession(matchId: string): MatchSession | undefined {
    return this.matches.get(matchId);
  }

  public static updateMatchSession(session: MatchSession): void {
    this.matches.set(session.matchId, session);
  }

  // --- Game Results ---
  public static saveGameResult(result: GameResult): void {
    this.results.set(result.matchId, result);
    this.logAudit('RESULT_RECORDED', result.playerWallet, {
      matchId: result.matchId,
      finalScore: result.finalScore,
      resultHash: result.resultHash,
    });
  }

  public static getGameResult(matchId: string): GameResult | undefined {
    return this.results.get(matchId);
  }

  public static getAllResultsForTournament(tournamentId: string): GameResult[] {
    return Array.from(this.results.values())
      .filter((r) => r.tournamentId === tournamentId)
      .sort((a, b) => b.finalScore - a.finalScore);
  }

  // --- Replay Attack Prevention ---
  public static isEventStreamConsumed(fingerprint: string): boolean {
    return this.consumedEventStreams.has(fingerprint);
  }

  public static getEventStreamMatch(fingerprint: string): string | undefined {
    return this.consumedEventStreams.get(fingerprint);
  }

  public static markEventStreamConsumed(fingerprint: string, matchId: string): void {
    this.consumedEventStreams.set(fingerprint, matchId);
  }

  // --- Verifications ---
  public static saveVerification(verification: ResultVerification): void {
    this.verifications.set(verification.matchId, verification);
    this.logAudit('RESULT_VERIFIED', verification.playerWallet, {
      matchId: verification.matchId,
      replayedScore: verification.replayedScore,
      status: verification.status,
      resultHash: verification.resultHash,
    });
  }

  public static getVerification(matchId: string): ResultVerification | undefined {
    return this.verifications.get(matchId);
  }

  // --- Final Rankings ---
  public static saveRanking(ranking: FinalRankingRecord): void {
    this.rankings.set(ranking.tournamentId, ranking);

    const t = this.getTournamentById(ranking.tournamentId);
    if (t) {
      t.finalRankingHash = ranking.rankingHash;
      t.state = 'FINALIZED';
      this.saveTournament(t);
    }

    this.logAudit('RANKING_FINALIZED', ranking.finalizedBy, {
      tournamentId: ranking.tournamentId,
      rankingHash: ranking.rankingHash,
      count: ranking.entries.length,
    });
  }

  public static getRanking(tournamentId: string): FinalRankingRecord | undefined {
    return this.rankings.get(tournamentId);
  }

  // --- Prize Agreements ---
  public static saveAgreement(agreement: PrizeAgreement): void {
    const list = this.agreements.get(agreement.tournamentId) || [];
    const existingIndex = list.findIndex((a) => a.version === agreement.version);

    if (existingIndex >= 0) {
      const prev = list[existingIndex];
      // Invariant: Once locked, an agreement cannot have its material terms modified
      if (prev.status === 'LOCKED' && agreement.status !== 'SUPERSEDED') {
        if (
          prev.prizePoolAmount !== agreement.prizePoolAmount ||
          prev.prizeAsset !== agreement.prizeAsset ||
          prev.version !== agreement.version ||
          prev.agreementHash !== agreement.agreementHash ||
          JSON.stringify(prev.allocationRules) !== JSON.stringify(agreement.allocationRules)
        ) {
          throw new Error('IMMUTABLE_AGREEMENT: Locked agreements cannot be modified.');
        }
      }
      list[existingIndex] = { ...agreement };
    } else {
      list.push({ ...agreement });
    }

    this.agreements.set(agreement.tournamentId, list);

    const t = this.getTournamentById(agreement.tournamentId);
    if (t) {
      t.activeAgreementVersion = agreement.version;
      this.saveTournament(t);
    }

    this.logAudit('AGREEMENT_SAVED', agreement.createdBy, {
      tournamentId: agreement.tournamentId,
      version: agreement.version,
      status: agreement.status,
      agreementHash: agreement.agreementHash,
    });
  }

  public static getAgreementsForTournament(tournamentId: string): PrizeAgreement[] {
    return this.agreements.get(tournamentId) || [];
  }

  public static getAgreement(tournamentId: string, version: number): PrizeAgreement | undefined {
    const list = this.getAgreementsForTournament(tournamentId);
    return list.find((a) => a.version === version);
  }

  public static getActiveAgreement(tournamentId: string): PrizeAgreement | undefined {
    const list = this.getAgreementsForTournament(tournamentId);
    // Prefer LOCKED agreements, otherwise highest version
    const locked = list.filter((a) => a.status === 'LOCKED').sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version);
    if (locked.length > 0) return locked[0];

    return list.sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version)[0];
  }

  // --- Prize Pool Fundings & Idempotency ---
  public static isTxConsumed(txHash: string): boolean {
    return this.consumedTxs.has(txHash);
  }

  public static recordFunding(funding: PrizePoolFunding): void {
    this.fundings.set(funding.txHash, funding);
    this.consumedTxs.add(funding.txHash);
    this.logAudit('PRIZE_FUNDED', funding.funderWallet, {
      tournamentId: funding.tournamentId,
      txHash: funding.txHash,
      amount: funding.amount,
    });
  }

  public static getFundingByTx(txHash: string): PrizePoolFunding | undefined {
    return this.fundings.get(txHash);
  }

  public static getFundingForTournament(tournamentId: string): PrizePoolFunding | undefined {
    for (const f of this.fundings.values()) {
      if (f.tournamentId === tournamentId) return f;
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  // --- Reconciliation ---
  public static getReconciliation(tournamentId: string): ReconciliationRecord {
    const t = this.getTournamentById(tournamentId);
    if (!t) {
      return {
        tournamentId,
        expectedAmount: 0,
        onChainAmount: 0,
        asset: 'UNKNOWN',
        status: 'RECONCILED',
        lastCheckedAt: Date.now(),
        details: 'Tournament not found.',
      };
    }

    const funding = this.getFundingForTournament(tournamentId);
    const onChainAmount = funding && funding.status === 'CONFIRMED' ? funding.amount : 0;
    const isReconciled = onChainAmount >= t.prizePoolAmount;

    return {
      tournamentId,
      expectedAmount: t.prizePoolAmount,
      onChainAmount,
      asset: t.prizeAsset,
      status: isReconciled ? 'RECONCILED' : 'RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED',
      lastCheckedAt: Date.now(),
      details: isReconciled
        ? `Prize pool is fully backed on-chain (${onChainAmount} ${t.prizeAsset}).`
        : `Discrepancy detected: Expected ${t.prizePoolAmount} ${t.prizeAsset}, on-chain holds ${onChainAmount}.`,
    };
  }

  // --- Audit ---
  public static logAudit(
    eventType: string,
    actor: string,
    details: Record<string, unknown>,
    timestamp: number = Date.now(),
  ): void {
    this.auditLogs.push({
      id: `audit-${timestamp}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`,
      timestamp,
      eventType,
      actor,
      details,
    });
  }

  public static getAuditLogs(): AuditEvent[] {
    return [...this.auditLogs];
  }

  // --- Settlements ---
  public static saveSettlement(settlement: SettlementRecord): void {
    settlement.updatedAt = Date.now();
    this.settlements.set(settlement.settlementId, settlement);

    const t = this.getTournamentById(settlement.tournamentId);
    if (t) {
      t.settlementId = settlement.settlementId;
      if (settlement.status === 'SETTLED' || settlement.status === 'RECONCILED') {
        t.state = 'SETTLED';
      } else if (
        settlement.status === 'SUBMITTING' ||
        settlement.status === 'CONFIRMING' ||
        settlement.status === 'AUTHORIZED'
      ) {
        t.state = 'SETTLING';
      }
      this.saveTournament(t);
    }

    this.logAudit('SETTLEMENT_SAVED', settlement.authorizedBy || 'SYSTEM', {
      settlementId: settlement.settlementId,
      tournamentId: settlement.tournamentId,
      status: settlement.status,
      settlementHash: settlement.settlementHash,
    });
  }

  public static getSettlement(settlementId: string): SettlementRecord | undefined {
    return this.settlements.get(settlementId);
  }

  public static getSettlementByTournament(tournamentId: string): SettlementRecord | undefined {
    for (const s of this.settlements.values()) {
      if (s.tournamentId === tournamentId) return s;
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  public static getAllSettlements(): SettlementRecord[] {
    return Array.from(this.settlements.values()).sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);
  }

  // --- Settlement Transactions ---
  public static saveSettlementTransactions(
    settlementId: string,
    transactions: SettlementTransactionRecord[],
  ): void {
    this.settlementTransactions.set(settlementId, transactions);
  }

  public static getSettlementTransactions(settlementId: string): SettlementTransactionRecord[] {
    return this.settlementTransactions.get(settlementId) || [];
  }

  // --- Settlement Reconciliation ---
  public static saveSettlementReconciliation(rec: SettlementReconciliationRecord): void {
    this.settlementReconciliations.set(rec.settlementId, rec);
    this.logAudit('SETTLEMENT_RECONCILED', 'SYSTEM', {
      settlementId: rec.settlementId,
      status: rec.status,
      expectedTotal: rec.expectedTotal,
      observedTotal: rec.observedTotal,
    });
  }

  public static getSettlementReconciliation(
    settlementId: string,
  ): SettlementReconciliationRecord | undefined {
    return this.settlementReconciliations.get(settlementId);
  }

  // --- Player Payout History ---
  public static getPlayerPayouts(playerWallet: string): Array<{
    settlement: SettlementRecord;
    recipient: SettlementRecipient;
    tournament: Tournament;
  }> {
    const results: Array<{
      settlement: SettlementRecord;
      recipient: SettlementRecipient;
      tournament: Tournament;
    }> = [];

    for (const settlement of this.settlements.values()) {
      const recipient = settlement.recipients.find((r) => r.playerWallet === playerWallet);
      if (recipient) {
        const tournament = this.getTournamentById(settlement.tournamentId);
        if (tournament) {
          results.push({
            settlement,
            recipient,
            tournament,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    return results.sort((a, b) => b.settlement.createdAt - a.settlement.createdAt);
  }

  /**
   * Resets test data for a specific tournament.
   */
  public static resetTournamentData(tournamentId: string): void {
    this.tournaments.delete(tournamentId);
    this.participants.delete(tournamentId);
    this.rankings.delete(tournamentId);
    this.agreements.delete(tournamentId);

    for (const [mId, m] of Array.from(this.matches.entries())) {
      if (m.tournamentId === tournamentId) {
        this.matches.delete(mId);
        this.results.delete(mId);
        this.verifications.delete(mId);
      }
    }

    for (const [tx, f] of Array.from(this.fundings.entries())) {
      if (f.tournamentId === tournamentId) {
        this.fundings.delete(tx);
        this.consumedTxs.delete(tx);
      }
    }

    for (const [sId, s] of Array.from(this.settlements.entries())) {
      if (s.tournamentId === tournamentId) {
        this.settlements.delete(sId);
        this.settlementTransactions.delete(sId);
        this.settlementReconciliations.delete(sId);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Resets all in-memory store collections.
   */
  public static reset(): void {
    this.tournaments.clear();
    this.participants.clear();
    this.matches.clear();
    this.results.clear();
    this.verifications.clear();
    this.rankings.clear();
    this.agreements.clear();
    this.settlements.clear();
    this.settlementTransactions.clear();
    this.settlementReconciliations.clear();
    this.fundings.clear();
    this.consumedTxs.clear();
    this.auditLogs = [];
  }

  /**
   * Exports full storage state into a serializable snapshot object.
   */
  public static exportAll(): Record<string, any> {
    return {
      version: 1,
      exportedAt: Date.now(),
      tournaments: Array.from(this.tournaments.values()),
      participants: Array.from(this.participants.entries()),
      matches: Array.from(this.matches.values()),
      results: Array.from(this.results.values()),
      verifications: Array.from(this.verifications.values()),
      rankings: Array.from(this.rankings.values()),
      agreements: Array.from(this.agreements.entries()),
      settlements: Array.from(this.settlements.values()),
      auditLogs: this.auditLogs,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Imports full storage state from a snapshot object.
   */
  public static importAll(data: Record<string, any>): void {
    if (!data) return;
    if (Array.isArray(data.tournaments)) {
      this.tournaments.clear();
      for (const t of data.tournaments) this.tournaments.set(t.id, t);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(data.participants)) {
      this.participants.clear();
      for (const [id, parts] of data.participants) this.participants.set(id, parts);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(data.matches)) {
      this.matches.clear();
      for (const m of data.matches) this.matches.set(m.matchId, m);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(data.results)) {
      this.results.clear();
      for (const r of data.results) this.results.set(r.matchId, r);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(data.settlements)) {
      this.settlements.clear();
      for (const s of data.settlements) this.settlements.set(s.settlementId, s);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(data.auditLogs)) {
      this.auditLogs = data.auditLogs;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Initializes persistent storage on startup by loading the disk snapshot if present.
   */
  public static async initializePersistence(fileName = 'engine-snapshot.json'): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const store = new PersistentStoreService();
      const data = await store.loadSnapshot<Record<string, any> | null>(fileName, null);
      if (data) {
        this.importAll(data);
        return true;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Failed to load storage snapshot on startup:', err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}
```

`src/services/token-allowlist-cache.service.ts`:

```ts
export interface TokenMetadata {
  address: string;
  symbol: string;
  decimals: number;
  issuer?: string;
  cachedAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;
}

/**
 * High-performance TTL cache service for verified Stellar asset trustlines
 * and Soroban token contracts authorized for tournament entry fees and prize pools.
 */
export class TokenAllowlistCacheService {
  private static readonly DEFAULT_TTL_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000; // 10 minutes
  private static cache: Map<string, TokenMetadata> = new Map();

  // Known default testnet tokens
  private static readonly DEFAULT_TOKENS: Array<Omit<TokenMetadata, 'cachedAt' | 'expiresAt'>> = [
    {
      address: 'CAS3J7GYLGXMF6TDJBBYYSE3HQ6BBSMLNUQ34T6TZMYMW2EVH34XOWMA',
      symbol: 'XLM',
      decimals: 7,
    },
    {
      address: 'CAQCFVLOBK5GIULPNZCGATSTPCHLNBHHCEYLZTG2NOX7I2L46MDQH2XU',
      symbol: 'USDC',
      decimals: 7,
    },
    {
      address: 'CBIELTK6YBZJU5UP2WWQEUCYKLPU6AUNZ2BQ4WWUIE3USCIHZXWH65SC',
      symbol: 'AQUA',
      decimals: 7,
    },
  ];

  static {
    this.seedDefaults();
  }

  /**
   * Resets cache and reseeds standard testnet token definitions.
   */
  public static reset(): void {
    this.cache.clear();
    this.seedDefaults();
  }

  private static seedDefaults(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const token of this.DEFAULT_TOKENS) {
      this.cache.set(token.address, {
        ...token,
        cachedAt: now,
        expiresAt: now + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000, // 1 year for defaults
      });
    }
  }

  /**
   * Checks whether a token contract is authorized and unexpired.
   */
  public static isTokenAllowed(address: string, currentTime: number = Date.now()): boolean {
    const entry = this.cache.get(address);
    if (!entry) return false;
    if (currentTime > entry.expiresAt) {
      this.cache.delete(address);
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Registers or updates an authorized token in the cache.
   */
  public static registerToken(
    address: string,
    symbol: string,
    decimals: number,
    issuer?: string,
    ttlMs: number = this.DEFAULT_TTL_MS,
    currentTime: number = Date.now(),
  ): TokenMetadata {
    const metadata: TokenMetadata = {
      address,
      symbol,
      decimals,
      issuer,
      cachedAt: currentTime,
      expiresAt: currentTime + ttlMs,
    };
    this.cache.set(address, metadata);
    return metadata;
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves metadata for an authorized token.
   */
  public static getTokenMetadata(
    address: string,
    currentTime: number = Date.now(),
  ): TokenMetadata | null {
    if (!this.isTokenAllowed(address, currentTime)) {
      return null;
    }
    return this.cache.get(address) || null;
  }

  /**
   * Returns all currently valid authorized tokens.
   */
  public static getAllowedTokens(currentTime: number = Date.now()): TokenMetadata[] {
    const valid: TokenMetadata[] = [];
    for (const [address, meta] of this.cache.entries()) {
      if (currentTime <= meta.expiresAt) {
        valid.push(meta);
      } else {
        this.cache.delete(address);
      }
    }
    return valid;
  }

  /**
   * Removes a token from the allowlist.
   */
  public static removeToken(address: string): boolean {
    return this.cache.delete(address);
  }

  /**
   * Prunes all expired tokens from cache. Returns count of pruned entries.
   */
  public static clearExpired(currentTime: number = Date.now()): number {
    let pruned = 0;
    for (const [address, meta] of this.cache.entries()) {
      if (currentTime > meta.expiresAt) {
        this.cache.delete(address);
        pruned++;
      }
    }
    return pruned;
  }
}
```

`src/services/tournament-cloning.service.ts`:

```ts
import { StoreService } from './store.service';
import { Tournament } from '../types';

export interface CloneTournamentOptions {
  sourceTournamentId: string;
  newTournamentId: string;
  title: string;
  startTime: number;
  endTime: number;
  creatorWallet: string;
  prizePoolAmount?: number;
}

/**
 * Service enabling creators to clone existing tournament parameters,
 * rules, and payout split templates into new sequential events.
 */
export class TournamentCloningService {
  /**
   * Clones a tournament template and registers the new tournament in StoreService.
   */
  public static cloneTournament(options: CloneTournamentOptions): Tournament {
    const source = StoreService.getTournamentById(options.sourceTournamentId);
    if (!source) {
      throw new Error(`Source tournament ${options.sourceTournamentId} not found`);
    }

    if (source.creatorWallet !== options.creatorWallet) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: only the original tournament creator can clone this event');
    }

    if (options.endTime <= options.startTime + 60_000) {
      throw new Error('Invalid timebounds: tournament duration must be at least 60 seconds');
    }

    const prizePool = options.prizePoolAmount ?? source.prizePoolAmount;

    const cloned: Tournament = {
      id: options.newTournamentId,
      name: options.title,
      description: source.description || `Cloned edition of ${source.name}`,
      gameId: source.gameId,
      gameVersion: source.gameVersion,
      creatorWallet: options.creatorWallet,
      prizePoolAmount: prizePool,
      prizeAsset: source.prizeAsset,
      vaultAddress: source.vaultAddress,
      maxParticipants: source.maxParticipants,
      currentParticipants: 0,
      startTime: options.startTime,
      endTime: options.endTime,
      state: 'DRAFT',
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
    };

    StoreService.saveTournament(cloned);

    // Audit log cloning event
    StoreService.logAudit('TOURNAMENT_CLONED', options.creatorWallet, {
      sourceTournamentId: options.sourceTournamentId,
      newTournamentId: options.newTournamentId,
      title: options.title,
    });

    return cloned;
  }
}
```

`src/services/tournament-refund.worker.ts`:

```ts
import { StoreService } from './store.service';
import { EventBusService } from './event-bus.service';
import { WebhookService } from './webhook.service';
import { Tournament } from '../types';

export interface RefundResult {
  tournamentId: string;
  creatorWallet: string;
  refundAmount: number;
  prizeAsset: string;
  reason: string;
  processedAt: number;
}

/**
 * Automated worker identifying cancelled or under-subscribed tournaments with
 * idle escrowed prize pool balances and dispatching refund events.
 */
export class TournamentRefundWorker {
  private static timer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
  private static running: boolean = false;
  private static processedRefunds: Set<string> = new Set();

  /**
   * Resets internal in-memory tracker (used in unit tests).
   */
  public static reset(): void {
    this.processedRefunds.clear();
    this.stop();
  }

  /**
   * Scans for tournaments eligible for refunds:
   * 1. State is 'CANCELLED'
   * 2. State is 'FINALIZED' but participants is 0 (uncontested)
   * 3. Has unspent prizePoolAmount > 0 and has not yet been refunded
   */
  public static processPendingRefunds(currentTime: number = Date.now()): RefundResult[] {
    const tournaments = StoreService.getAllTournaments();
    const refunds: RefundResult[] = [];

    for (const t of tournaments) {
      if (this.processedRefunds.has(t.id)) {
        continue;
      }

      const isCancelled = t.state === 'CANCELLED';
      const isUncontested =
        t.state === 'FINALIZED' && (t.currentParticipants === 0 || !t.currentParticipants);

      if ((isCancelled || isUncontested) && t.prizePoolAmount > 0) {
        const reason = isCancelled ? 'TOURNAMENT_CANCELLED' : 'ZERO_PARTICIPANTS_UNCONTESTED';

        const result: RefundResult = {
          tournamentId: t.id,
          creatorWallet: t.creatorWallet,
          refundAmount: t.prizePoolAmount,
          prizeAsset: t.prizeAsset,
          reason,
          processedAt: currentTime,
        };

        this.processedRefunds.add(t.id);
        refunds.push(result);

        // Broadcast platform SSE event
        EventBusService.broadcast('TOURNAMENT_FINALIZED', {
          tournamentId: t.id,
          action: 'REFUND_DISPATCHED',
          refundAmount: t.prizePoolAmount,
          recipient: t.creatorWallet,
          reason,
        });

        // Fire outbound webhook
        WebhookService.dispatch('tournament.created', {
          event: 'tournament.refund_issued',
          tournamentId: t.id,
          recipient: t.creatorWallet,
          amount: t.prizePoolAmount,
          asset: t.prizeAsset,
          reason,
        }).catch(() => {
          // Non-blocking webhook dispatch
        });

        // Audit log
        StoreService.logAudit('TOURNAMENT_REFUND_PROCESSED', 'refund-worker', {
          tournamentId: t.id,
          recipient: t.creatorWallet,
          amount: t.prizePoolAmount,
          reason,
        });
      }
    }

    return refunds;
  }

  /**
   * Starts periodic polling worker.
   */
  public static start(intervalMs: number = 20_000): void {
    if (this.running) return;

    this.running = true;
    this.timer = setInterval(() => {
      try {
        this.processPendingRefunds();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error during tournament refund cycle', err);
      }
    }, intervalMs);

    if (this.timer.unref) {
      this.timer.unref();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Stops the background worker.
   */
  public static stop(): void {
    if (this.timer) {
      clearInterval(this.timer);
      this.timer = null;
    }
    this.running = false;
  }
}
```

`src/services/transaction-poller.service.ts`:

```ts
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

export interface TransactionPollStatus {
  txHash: string;
  status: 'SUCCESS' | 'FAILED' | 'NOT_FOUND' | 'PENDING';
  ledger?: number;
  createdAt?: string;
  envelopeXdr?: string;
  resultXdr?: string;
  errorResultXdr?: string;
  polledAt: number;
}

export class TransactionStatusPollerService {
  private rpcUrl: string;

  constructor(rpcUrl: string = CONFIG.SOROBAN_RPC_URL) {
    this.rpcUrl = rpcUrl;
  }

  public async getStatus(txHash: string): Promise<TransactionPollStatus> {
    if (!txHash || typeof txHash !== 'string') {
      return {
        txHash: txHash || '',
        status: 'FAILED',
        polledAt: Date.now(),
      };
    }

    try {
      const response = await fetch(this.rpcUrl, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          jsonrpc: '2.0',
          id: 1,
          method: 'getTransaction',
          params: { hash: txHash },
        }),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        return { txHash, status: 'NOT_FOUND', polledAt: Date.now() };
      }

      const data = (await response.json()) as any;
      if (data.error || !data.result) {
        return { txHash, status: 'NOT_FOUND', polledAt: Date.now() };
      }

      const res = data.result;
      const status = res.status as 'SUCCESS' | 'FAILED' | 'NOT_FOUND';
      return {
        txHash,
        status: status || 'PENDING',
        ledger: res.latestLedger,
        createdAt: res.createdAt,
        envelopeXdr: res.envelopeXdr,
        resultXdr: res.resultXdr,
        errorResultXdr: res.resultErrorXdr,
        polledAt: Date.now(),
      };
    } catch {
      // Mock result for offline testing
      return {
        txHash,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        ledger: 123456,
        polledAt: Date.now(),
      };
    }
  }
}

export const transactionStatusPoller = new TransactionStatusPollerService();
```

`src/services/wallet-balance-tracker.service.ts`:

```ts
import { CONFIG } from '../config';

export interface TokenBalance {
  assetCode: string;
  assetIssuer?: string;
  balance: string;
  stroops: string;
  isNative: boolean;
}

export interface WalletBalancesResult {
  address: string;
  exists: boolean;
  balances: TokenBalance[];
  nativeBalanceXlm: string;
  fetchedAt: number;
}

export class WalletBalanceTrackerService {
  private horizonUrl: string;
  private cache: Map<string, { data: WalletBalancesResult; expiresAt: number }> = new Map();
  private cacheTtlMs: number = 10_000;

  constructor(horizonUrl: string = CONFIG.HORIZON_URL) {
    this.horizonUrl = horizonUrl;
  }

  public async getBalances(address: string): Promise<WalletBalancesResult> {
    const cached = this.cache.get(address);
    if (cached && Date.now() < cached.expiresAt) {
      return cached.data;
    }

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${this.horizonUrl}/accounts/${address}`);
      if (!response.ok) {
        const notFoundResult: WalletBalancesResult = {
          address,
          exists: false,
          balances: [],
          nativeBalanceXlm: '0',
          fetchedAt: Date.now(),
        };
        this.cache.set(address, { data: notFoundResult, expiresAt: Date.now() + this.cacheTtlMs });
        return notFoundResult;
      }

      const accountData = (await response.json()) as any;
      const rawBalances = Array.isArray(accountData.balances) ? accountData.balances : [];

      let nativeBalanceXlm = '0';
      const balances: TokenBalance[] = rawBalances.map((b: any) => {
        const isNative = b.asset_type === 'native';
        const code = isNative ? 'XLM' : b.asset_code || 'UNKNOWN';
        const balance = b.balance || '0';
        if (isNative) {
          nativeBalanceXlm = balance;
        }
        const stroops = Math.round(parseFloat(balance) * 10_000_000).toString();
        return {
          assetCode: code,
          assetIssuer: b.asset_issuer,
          balance,
          stroops,
          isNative,
        };
      });

      const result: WalletBalancesResult = {
        address,
        exists: true,
        balances,
        nativeBalanceXlm,
        fetchedAt: Date.now(),
      };
      this.cache.set(address, { data: result, expiresAt: Date.now() + this.cacheTtlMs });
      return result;
    } catch {
      // Mock result for offline testing
      return {
        address,
        exists: true,
        balances: [
          {
            assetCode: 'XLM',
            balance: '10000.0000000',
            stroops: '100000000000',
            isNative: true,
          },
        ],
        nativeBalanceXlm: '10000.0000000',
        fetchedAt: Date.now(),
      };
    }
  }

  public clearCache(): void {
    this.cache.clear();
  }
}

export const walletBalanceTracker = new WalletBalanceTrackerService();

```

`src/services/webhook-dispatcher.service.ts`:

```ts
import crypto from 'crypto';
import {
  WebhookDeliveryAttempt,
  WebhookEventType,
  WebhookPayload,
  WebhookRegistration,
} from '../types/webhook-dispatcher';

export class WebhookDispatcherService {
  private registrations: Map<string, WebhookRegistration> = new Map();
  private deliveryHistory: WebhookDeliveryAttempt[] = [];

  public registerWebhook(
    url: string,
    events: WebhookEventType[],
    secret?: string,
  ): WebhookRegistration {
    const id = `wh_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`;
    const reg: WebhookRegistration = {
      id,
      url,
      events,
      secret: secret || crypto.randomBytes(24).toString('hex'),
      isActive: true,
      createdAt: Date.now(),
    };
    this.registrations.set(id, reg);
    return reg;
  }

  public getWebhooks(): WebhookRegistration[] {
    return Array.from(this.registrations.values());
  }

  public deleteWebhook(id: string): boolean {
    return this.registrations.delete(id);
  }

  public createSignature(payload: string, secret: string): string {
    return crypto.createHmac('sha256', secret).update(payload).digest('hex');
  }

  public async dispatchEvent(
    eventType: WebhookEventType,
    data: Record<string, any>,
  ): Promise<WebhookDeliveryAttempt[]> {
    const eventId = `ev_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`;
    const payload: WebhookPayload = {
      eventId,
      eventType,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      data,
    };
    const bodyStr = JSON.stringify(payload);
    const attempts: WebhookDeliveryAttempt[] = [];

    for (const webhook of this.registrations.values()) {
      if (!webhook.isActive || !webhook.events.includes(eventType)) continue;

      const signature = this.createSignature(bodyStr, webhook.secret);
      const attemptId = `att_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`;

      try {
        const res = await fetch(webhook.url, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'X-Stellar-Splash-Signature': signature,
            'X-Stellar-Splash-Event': eventType,
          },
          body: bodyStr,
        });

        const attempt: WebhookDeliveryAttempt = {
          id: attemptId,
          webhookId: webhook.id,
          eventId,
          statusCode: res.status,
          success: res.ok,
          attemptedAt: Date.now(),
        };
        attempts.push(attempt);
        this.deliveryHistory.push(attempt);
      } catch (err: any) {
        const attempt: WebhookDeliveryAttempt = {
          id: attemptId,
          webhookId: webhook.id,
          eventId,
          success: false,
          error: err?.message || 'Network request failed',
          attemptedAt: Date.now(),
        };
        attempts.push(attempt);
        this.deliveryHistory.push(attempt);
      }
    }

    return attempts;
  }

  public getHistory(limit: number = 50): WebhookDeliveryAttempt[] {
    return this.deliveryHistory.slice(-limit);
  }

  public clear(): void {
    this.registrations.clear();
    this.deliveryHistory = [];
  }
}

export const webhookDispatcher = new WebhookDispatcherService();

```

`src/services/webhook.service.ts`:

```ts
import crypto from 'crypto';

export interface WebhookConfig {
  id: string;
  url: string;
  secret: string;
  events: string[];
  active: boolean;
  description?: string;
}

export interface WebhookDeliveryReport {
  webhookId: string;
  deliveryId: string;
  url: string;
  event: string;
  statusCode?: number;
  success: boolean;
  error?: string;
  durationMs: number;
  timestamp: number;
}

export class WebhookService {
  private static webhooks: Map<string, WebhookConfig> = new Map();
  private static deliveryHistory: WebhookDeliveryReport[] = [];
  private static readonly MAX_HISTORY = 100;

  /**
   * Registers a new outgoing webhook endpoint.
   */
  public static registerWebhook(
    config: Omit<WebhookConfig, 'id'> & { id?: string },
  ): WebhookConfig {
    const id = config.id || `wh-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
    const fullConfig: WebhookConfig = {
      ...config,
      id,
    };
    this.webhooks.set(id, fullConfig);
    return fullConfig;
  }

  /**
   * Removes a registered webhook by ID.
   */
  public static removeWebhook(id: string): boolean {
    return this.webhooks.delete(id);
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves all registered webhooks.
   */
  public static getWebhooks(): WebhookConfig[] {
    return Array.from(this.webhooks.values());
  }

  /**
   * Clears all registered webhooks and history.
   */
  public static clear(): void {
    this.webhooks.clear();
    this.deliveryHistory = [];
  }

  /**
   * Computes HMAC-SHA256 signature for payload verification.
   */
  public static generateSignature(payloadString: string, secret: string): string {
    return crypto.createHmac('sha256', secret).update(payloadString).digest('hex');
  }

  /**
   * Verifies an incoming or dispatched HMAC-SHA256 signature against payload.
   */
  public static verifySignature(
    payloadString: string,
    secret: string,
    receivedSignature: string,
  ): boolean {
    const cleanSignature = receivedSignature.replace(/^sha256=/, '');
    const expected = this.generateSignature(payloadString, secret);
    return crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(cleanSignature, 'hex'), Buffer.from(expected, 'hex'));
  }

  /**
   * Dispatches an event to all subscribed active webhooks.
   */
  public static async dispatch(
    event: string,
    data: Record<string, unknown>,
  ): Promise<WebhookDeliveryReport[]> {
    const matchingWebhooks = Array.from(this.webhooks.values()).filter(
      (w) => w.active && (w.events.includes(event) || w.events.includes('*')),
    );

    if (matchingWebhooks.length === 0) {
      return [];
    }

    const payload = {
      id: `evt-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`,
      event,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      data,
    };

    const payloadString = JSON.stringify(payload);
    const reports: WebhookDeliveryReport[] = [];

    for (const hook of matchingWebhooks) {
      const deliveryId = `del-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`;
      const signature = this.generateSignature(payloadString, hook.secret);
      const startTime = Date.now();

      try {
        const response = await fetch(hook.url, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'X-StellarSplash-Event': event,
            'X-StellarSplash-Delivery': deliveryId,
            'X-StellarSplash-Signature': `sha256=${signature}`,
          },
          body: payloadString,
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000),
        });

        const report: WebhookDeliveryReport = {
          webhookId: hook.id,
          deliveryId,
          url: hook.url,
          event,
          statusCode: response.status,
          success: response.ok,
          durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
        };
        reports.push(report);
        this.recordDelivery(report);
      } catch (err: any) {
        const report: WebhookDeliveryReport = {
          webhookId: hook.id,
          deliveryId,
          url: hook.url,
          event,
          success: false,
          error: err.message || 'Network request failed',
          durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
        };
        reports.push(report);
        this.recordDelivery(report);
      }
    }

    return reports;
  }

  private static recordDelivery(report: WebhookDeliveryReport): void {
    this.deliveryHistory.unshift(report);
    if (this.deliveryHistory.length > this.MAX_HISTORY) {
      this.deliveryHistory.pop();
    }
  }

  public static getDeliveryHistory(): WebhookDeliveryReport[] {
    return [...this.deliveryHistory];
  }
}
```

`src/tests/agreement.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { AgreementService } from '../services/agreement.service';
import { StoreService } from '../services/store.service';
import { AllocationRule } from '../types';

describe('Prize Agreement Financial Rules & Invariants', () => {
  const validRules: AllocationRule[] = [
    { rank: 1, basisPoints: 5000, label: '1st Place' }, // 50%
    { rank: 2, basisPoints: 3000, label: '2nd Place' }, // 30%
    { rank: 3, basisPoints: 2000, label: '3rd Place' }, // 20%
  ];

  it('successfully creates an agreement when allocation equals exactly 10,000 basis points', () => {
    const agreement = AgreementService.createAgreement({
      tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
      version: 1,
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 100,
      allocationRules: validRules,
      createdBy: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
    });

    expect(agreement.agreementHash).toBeDefined();
    expect(agreement.agreementHash.length).toBe(64);
    expect(agreement.status).toBe('PENDING_APPROVAL');
    expect(agreement.version).toBe(1);
  });

  it('strictly rejects agreements where basis points do not sum to 10,000 (over-allocation)', () => {
    const invalidRules: AllocationRule[] = [
      { rank: 1, basisPoints: 6000, label: '1st' },
      { rank: 2, basisPoints: 5000, label: '2nd' }, // 6000 + 5000 = 11000
    ];

    expect(() => {
      AgreementService.createAgreement({
        tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
        version: 2,
        prizeAsset: 'USDC',
        prizePoolAmount: 100,
        allocationRules: invalidRules,
        createdBy: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
      });
    }).toThrow(/must equal exactly 10,000/);
  });

  it('strictly rejects agreements where basis points are under-allocated', () => {
    const invalidRules: AllocationRule[] = [
      { rank: 1, basisPoints: 5000, label: '1st' },
      { rank: 2, basisPoints: 3000, label: '2nd' }, // 8000 bps
    ];

    expect(() => {
      AgreementService.createAgreement({
        tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
        version: 2,
        prizeAsset: 'USDC',
        prizePoolAmount: 100,
        allocationRules: invalidRules,
        createdBy: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
      });
    }).toThrow(/must equal exactly 10,000/);
  });

  it('calculates deterministic payouts with remainder dust awarded to Rank 1', () => {
    // 10001 units with 50/30/20:
    // 50% = 5000.5 -> floor = 5000
    // 30% = 3000.3 -> floor = 3000
    // 20% = 2000.2 -> floor = 2000
    // Sum = 10000. Dust = 1 unit.
    // Rank 1 gets 5000 + 1 = 5001.
    const allocations = AgreementService.calculateSettlementAllocations(10001, validRules);

    expect(allocations[0].amount).toBe(5001); // Rank 1 dust recipient
    expect(allocations[1].amount).toBe(3000);
    expect(allocations[2].amount).toBe(2000);

    const totalDistributed = allocations.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr.amount, 0);
    expect(totalDistributed).toBe(10001);
  });

  it('generates different agreement hashes when material terms change', () => {
    const ag1 = AgreementService.createAgreement({
      tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
      version: 1,
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 100,
      allocationRules: validRules,
      createdBy: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
    });

    const ag2 = AgreementService.createAgreement({
      tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
      version: 1,
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 200, // Changed pool amount
      allocationRules: validRules,
      createdBy: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
    });

    expect(ag1.agreementHash).not.toBe(ag2.agreementHash);
  });

  it('enforces immutability: locked agreements cannot be mutated or overwritten', () => {
    const agreement = AgreementService.createAgreement({
      tournamentId: 'tourn-immutable-test',
      version: 1,
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 50,
      allocationRules: validRules,
      createdBy: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
    });

    agreement.status = 'LOCKED';
    StoreService.saveAgreement(agreement);

    // Attempting to overwrite locked agreement with different status should fail
    const mutated = { ...agreement, prizePoolAmount: 999 };
    expect(() => {
      StoreService.saveAgreement(mutated);
    }).toThrow(/Locked agreements cannot be modified/);
  });

  it('rejects invalid parameters and payloads via Zod schema validation', async () => {
    const { createApp } = await import('../app');
    const request = (await import('supertest')).default;
    const app = createApp();

    // 1. Invalid version in URL params (non-numeric)
    const resBadParam = await request(app)
      .post('/api/agreements/tourn-1/not-a-number/approve')
      .send({
        approverWallet: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
      });
    expect(resBadParam.status).toBe(400);
    expect(resBadParam.body.success).toBe(false);

    // 2. Invalid basis points in POST /api/agreements payload (sum != 10000)
    const resBadPayload = await request(app)
      .post('/api/agreements')
      .send({
        tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
        version: 1,
        prizeAsset: 'USDC',
        prizePoolAmount: 100,
        allocationRules: [
          { rank: 1, basisPoints: 8000, label: '1st' }, // 8000 != 10000
        ],
        createdBy: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
      });
    expect(resBadPayload.status).toBe(400);
    expect(resBadPayload.body.success).toBe(false);
    expect(JSON.stringify(resBadPayload.body.errors)).toContain(
      'Sum of basis points across all allocation rules must equal exactly 10,000',
    );
  });
});
```

`src/tests/api.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '../app';

const app = createApp();

describe('Stellar Splash Engine API', () => {
  it('GET /api/health returns operational status and telemetry metrics', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/health');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.status).toBe('ok');
    expect(res.body.service).toBe('stellar-splash-engine');
    expect(res.body.network).toBe('TESTNET');
    expect(typeof res.body.uptimeSeconds).toBe('number');
    expect(res.body.memory.rssMb).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(res.body.memory.heapUsedMb).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(res.body.process.nodeVersion).toBeDefined();
    expect(res.body.soroban.contractId).toBe(
      'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
    );
  });

  it('GET /api/tournaments lists tournaments with initial seed', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/tournaments');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.tournaments.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(res.body.tournaments[0].id).toBe('tourn-splash-001');
  });

  it('POST /api/tournaments creates a tournament in FUNDING state', async () => {
    const newTourn = {
      name: 'Soroban Speedrun #002',
      description: 'Competitive speed trial on Stellar Testnet',
      creatorWallet: 'GB6V2A73VUX6KAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2',
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 50,
      maxParticipants: 16,
      startTime: Date.now() + 1000,
      endTime: Date.now() + 86400 * 1000,
    };

    const res = await request(app).post('/api/tournaments').send(newTourn);
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.tournament.state).toBe('FUNDING');
    expect(res.body.tournament.prizePoolAmount).toBe(50);
  });

  it('Player can join an OPEN tournament, duplicate joining is rejected', async () => {
    const playerWallet = 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB';

    // 1. Join open tournament
    const joinRes = await request(app)
      .post('/api/tournaments/tourn-splash-001/join')
      .send({ playerWallet });
    expect(joinRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(joinRes.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(joinRes.body.participant.playerWallet).toBe(playerWallet);

    // 2. Duplicate join should return 409
    const dupRes = await request(app)
      .post('/api/tournaments/tourn-splash-001/join')
      .send({ playerWallet });
    expect(dupRes.status).toBe(409);
    expect(dupRes.body.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('Complete Game lifecycle: start session, complete match, verify score', async () => {
    const playerWallet = 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB';

    // 1. Start game session
    const startRes = await request(app).post('/api/games/start').send({
      tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
      playerWallet,
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
    });

    expect(startRes.status).toBe(201);
    const { matchId, sessionToken } = startRes.body.match;
    expect(matchId).toBeDefined();
    expect(sessionToken).toBeDefined();

    // 2. Complete game session with events
    const completeRes = await request(app)
      .post('/api/games/complete')
      .send({
        matchId,
        sessionToken,
        endTime: Date.now() + 45000,
        events: [
          { seq: 1, timestamp: 1000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 200, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
          { seq: 2, timestamp: 2000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 300, combo: 2, multiplier: 2 },
          { seq: 3, timestamp: 3000, type: 'HAZARD_HIT', points: 50, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 },
        ],
      });

    expect(completeRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(completeRes.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(completeRes.body.result.finalScore).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(completeRes.body.result.resultHash).toHaveLength(64);

    // 3. Idempotent complete: resubmitting same match returns success with cached result
    const reSubmitRes = await request(app)
      .post('/api/games/complete')
      .send({
        matchId,
        sessionToken,
        endTime: Date.now() + 45000,
        events: [],
      });
    expect(reSubmitRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(reSubmitRes.body.message).toContain('idempotent');

    // 4. Export match replay for transparent auditing
    const exportRes = await request(app).get(`/api/games/matches/${matchId}/export`);
    expect(exportRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(exportRes.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(exportRes.body.matchId).toBe(matchId);
    expect(exportRes.body.events.length).toBe(3);
    expect(exportRes.body.result).toBeDefined();
    expect(exportRes.body.result.resultHash).toBe(completeRes.body.result.resultHash);

    // 5. Verify leaderboard reflects score
    const lbRes = await request(app).get('/api/tournaments/tourn-splash-001/leaderboard');
    expect(lbRes.status).toBe(200);
    const entry = lbRes.body.leaderboard.find(
      (e: { playerWallet: string }) => e.playerWallet === playerWallet,
    );
    expect(entry).toBeDefined();
    expect(entry.currentScore).toBe(completeRes.body.result.finalScore);
    expect(entry.scoreLabel).toBe('Current Score');

    // 6. Replay attack rejection: Another match attempting to submit duplicate event stream
    const player2 = 'GB6V2A73VUX6KAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2';
    await request(app)
      .post('/api/tournaments/tourn-splash-001/join')
      .send({ playerWallet: player2 });
    const start2 = await request(app).post('/api/games/start').send({
      tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
      playerWallet: player2,
    });
    const replayAttackRes = await request(app)
      .post('/api/games/complete')
      .send({
        matchId: start2.body.match.matchId,
        sessionToken: start2.body.match.sessionToken,
        endTime: Date.now() + 45000,
        events: [
          { seq: 1, timestamp: 1000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 200, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
          { seq: 2, timestamp: 2000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 300, combo: 2, multiplier: 2 },
          { seq: 3, timestamp: 3000, type: 'HAZARD_HIT', points: 50, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 },
        ],
      });
    expect(replayAttackRes.status).toBe(409);
    expect(replayAttackRes.body.error).toContain('REPLAY_ATTACK_DETECTED');
  });

  it('GET /api/reconciliation returns reconciled status for funded tournaments', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/reconciliation/tourn-splash-001');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.reconciliation.status).toBe('RECONCILED');
    expect(res.body.reconciliation.expectedAmount).toBe(100);
    expect(res.body.reconciliation.onChainAmount).toBe(100);
  });

  it('Verification API: verify match, inspect verification record and attestation', async () => {
    // 1. Verify seed match
    const res = await request(app).post('/api/verification/match/match-genesis-001');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.verification.status).toBe('VERIFIED');
    expect(res.body.verification.attestation).toBeDefined();

    // 2. Fetch verification record
    const getRes = await request(app).get('/api/verification/match/match-genesis-001');
    expect(getRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(getRes.body.verification.resultHash).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('Prize Agreement API: list, create, approve, and lock agreement', async () => {
    const tournamentId = 'tourn-splash-001';
    const creatorWallet = 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB';

    // 1. List existing agreements
    const listRes = await request(app).get(`/api/agreements/${tournamentId}`);
    expect(listRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(listRes.body.agreements.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // 2. Create version 2 with updated tiers
    const createRes = await request(app)
      .post('/api/agreements')
      .send({
        tournamentId,
        version: 2,
        prizeAsset: 'USDC',
        prizePoolAmount: 100,
        allocationRules: [
          { rank: 1, basisPoints: 6000, label: '1st (60%)' },
          { rank: 2, basisPoints: 2500, label: '2nd (25%)' },
          { rank: 3, basisPoints: 1500, label: '3rd (15%)' },
        ],
        createdBy: creatorWallet,
      });

    expect(createRes.status).toBe(201);
    expect(createRes.body.agreement.version).toBe(2);
    expect(createRes.body.agreement.agreementHash).toBeDefined();

    // 3. Approve version 2
    const approveRes = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/agreements/${tournamentId}/2/approve`)
      .send({ approverWallet: creatorWallet });
    expect(approveRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(approveRes.body.agreement.status).toBe('READY_TO_LOCK');

    // 4. Lock version 2
    const lockRes = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/agreements/${tournamentId}/2/lock`)
      .send({ actorWallet: creatorWallet });
    expect(lockRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(lockRes.body.agreement.status).toBe('LOCKED');

    // 5. Verify active agreement is now version 2
    const activeRes = await request(app).get(`/api/agreements/${tournamentId}/active`);
    expect(activeRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(activeRes.body.agreement.version).toBe(2);
    expect(activeRes.body.agreement.status).toBe('LOCKED');
  });

  it('Rankings API: view verified ranking preview and finalize rankings', async () => {
    const tournamentId = 'tourn-splash-001';
    const creatorWallet = 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB';

    // 1. Get preview ranking
    const getRes = await request(app).get(`/api/rankings/${tournamentId}`);
    expect(getRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(getRes.body.ranking.entries.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // 2. Finalize ranking
    const finalizeRes = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/rankings/${tournamentId}/finalize`)
      .send({ finalizedBy: creatorWallet });
    expect(finalizeRes.status).toBe(201);
    expect(finalizeRes.body.ranking.rankingHash).toHaveLength(64);

    // 3. Immutability: Attempting to finalize again returns 409
    const secondFinalize = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/rankings/${tournamentId}/finalize`)
      .send({ finalizedBy: creatorWallet });
    expect(secondFinalize.status).toBe(409);
  });

  it('Rejects unauthenticated channel registration with HTTP 403', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).post('/api/channels/register').send({
      publicKey: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
      secretKey: 'SCXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX',
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('Rejects game completion submissions with out-of-bounds scores', async () => {
    const inflatedRes = await request(app)
      .post('/api/games/complete')
      .send({
        matchId: 'match-bounds-test',
        sessionToken: 'token-abc-123',
        endTime: Date.now() + 45000,
        events: [
          { seq: 1, timestamp: 1000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 9999999, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
        ],
      });
    expect(inflatedRes.status).toBe(400);
    expect(inflatedRes.body.success).toBe(false);
    expect(inflatedRes.body.errors).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('Verifies global security headers on API responses', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/health');
    expect(res.headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(res.headers['x-frame-options']).toBe('DENY');
    expect(res.headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('strict-origin-when-cross-origin');
  });
});
```

`src/tests/attestation.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { AttestationService } from '../services/attestation.service';
import { ResultVerification } from '../types';

describe('AttestationService with Ed25519 Cryptographic Signatures', () => {
  const sampleVerification: ResultVerification = {
    matchId: 'match-101',
    tournamentId: 'tourn-1',
    playerWallet: 'GCQ3OLBMJUGWN3PHYE3SDIKKIPVLJSFFWG4Q4CEABGHWGBEBPBKMY6OW',
    submittedScore: 1250,
    replayedScore: 1250,
    accuracy: 94.5,
    eventCount: 45,
    resultHash: '0x1234567890abcdef1234567890abcdef1234567890abcdef1234567890abcdef',
    verificationVersion: '1.0.0',
    verifier: 'Stellar Splash Engine v1',
    verifiedAt: Date.now(),
    status: 'VERIFIED',
  };

  it('generates an attestation with a valid Ed25519 signature', () => {
    const attestation = AttestationService.createAttestation(sampleVerification);

    expect(attestation.attestationId).toMatch(/^attest-/);
    expect(attestation.signature).toBeDefined();
    expect(attestation.signature?.length).toBeGreaterThan(64);
    expect(attestation.signerPublicKey).toBeDefined();
    expect(attestation.signerPublicKey?.startsWith('G')).toBe(true);

    const isValid = AttestationService.verifyAttestation(attestation, sampleVerification);
    expect(isValid).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects tampered verification score', () => {
    const attestation = AttestationService.createAttestation(sampleVerification);

    const tamperedVerification: ResultVerification = {
      ...sampleVerification,
      replayedScore: 9999, // tampered score
    };

    const isValid = AttestationService.verifyAttestation(attestation, tamperedVerification);
    expect(isValid).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects forged attestation signature', () => {
    const attestation = AttestationService.createAttestation(sampleVerification);

    const originalSig = attestation.signature || '';
    const corruptChar = originalSig.charAt(0) === 'a' ? 'b' : 'a';
    const forgedAttestation = {
      ...attestation,
      signature: corruptChar + originalSig.slice(1),
    };

    const isValid = AttestationService.verifyAttestation(forgedAttestation, sampleVerification);
    expect(isValid).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`src/tests/error-middleware.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import express from 'express';
import { createApp } from '../app';
import { AppError, errorHandler, notFoundHandler } from '../middleware/error.middleware';

describe('Error Middleware', () => {
  it('returns normalized JSON 404 for unmapped endpoints', async () => {
    const app = createApp();
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/unmapped-endpoint-random');

    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(false);
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('NOT_FOUND');
    expect(res.body.message).toContain('not found');
    expect(res.body.timestamp).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('handles custom AppError with specified status code and error code', async () => {
    const testApp = express();
    testApp.use(express.json());
    testApp.get('/test-error', () => {
      throw new AppError('Unauthorized token', 401, 'UNAUTHORIZED_ACCESS', { reason: 'expired' });
    });
    testApp.use(errorHandler);

    const res = await request(testApp).get('/test-error');
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(false);
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('UNAUTHORIZED_ACCESS');
    expect(res.body.message).toBe('Unauthorized token');
    expect(res.body.details).toEqual({ reason: 'expired' });
  });

  it('handles malformed JSON body with normalized 400 error', async () => {
    const testApp = express();
    testApp.use(express.json());
    testApp.post('/test-json', (req, res) => res.json({ ok: true }));
    testApp.use(errorHandler);

    const res = await request(testApp)
      .post('/test-json')
      .set('Content-Type', 'application/json')
      .send('{ "brokenJson: true '); // deliberately broken

    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(false);
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('INVALID_JSON_BODY');
  });
});
```

`src/tests/event-bus.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { EventBusService } from '../services/event-bus.service';
import { Response } from 'express';

describe('EventBusService with SSE Heartbeat', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    EventBusService.stopHeartbeat();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    EventBusService.stopHeartbeat();
  });

  it('manages subscribers and tracks active count', () => {
    const mockRes = {
      write: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as Response;

    EventBusService.addSubscriber(mockRes);
    expect(EventBusService.getSubscriberCount()).toBe(1);

    EventBusService.broadcast('TOURNAMENT_CREATED', { tournamentId: 't1' });
    expect(mockRes.write).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.stringContaining('event: TOURNAMENT_CREATED'),
    );

    EventBusService.removeSubscriber(mockRes);
    expect(EventBusService.getSubscriberCount()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('automatically prunes disconnected client when write fails', () => {
    const brokenRes = {
      write: vi.fn().mockImplementation(() => {
        throw new Error('Broken pipe');
      }),
    } as unknown as Response;

    EventBusService.addSubscriber(brokenRes);
    expect(EventBusService.getSubscriberCount()).toBe(1);

    EventBusService.broadcast('MATCH_STARTED', { matchId: 'm1' });
    expect(EventBusService.getSubscriberCount()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('reports telemetry accurately and filters broadcast recipients', () => {
    const client1 = {
      write: vi.fn(),
      locals: { channel: 'alpha' },
    } as unknown as Response;

    const client2 = {
      write: vi.fn(),
      locals: { channel: 'beta' },
    } as unknown as Response;

    EventBusService.addSubscriber(client1);
    EventBusService.addSubscriber(client2);

    const telemetryBefore = EventBusService.getTelemetry();
    expect(telemetryBefore.activeSubscribers).toBe(2);

    EventBusService.broadcastToFiltered(
      'LEADERBOARD_UPDATED',
      { scores: [100, 90] },
      (res: any) => res.locals?.channel === 'alpha',
    );

    expect(client1.write).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(client2.write).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    EventBusService.removeSubscriber(client1);
    EventBusService.removeSubscriber(client2);
  });

  it('prunes clients marked as writableEnded or destroyed', () => {
    const endedClient = {
      write: vi.fn(),
      writableEnded: true,
    } as unknown as Response;

    EventBusService.addSubscriber(endedClient);
    expect(EventBusService.getSubscriberCount()).toBe(1);

    const pruned = EventBusService.pruneStaleConnections();
    expect(pruned).toBe(1);
    expect(EventBusService.getSubscriberCount()).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`src/tests/latency-timer.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { latencyTimerMiddleware } from '../middleware/latency-timer.middleware';
import { MetricsService } from '../services/metrics.service';
import { Request, Response } from 'express';
import EventEmitter from 'events';

describe('latencyTimerMiddleware', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    MetricsService.reset();
  });

  it('sets X-Response-Time header and records Prometheus HTTP metrics on finish', async () => {
    const emitter = new EventEmitter();
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};

    const req = {
      method: 'GET',
      path: '/api/tournaments',
      url: '/api/tournaments',
    } as unknown as Request;

    const res = Object.assign(emitter, {
      headersSent: false,
      statusCode: 200,
      setHeader: vi.fn((key: string, val: string) => {
        headers[key.toLowerCase()] = val;
      }),
      writeHead: vi.fn(),
    }) as unknown as Response;

    const next = vi.fn();

    latencyTimerMiddleware(req, res, next);
    expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

    // Trigger writeHead
    (res as any).writeHead(200);
    expect(res.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      'X-Response-Time',
      expect.stringMatching(/^\d+(\.\d+)?ms$/),
    );

    // Emit response finish
    emitter.emit('finish');

    const metrics = MetricsService.getMetrics();
    expect(metrics.httpRequests['GET_/api/tournaments_200']).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

`src/tests/lifecycle.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { LifecycleService, ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS } from '../services/lifecycle.service';
import { Tournament } from '../types';

const createMockTournament = (overrides?: Partial<Tournament>): Tournament => ({
  id: 'tourn-lifecycle-001',
  name: 'Lifecycle Championship',
  description: 'Testing lifecycle state machine',
  gameId: 'splash-rush',
  gameVersion: '1.0.0',
  creatorWallet: 'GBBD67VQMVO455V224GZ622D2J2J45LPOJ24G6V4I6J4S7PGLFOKP7V2',
  prizeAsset: 'XLM',
  prizePoolAmount: 500,
  vaultAddress: 'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
  maxParticipants: 16,
  currentParticipants: 8,
  startTime: Date.now() - 3600000,
  endTime: Date.now() + 3600000,
  state: 'DRAFT',
  createdAt: Date.now() - 7200000,
  updatedAt: Date.now() - 7200000,
  ...overrides,
});

describe('LifecycleService State Machine Validator', () => {
  it('correctly maps allowed state transitions', () => {
    expect(LifecycleService.canTransition('DRAFT', 'FUNDING')).toBe(true);
    expect(LifecycleService.canTransition('DRAFT', 'CANCELLED')).toBe(true);
    expect(LifecycleService.canTransition('DRAFT', 'SETTLED')).toBe(false);
    expect(LifecycleService.canTransition('OPEN', 'IN_PROGRESS')).toBe(true);
    expect(LifecycleService.canTransition('COMPLETED', 'OPEN')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects illegal non-adjacent transitions with descriptive AppError', () => {
    const tournament = createMockTournament({ state: 'DRAFT' });

    expect(() => {
      LifecycleService.validateTransition(tournament, 'SETTLED');
    }).toThrowError(/Illegal state transition from 'DRAFT' to 'SETTLED'/);
  });

  it('prohibits state changes from terminal states COMPLETED and CANCELLED', () => {
    const completed = createMockTournament({ state: 'COMPLETED' });
    expect(() => {
      LifecycleService.validateTransition(completed, 'OPEN');
    }).toThrowError(/terminal state 'COMPLETED'/);

    const cancelled = createMockTournament({ state: 'CANCELLED' });
    expect(() => {
      LifecycleService.validateTransition(cancelled, 'DRAFT');
    }).toThrowError(/terminal state 'CANCELLED'/);
  });

  it('enforces verified funding requirement when transitioning to OPEN', () => {
    const unfunded = createMockTournament({
      state: 'FUNDING',
      prizePoolAmount: 0,
      fundingTxHash: undefined,
    });

    expect(() => {
      LifecycleService.validateTransition(unfunded, 'OPEN');
    }).toThrowError(/verified on-chain prize funding/);
  });

  it('enforces ranking hash requirement when transitioning to SETTLING', () => {
    const tournament = createMockTournament({
      state: 'FINALIZED',
      finalRankingHash: undefined,
    });

    expect(() => {
      LifecycleService.validateTransition(tournament, 'SETTLING');
    }).toThrowError(/verified final ranking hash/i);
  });

  it('successfully transitions tournament and mutates updatedAt timestamp', () => {
    const tournament = createMockTournament({ state: 'DRAFT' });
    const initialUpdated = tournament.updatedAt;

    const event = LifecycleService.applyTransition(
      tournament,
      'FUNDING',
      'Creator requested funding phase',
    );

    expect(tournament.state).toBe('FUNDING');
    expect(tournament.updatedAt).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(initialUpdated);
    expect(event.fromState).toBe('DRAFT');
    expect(event.toState).toBe('FUNDING');
  });
});
```

`src/tests/persistent-store.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs';
import { PersistentStoreService } from '../services/persistent-store.service';

describe('PersistentStoreService', () => {
  const testDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'scratch-test-data');
  let store: PersistentStoreService;

  beforeEach(() => {
    if (fs.existsSync(testDir)) {
      fs.rmSync(testDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
    store = new PersistentStoreService({ dataDir: testDir });
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    if (fs.existsSync(testDir)) {
      fs.rmSync(testDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  it('creates target directory automatically', () => {
    expect(fs.existsSync(testDir)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('saves and loads JSON snapshots atomically', async () => {
    const payload = {
      tournaments: [
        { id: 't-1', name: 'Genesis Cup', prizePool: 500 },
        { id: 't-2', name: 'Stellar Splashdown', prizePool: 1200 },
      ],
      updatedAt: 1729000000,
    };

    await store.saveSnapshot('tournaments.json', payload);
    const loaded = await store.loadSnapshot('tournaments.json', { tournaments: [] });

    expect(loaded).toEqual(payload);
  });

  it('returns fallback data when snapshot file does not exist', async () => {
    const fallback = { count: 0, items: [] };
    const loaded = await store.loadSnapshot('non-existent.json', fallback);

    expect(loaded).toEqual(fallback);
  });

  it('deletes snapshot file cleanly', async () => {
    await store.saveSnapshot('temp.json', { ok: true });
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(testDir, 'temp.json'))).toBe(true);

    const deleted = await store.deleteSnapshot('temp.json');
    expect(deleted).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(testDir, 'temp.json'))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

describe('Postman Collection Verification', () => {
  const collectionPath = path.join(
    __dirname,
    '..',
    '..',
    'docs',
    'StellarSplash_Engine.postman_collection.json',
  );

  it('exists and is valid JSON', () => {
    expect(fs.existsSync(collectionPath)).toBe(true);
    const content = fs.readFileSync(collectionPath, 'utf8');
    const parsed = JSON.parse(content);
    expect(parsed.info).toBeDefined();
    expect(parsed.info.name).toBe('Stellar Splash Engine API');
    expect(parsed.info.schema).toContain('schema.getpostman.com');
  });

  it('contains expected endpoint groups', () => {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(collectionPath, 'utf8'));
    const groupNames = parsed.item.map((i: { name: string }) => i.name);
    expect(groupNames).toContain('Health & System');
    expect(groupNames).toContain('Tournaments & Verification');
    expect(groupNames).toContain('Live Events (SSE)');
  });
});
```

`src/tests/prng.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { PrngService } from '../services/prng.service';

describe('PrngService - Deterministic Seeded Target Generation', () => {
  it('computes deterministic 32-bit integer seeds from string match identifiers', () => {
    const seed1 = PrngService.hashStringToSeed('tourn-101:match-999:player-A');
    const seed2 = PrngService.hashStringToSeed('tourn-101:match-999:player-A');
    const seedDifferent = PrngService.hashStringToSeed('tourn-101:match-999:player-B');

    expect(seed1).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(seed1).toEqual(seed2);
    expect(seed1).not.toEqual(seedDifferent);
  });

  it('generates identical pseudorandom float sequences given identical seeds', () => {
    const prngA = PrngService.createMulberry32(123456789);
    const prngB = PrngService.createMulberry32(123456789);

    const seqA = [prngA(), prngA(), prngA(), prngA(), prngA()];
    const seqB = [prngB(), prngB(), prngB(), prngB(), prngB()];

    expect(seqA).toEqual(seqB);
  });

  it('generates deterministic target sequence bounded by canvas geometry and match duration', () => {
    const matchSeed = 'testnet-splash-match-uuid-55';
    const targets = PrngService.generateTargetSequence(matchSeed, 45000, 750, 790, 460);

    expect(targets.length).toBeGreaterThan(20);

    for (const t of targets) {
      expect(t.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(50);
      expect(t.x).toBeLessThanOrEqual(740);
      expect(t.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(50);
      expect(t.y).toBeLessThanOrEqual(410);
      expect(t.spawnTimeMs).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(t.expireTimeMs).toBeGreaterThan(t.spawnTimeMs);
      expect(['STANDARD', 'BURST', 'GOLDEN', 'HAZARD']).toContain(t.type);
    }

    // Verify reproducibility
    const duplicateTargets = PrngService.generateTargetSequence(matchSeed, 45000, 750, 790, 460);
    expect(duplicateTargets).toEqual(targets);
  });

  it('validates hit coordinates and timestamps against active target sequence', () => {
    const matchSeed = 'verifiable-hit-match-seed';
    const targets = PrngService.generateTargetSequence(matchSeed, 30000);
    const firstTarget = targets[0];

    // Valid hit centered on first target within its lifespan
    const hitResult = PrngService.validateHitAgainstTargetSequence(
      targets,
      firstTarget.x,
      firstTarget.y,
      firstTarget.spawnTimeMs + 500,
    );
    expect(hitResult.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(hitResult.target?.id).toEqual(firstTarget.id);

    // Hit after target expiration
    const expiredHit = PrngService.validateHitAgainstTargetSequence(
      targets,
      firstTarget.x,
      firstTarget.y,
      firstTarget.expireTimeMs + 500,
    );
    expect(expiredHit.valid).toBe(false);

    // Hit far outside target coordinates
    const missedHit = PrngService.validateHitAgainstTargetSequence(
      targets,
      firstTarget.x + 150,
      firstTarget.y + 150,
      firstTarget.spawnTimeMs + 500,
    );
    expect(missedHit.valid).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`src/tests/ranking.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { RankingService } from '../services/ranking.service';
import { ResultVerification, GameResult } from '../types';

describe('Deterministic Tournament Rankings Engine', () => {
  const tournamentId = 'tourn-ranking-test';

  const mockResults: GameResult[] = [
    {
      matchId: 'match-1',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: 'GA_PLAYER_1',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      startTime: 1000,
      endTime: 2000,
      durationMs: 1000,
      eventsCount: 10,
      baseScore: 1000,
      comboBonus: 0,
      multiplierBonus: 0,
      penalties: 0,
      finalScore: 1000,
      accuracy: 90,
      maxCombo: 5,
      resultHash: 'hash-1',
      submittedAt: 2050,
      verified: true,
    },
    {
      matchId: 'match-2',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: 'GA_PLAYER_2',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      startTime: 1000,
      endTime: 2000,
      durationMs: 1000,
      eventsCount: 10,
      baseScore: 1200,
      comboBonus: 0,
      multiplierBonus: 0,
      penalties: 0,
      finalScore: 1200, // Highest score
      accuracy: 85,
      maxCombo: 6,
      resultHash: 'hash-2',
      submittedAt: 2060,
      verified: true,
    },
    {
      matchId: 'match-3-tied-score',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: 'GA_PLAYER_3',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      startTime: 1000,
      endTime: 2000,
      durationMs: 1000,
      eventsCount: 10,
      baseScore: 1000,
      comboBonus: 0,
      multiplierBonus: 0,
      penalties: 0,
      finalScore: 1000, // Tied with Player 1, but higher accuracy
      accuracy: 98, // Higher accuracy => breaks tie
      maxCombo: 8,
      resultHash: 'hash-3',
      submittedAt: 2070,
      verified: true,
    },
    {
      matchId: 'match-4-unverified',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: 'GA_PLAYER_4',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      startTime: 1000,
      endTime: 2000,
      durationMs: 1000,
      eventsCount: 10,
      baseScore: 5000, // Highest submitted score, but UNVERIFIED!
      comboBonus: 0,
      multiplierBonus: 0,
      penalties: 0,
      finalScore: 5000,
      accuracy: 99,
      maxCombo: 10,
      resultHash: 'hash-4',
      submittedAt: 2080,
      verified: false,
    },
  ];

  const mockVerifications: ResultVerification[] = [
    {
      verificationId: 'v-1',
      matchId: 'match-1',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: 'GA_PLAYER_1',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      submittedScore: 1000,
      replayedScore: 1000,
      scoreMatch: true,
      resultHash: 'hash-1',
      verificationAlgorithm: 'DETERMINISTIC_REPLAY_ENGINE',
      verificationVersion: '1.0.0',
      verifier: 'verifier.stellar-splash.org',
      verifiedAt: 2100,
      status: 'VERIFIED',
    },
    {
      verificationId: 'v-2',
      matchId: 'match-2',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: 'GA_PLAYER_2',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      submittedScore: 1200,
      replayedScore: 1200,
      scoreMatch: true,
      resultHash: 'hash-2',
      verificationAlgorithm: 'DETERMINISTIC_REPLAY_ENGINE',
      verificationVersion: '1.0.0',
      verifier: 'verifier.stellar-splash.org',
      verifiedAt: 2105,
      status: 'VERIFIED',
    },
    {
      verificationId: 'v-3',
      matchId: 'match-3-tied-score',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: 'GA_PLAYER_3',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      submittedScore: 1000,
      replayedScore: 1000,
      scoreMatch: true,
      resultHash: 'hash-3',
      verificationAlgorithm: 'DETERMINISTIC_REPLAY_ENGINE',
      verificationVersion: '1.0.0',
      verifier: 'verifier.stellar-splash.org',
      verifiedAt: 2110,
      status: 'VERIFIED',
    },
  ];

  it('ranks players deterministically by primary score and secondary accuracy tie-breaker', () => {
    const entries = RankingService.calculateRankings(mockVerifications, mockResults);

    expect(entries.length).toBe(3);

    // Rank 1: Player 2 (Score: 1200)
    expect(entries[0].playerWallet).toBe('GA_PLAYER_2');
    expect(entries[0].rank).toBe(1);
    expect(entries[0].score).toBe(1200);

    // Rank 2: Player 3 (Score: 1000, Accuracy: 98% > Player 1's 90%)
    expect(entries[1].playerWallet).toBe('GA_PLAYER_3');
    expect(entries[1].rank).toBe(2);
    expect(entries[1].accuracy).toBe(98);

    // Rank 3: Player 1 (Score: 1000, Accuracy: 90%)
    expect(entries[2].playerWallet).toBe('GA_PLAYER_1');
    expect(entries[2].rank).toBe(3);
  });

  it('strictly excludes unverified matches from ranking calculations', () => {
    const entries = RankingService.calculateRankings(mockVerifications, mockResults);

    // Player 4 submitted score 5000, but has NO verification record
    const hasUnverified = entries.some((e) => e.playerWallet === 'GA_PLAYER_4');
    expect(hasUnverified).toBe(false);
  });

  it('generates a deterministic 32-byte cryptographic ranking hash', () => {
    const entries = RankingService.calculateRankings(mockVerifications, mockResults);
    const hash1 = RankingService.generateRankingHash(tournamentId, 1, entries);
    const hash2 = RankingService.generateRankingHash(tournamentId, 1, entries);

    expect(hash1).toBe(hash2);
    expect(hash1.length).toBe(64);

    const bytes = RankingService.generateRankingHashBytes(tournamentId, 1, entries);
    expect(bytes).toBeInstanceOf(Buffer);
    expect(bytes.length).toBe(32);
    expect(bytes.toString('hex')).toBe(hash1);

    expect(RankingService.verifyRankingHash(hash1, tournamentId, 1, entries)).toBe(true);
    expect(RankingService.verifyRankingHash('deadbeef'.repeat(8), tournamentId, 1, entries)).toBe(
      false,
    );
  });
});
```
