`src/tests/rate-limiter.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { SlidingWindowRateLimiter } from '../middleware/rate-limiter.middleware';
import { Request, Response } from 'express';

describe('SlidingWindowRateLimiter Middleware', () => {
  let limiter: SlidingWindowRateLimiter;

  beforeEach(() => {
    limiter = new SlidingWindowRateLimiter({
      windowMs: 1000,
      maxRequests: 3,
      message: 'Test rate limit exceeded',
    });
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    limiter.destroy();
  });

  const createMockReqRes = (ip = '192.168.1.100') => {
    const req = {
      ip,
      headers: {},
      socket: { remoteAddress: ip },
    } as unknown as Request;

    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    let statusCode = 200;
    let jsonBody: any = null;

    const res = {
      setHeader: vi.fn((key: string, val: string) => {
        headers[key] = val;
      }),
      status: vi.fn((code: number) => {
        statusCode = code;
        return res;
      }),
      json: vi.fn((body: any) => {
        jsonBody = body;
        return res;
      }),
    } as unknown as Response;

    return { req, res, headers, getStatusCode: () => statusCode, getJsonBody: () => jsonBody };
  };

  it('allows requests below the threshold and sets RateLimit headers', () => {
    const middleware = limiter.middleware();
    const nextMock = vi.fn();

    const { req, res, headers } = createMockReqRes();
    middleware(req, res, nextMock);

    expect(nextMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(headers['RateLimit-Limit']).toBe('3');
    expect(headers['RateLimit-Remaining']).toBe('2');
  });

  it('blocks excess requests with HTTP 429 when maxRequests is reached', () => {
    const middleware = limiter.middleware();
    const nextMock = vi.fn();

    // Send 3 allowed requests
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const { req, res } = createMockReqRes();
      middleware(req, res, nextMock);
    }
    expect(nextMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);

    // 4th request must be rate limited
    const { req, res, getStatusCode, getJsonBody, headers } = createMockReqRes();
    middleware(req, res, nextMock);

    expect(nextMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3); // Not called again
    expect(getStatusCode()).toBe(429);
    expect(getJsonBody().error).toBe('RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED');
    expect(getJsonBody().message).toBe('Test rate limit exceeded');
    expect(headers['Retry-After']).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('differentiates client rate limit windows by IP address', () => {
    const middleware = limiter.middleware();
    const nextMock = vi.fn();

    // Exhaust client A
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const { req, res } = createMockReqRes('10.0.0.1');
      middleware(req, res, nextMock);
    }

    // Client B should still be allowed
    const { req, res, getStatusCode } = createMockReqRes('10.0.0.2');
    middleware(req, res, nextMock);

    expect(getStatusCode()).toBe(200);
    expect(nextMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4);
  });

  it('exposes getStatus and sets X-RateLimit headers', () => {
    const middleware = limiter.middleware();
    const nextMock = vi.fn();

    const { req, res, headers } = createMockReqRes('192.168.1.50');
    middleware(req, res, nextMock);

    expect(headers['X-RateLimit-Limit']).toBe('3');
    expect(headers['X-RateLimit-Remaining']).toBe('2');
    expect(headers['X-RateLimit-Reset']).toBeDefined();

    const status = limiter.getStatus('192.168.1.50');
    expect(status.limit).toBe(3);
    expect(status.remaining).toBe(2);
    expect(status.reset).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});
```

`src/tests/replay.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { ReplayService } from '../services/replay.service';
import { AttestationService } from '../services/attestation.service';
import { CanonicalService } from '../services/canonical.service';
import { MatchSession, GameResult, GameEvent } from '../types';

describe('Deterministic Replay & Verification Engine', () => {
  const session: MatchSession = {
    matchId: 'match-replay-001',
    tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
    playerWallet: 'GCKAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2GB6V2A73VU',
    gameId: 'splash-rush',
    gameVersion: '1.0.0',
    sessionToken: 'session-tok-123',
    startTime: 1000000,
    endTime: 1045000,
    status: 'COMPLETED',
  };

  const validEvents: GameEvent[] = [
    { seq: 1, timestamp: 1005000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
    { seq: 2, timestamp: 1010000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 150, combo: 2, multiplier: 2 },
    { seq: 3, timestamp: 1015000, type: 'COMBO_STREAK', points: 50, combo: 3, multiplier: 2 },
    { seq: 4, timestamp: 1020000, type: 'HAZARD_HIT', points: 50, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 },
  ];

  // Calculation:
  // Base: 100 + 150 = 250
  // ComboBonus: Event 2 (combo 2 => +10) + Event 3 (streak => +50) = 60
  // MultiplierBonus: Event 2 (mult 2 => +150) = 150
  // Penalties: Event 4 => 50
  // Expected = 250 + 60 + 150 - 50 = 410

  const validResult: GameResult = {
    matchId: session.matchId,
    tournamentId: session.tournamentId,
    playerWallet: session.playerWallet,
    gameVersion: '1.0.0',
    startTime: session.startTime,
    endTime: session.endTime!,
    durationMs: 45000,
    eventsCount: validEvents.length,
    baseScore: 250,
    comboBonus: 60,
    multiplierBonus: 150,
    penalties: 50,
    finalScore: 410,
    accuracy: 66,
    maxCombo: 3,
    resultHash: '',
    submittedAt: 1046000,
    verified: false,
  };

  it('successfully verifies legitimate client score through independent replay', () => {
    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(session, validResult, validEvents);

    expect(verification.status).toBe('VERIFIED');
    expect(verification.scoreMatch).toBe(true);
    expect(verification.replayedScore).toBe(410);
    expect(verification.submittedScore).toBe(410);
    expect(verification.resultHash).toBeDefined();
    expect(verification.resultHash.length).toBe(64);
  });

  it('rejects tampered or forged client scores (untrusted input principle)', () => {
    const tamperedResult = { ...validResult, finalScore: 9999 }; // Client lied about score
    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(session, tamperedResult, validEvents);

    expect(verification.status).toBe('VERIFICATION_FAILED');
    expect(verification.scoreMatch).toBe(false);
    expect(verification.replayedScore).toBe(410);
    expect(verification.submittedScore).toBe(9999);
    expect(verification.rejectionReason).toContain('Score mismatch');
  });

  it('rejects non-monotonic or disordered event sequences', () => {
    const corruptedEvents: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 2, timestamp: 1010000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 150, combo: 2, multiplier: 2 },
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1005000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
    ];

    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(session, validResult, corruptedEvents);

    expect(verification.status).toBe('REJECTED');
    expect(verification.rejectionReason).toContain(
      'Sequence numbering must be strictly monotonically increasing',
    );
  });

  it('rejects events with backwards timestamps', () => {
    const backwardsEvents: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1010000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
      { seq: 2, timestamp: 1005000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 150, combo: 2, multiplier: 2 },
    ];

    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(session, validResult, backwardsEvents);

    expect(verification.status).toBe('REJECTED');
    expect(verification.rejectionReason).toContain('Non-monotonic event timestamp');
  });

  it('rejects games played on mismatched or unsupported versions', () => {
    const staleSession = { ...session, gameVersion: '0.9.0-alpha' };
    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(staleSession, validResult, validEvents);

    expect(verification.status).toBe('REJECTED');
    expect(verification.rejectionReason).toContain('Unsupported or mismatched game version');
  });

  it('creates verifiable cryptographic attestations bound to the canonical result', () => {
    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(session, validResult, validEvents);
    const attestation = AttestationService.createAttestation(verification);

    expect(attestation.resultHash).toBe(verification.resultHash);
    expect(attestation.finalScore).toBe(410);
    expect(attestation.attestationDigest).toBeDefined();

    const isValid = AttestationService.verifyAttestation(attestation, verification);
    expect(isValid).toBe(true);
  });

  it('ensures canonical serialization produces deterministic hashes regardless of object key order', () => {
    const objA = { z: 1, a: 'test', m: { b: 2, a: 1 } };
    const objB = { a: 'test', m: { a: 1, b: 2 }, z: 1 };

    const normA = CanonicalService.canonicalSerialize(objA);
    const normB = CanonicalService.canonicalSerialize(objB);

    expect(normA).toBe(normB);
    expect(CanonicalService.sha256Hex(normA)).toBe(CanonicalService.sha256Hex(normB));
  });

  it('detects and rejects inhuman click velocity (autoclicker / macro bots)', () => {
    const macroEvents: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1005000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
      // 10ms interval = 100 clicks/sec -> bot signature!
      { seq: 2, timestamp: 1005010, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 },
    ];

    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(
      session,
      { ...validResult, finalScore: 200 },
      macroEvents,
    );
    expect(verification.status).toBe('REJECTED');
    expect(verification.rejectionReason).toContain('INHUMAN_CLICK_VELOCITY');
  });

  it('detects and rejects out-of-bounds target hit coordinates', () => {
    const outOfBoundsEvents: GameEvent[] = [
      {
        seq: 1,
        timestamp: 1005000,
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 100,
        combo: 1,
        multiplier: 1,
        data: {
          clickX: 100,
          clickY: 100,
          targetX: 500, // 400px away!
          targetY: 500,
          targetRadius: 40,
        },
      },
    ];

    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(
      session,
      { ...validResult, finalScore: 100 },
      outOfBoundsEvents,
    );
    expect(verification.status).toBe('REJECTED');
    expect(verification.rejectionReason).toContain('GEOMETRIC_OUT_OF_BOUNDS');
  });

  it('records structured anti-cheat fraud detection logs when violations occur', () => {
    const invalidVersionSession: MatchSession = {
      ...session,
      matchId: 'match-fraud-test',
      gameVersion: '0.0.1-hacked',
    };

    ReplayService.verifyMatch(invalidVersionSession, validResult, validEvents);

    const fraudLogs = ReplayService.getFraudAuditLogs({ tournamentId: session.tournamentId });
    expect(fraudLogs.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const lastFraud = fraudLogs[fraudLogs.length - 1];
    expect(lastFraud.eventType).toBe('ANTI_CHEAT_FRAUD_FLAG');
    expect((lastFraud.details as any).reasonCode).toBe('UNSUPPORTED_GAME_VERSION');
  });

  it('rejects truncated or speed-hacked matches deviating by > 2000ms from 45s round duration', () => {
    // 20s match instead of 45s
    const truncatedResult = {
      ...validResult,
      endTime: session.startTime + 20000,
      durationMs: 20000,
    };

    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(session, truncatedResult, validEvents);
    expect(verification.status).toBe('REJECTED');
    expect(verification.rejectionReason).toContain('INVALID_MATCH_DURATION');
  });

  it('rejects extended or frozen timer matches exceeding duration tolerance', () => {
    // 60s match instead of 45s
    const extendedResult = {
      ...validResult,
      endTime: session.startTime + 60000,
      durationMs: 60000,
    };

    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(session, extendedResult, validEvents);
    expect(verification.status).toBe('REJECTED');
    expect(verification.rejectionReason).toContain('INVALID_MATCH_DURATION');
  });

  it('accepts legitimate match duration within +/- 2000ms tolerance', () => {
    // 45.8s match (network delay)
    const tolerantResult = {
      ...validResult,
      endTime: session.startTime + 45800,
      durationMs: 45800,
    };

    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatch(session, tolerantResult, validEvents);
    expect(verification.status).toBe('VERIFIED');
  });
});
```

`src/tests/request-id.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '../app';

describe('Request ID Tracing Middleware', () => {
  const app = createApp();

  it('generates a new UUIDv4 X-Request-Id header when none is provided', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/health');

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const reqId = res.headers['x-request-id'];
    expect(reqId).toBeDefined();
    // Validate UUID format: 8-4-4-4-12 hex characters
    expect(reqId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i);
  });

  it('preserves client-provided X-Request-Id header for distributed request correlation', async () => {
    const customId = 'client-trace-abc-123-xyz';
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/health').set('X-Request-Id', customId);

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers['x-request-id']).toBe(customId);
  });
});
```

`src/tests/scoring.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { ScoringService } from '../services/scoring.service';
import { GameEvent, MatchSession } from '../types';

describe('ScoringService', () => {
  it('deterministically calculates score using integer arithmetic', () => {
    const events: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
      { seq: 2, timestamp: 1500, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 150, combo: 2, multiplier: 2 },
      { seq: 3, timestamp: 2000, type: 'HAZARD_HIT', points: 50, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 },
      { seq: 4, timestamp: 2500, type: 'COMBO_STREAK', points: 30, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 },
      { seq: 5, timestamp: 3000, type: 'MULTIPLIER_UP', points: 0, combo: 0, multiplier: 3 },
    ];

    const breakdown = ScoringService.calculateScore(events);

    // Event 1: TARGET_HIT: points 100, combo 1 (no combo bonus), mult 1 (no mult bonus)
    // Event 2: TARGET_HIT: points 150, combo 2 (+10 combo bonus), mult 2 (+150 mult bonus)
    // Event 3: HAZARD_HIT: penalties 50
    // Event 4: COMBO_STREAK: comboBonus +30
    // Event 5: MULTIPLIER_UP: multiplierBonus +30 (3 * 10)
    // BaseScore = 100 + 150 = 250
    // ComboBonus = 10 + 30 = 40
    // MultiplierBonus = 150 + 30 = 180
    // Penalties = 50
    // Total = 250 + 40 + 180 - 50 = 420
    expect(breakdown.baseScore).toBe(250);
    expect(breakdown.comboBonus).toBe(40);
    expect(breakdown.multiplierBonus).toBe(180);
    expect(breakdown.penalties).toBe(50);
    expect(breakdown.finalScore).toBe(420);
    expect(breakdown.maxCombo).toBe(2);
    // Total target attempts = 2 target hits + 1 hazard = 3 attempts. Target hits = 2. Accuracy = floor(2 * 100 / 3) = 66
    expect(breakdown.accuracy).toBe(66);
  });

  it('guarantees deterministic result hashes for identical match runs', () => {
    const session: MatchSession = {
      matchId: 'match-test-123',
      tournamentId: 'tourn-splash-001',
      playerWallet: 'GCKAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2GB6V2A73VU',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      sessionToken: 'token-abc',
      startTime: 1000,
      status: 'STARTED',
    };

    const events: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1200, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
      { seq: 2, timestamp: 1800, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 200, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 },
    ];

    const result1 = ScoringService.buildGameResult(session, events, 5000);
    const result2 = ScoringService.buildGameResult(session, events, 5000);

    expect(result1.resultHash).toBe(result2.resultHash);
    expect(result1.resultHash.length).toBe(64); // 256-bit SHA-256 hex string
    expect(result1.finalScore).toBe(310); // 100 + 200 + (2 * 5) = 310
  });

  it('calculates deterministic 10,000 bps prize shares and awards fractional dust to Rank 1', () => {
    const allocations = [
      { rank: 1, basisPoints: 5000 },
      { rank: 2, basisPoints: 3000 },
      { rank: 3, basisPoints: 2000 },
    ];

    // Total: 10,001 Stroops -> Dust = 1 Stroop
    const totalPrize = 10_001n;
    const shares = ScoringService.calculatePrizePoolBpsShares(totalPrize, allocations);

    expect(shares.length).toBe(3);
    expect(shares[0].rank).toBe(1);
    expect(shares[0].shareStroops).toBe(5001n);
    expect(shares[0].isDustRecipient).toBe(true);

    expect(shares[1].rank).toBe(2);
    expect(shares[1].shareStroops).toBe(3000n);
    expect(shares[1].isDustRecipient).toBe(false);

    expect(shares[2].rank).toBe(3);
    expect(shares[2].shareStroops).toBe(2000n);

    // Sum invariant
    const sum = shares.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr.shareStroops, 0n);
    expect(sum).toBe(totalPrize);
  });

  it('throws an error when basis points do not sum to 10,000', () => {
    const invalid = [
      { rank: 1, basisPoints: 5000 },
      { rank: 2, basisPoints: 4999 },
    ];
    expect(() => ScoringService.calculatePrizePoolBpsShares(100_000n, invalid)).toThrow(
      'INVALID_BPS_SUM',
    );
  });
});

```

`src/tests/settlement.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '../app';
import { StoreService } from '../services/store.service';
import { SettlementService } from '../services/settlement.service';
import { CanonicalService } from '../services/canonical.service';
import { AgreementService } from '../services/agreement.service';
import { Tournament, FinalRankingRecord, PrizeAgreement } from '../types';

describe('Settlement and Reconciliation Engine', () => {
  const app = createApp();

  const testTournamentId = 'tourn-settle-test';
  const player1 = 'GCKAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2GB6V2A73VU';
  const player2 = 'GD4KQL3ZPL2GB6V2A73VUX6KAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV';
  const player3 = 'GBOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2GB6V2A73VUX6KAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V';
  const creator = 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB';

  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.resetTournamentData(testTournamentId);

    // 1. Setup test tournament
    const tournament: Tournament = {
      id: testTournamentId,
      name: 'Championship Finals',
      description: 'Verifiable settlement test tournament',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      creatorWallet: creator,
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 100,
      vaultAddress: 'GB6V2A73VUX6KAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2',
      maxParticipants: 16,
      currentParticipants: 3,
      startTime: Date.now() - 3600_000,
      endTime: Date.now() - 1000,
      state: 'FINALIZED',
      fundingTxHash: 'a1b2c3d4e5f60718293a4b5c6d7e8f9a0b1c2d3e4f5a6b7c8d9e0f1a2b3c4d5e',
      fundingVerifiedAt: Date.now() - 3500_000,
      activeAgreementVersion: 1,
      createdAt: Date.now() - 3600_000,
      updatedAt: Date.now() - 1000,
    };
    StoreService.saveTournament(tournament);

    // 2. Setup verified results
    const results = [
      { matchId: 'm1', score: 3000, player: player1 },
      { matchId: 'm2', score: 2500, player: player2 },
      { matchId: 'm3', score: 1800, player: player3 },
    ];

    for (const r of results) {
      StoreService.saveGameResult({
        matchId: r.matchId,
        tournamentId: testTournamentId,
        playerWallet: r.player,
        gameVersion: '1.0.0',
        startTime: Date.now() - 2000,
        endTime: Date.now() - 1000,
        durationMs: 45000,
        eventsCount: 30,
        baseScore: r.score,
        comboBonus: 0,
        multiplierBonus: 0,
        penalties: 0,
        finalScore: r.score,
        accuracy: 95,
        maxCombo: 10,
        resultHash: `hash-${r.matchId}`,
        submittedAt: Date.now() - 1000,
        verified: true,
      });

      StoreService.saveVerification({
        verificationId: `v-${r.matchId}`,
        matchId: r.matchId,
        tournamentId: testTournamentId,
        playerWallet: r.player,
        gameVersion: '1.0.0',
        submittedScore: r.score,
        replayedScore: r.score,
        scoreMatch: true,
        resultHash: `hash-${r.matchId}`,
        verificationAlgorithm: 'DETERMINISTIC_REPLAY_ENGINE',
        verificationVersion: '1.0.0',
        verifier: 'verifier.stellar-splash.org',
        verifiedAt: Date.now() - 500,
        status: 'VERIFIED',
      });
    }

    // 3. Setup Final Ranking
    const rankingEntries = [
      {
        rank: 1,
        playerWallet: player1,
        score: 3000,
        accuracy: 95,
        completedAt: Date.now() - 1000,
        matchId: 'm1',
        resultHash: 'hash-m1',
      },
      {
        rank: 2,
        playerWallet: player2,
        score: 2500,
        accuracy: 90,
        completedAt: Date.now() - 1000,
        matchId: 'm2',
        resultHash: 'hash-m2',
      },
      {
        rank: 3,
        playerWallet: player3,
        score: 1800,
        accuracy: 85,
        completedAt: Date.now() - 1000,
        matchId: 'm3',
        resultHash: 'hash-m3',
      },
    ];

    const rankingHash = CanonicalService.computeRankingHash(testTournamentId, 1, rankingEntries);
    const ranking: FinalRankingRecord = {
      tournamentId: testTournamentId,
      rankingVersion: 1,
      entries: rankingEntries,
      rankingHash,
      finalizedAt: Date.now() - 500,
      finalizedBy: creator,
    };
    StoreService.saveRanking(ranking);

    // 4. Setup Locked Prize Agreement
    const agreement = AgreementService.createAgreement({
      tournamentId: testTournamentId,
      version: 1,
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 100,
      allocationRules: [
        { rank: 1, basisPoints: 5000, label: '1st Place (50%)' },
        { rank: 2, basisPoints: 3000, label: '2nd Place (30%)' },
        { rank: 3, basisPoints: 2000, label: '3rd Place (20%)' },
      ],
      createdBy: creator,
    });
    agreement.status = 'LOCKED';
    agreement.approvedBy = creator;
    agreement.approvedAt = Date.now() - 1000;
    agreement.lockedAt = Date.now() - 1000;
    StoreService.saveAgreement(agreement);

    // 5. Setup Prize Pool Funding
    StoreService.recordFunding({
      tournamentId: testTournamentId,
      txHash: tournament.fundingTxHash!,
      funderWallet: creator,
      destinationVault: tournament.vaultAddress,
      asset: 'USDC',
      amount: 100,
      ledger: 4893100,
      verifiedAt: Date.now() - 3500_000,
      explorerUrl: `https://stellar.expert/explorer/testnet/tx/${tournament.fundingTxHash}`,
      status: 'CONFIRMED',
    });
  });

  describe('Eligibility Pipeline', () => {
    it('approves a tournament with locked agreement and finalized ranking', () => {
      const eligibility = SettlementService.checkEligibility(testTournamentId);
      expect(eligibility.eligible).toBe(true);
      expect(eligibility.reasons.length).toBe(0);
      expect(eligibility.agreement?.status).toBe('LOCKED');
      expect(eligibility.ranking?.entries.length).toBe(3);
    });

    it('rejects settlement when prize agreement is not LOCKED', () => {
      const agreement = StoreService.getActiveAgreement(testTournamentId)!;
      // Mutate status to DRAFT
      const unlockedAgreement: PrizeAgreement = {
        ...agreement,
        status: 'DRAFT',
      };
      // Force save by changing status
      const list = StoreService.getAgreementsForTournament(testTournamentId);
      list[0] = unlockedAgreement;

      const eligibility = SettlementService.checkEligibility(testTournamentId);
      expect(eligibility.eligible).toBe(false);
      expect(eligibility.reasons.some((r) => r.includes('AGREEMENT_NOT_LOCKED'))).toBe(true);
    });

    it('rejects settlement if ranking hash does not match canonical calculation', () => {
      const ranking = StoreService.getRanking(testTournamentId)!;
      StoreService.saveRanking({
        ...ranking,
        rankingHash: 'tampered-fake-hash-00000000000000000000000000000000000000000000',
      });

      const eligibility = SettlementService.checkEligibility(testTournamentId);
      expect(eligibility.eligible).toBe(false);
      expect(eligibility.reasons.some((r) => r.includes('RANKING_HASH_MISMATCH'))).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe('Deterministic Allocation & Accounting Invariants', () => {
    it('accurately divides 100 USDC with 5000, 3000, 2000 basis points', () => {
      const { snapshot, settlementRecord } = SettlementService.createSnapshot(testTournamentId);

      expect(snapshot.recipients.length).toBe(3);
      expect(snapshot.recipients[0].amount).toBe(50);
      expect(snapshot.recipients[0].playerWallet).toBe(player1);
      expect(snapshot.recipients[1].amount).toBe(30);
      expect(snapshot.recipients[1].playerWallet).toBe(player2);
      expect(snapshot.recipients[2].amount).toBe(20);
      expect(snapshot.recipients[2].playerWallet).toBe(player3);

      // Invariant: sum of allocations equals gross pool
      const sum = snapshot.recipients.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.amount, 0);
      expect(sum).toBe(100);
      expect(settlementRecord.grossPrizePool).toBe(100);
    });

    it('allocates residual dust deterministically to Rank 1', () => {
      const payouts = AgreementService.calculateSettlementAllocations(10001, [
        { rank: 1, basisPoints: 5000, label: '1st' },
        { rank: 2, basisPoints: 3000, label: '2nd' },
        { rank: 3, basisPoints: 2000, label: '3rd' },
      ]);

      // 10001 * 0.50 = 5000
      // 10001 * 0.30 = 3000
      // 10001 * 0.20 = 2000
      // sum = 10000 -> dust = 1 -> awarded to Rank 1
      expect(payouts[0].amount).toBe(5001);
      expect(payouts[0].isResidualRecipient).toBe(true);
      expect(payouts[1].amount).toBe(3000);
      expect(payouts[2].amount).toBe(2000);
      expect(payouts.reduce((a, b) => a + b.amount, 0)).toBe(10001);
    });

    it('produces identical deterministic settlement hash for same inputs', () => {
      const { settlementRecord: s1 } = SettlementService.createSnapshot(testTournamentId);
      const hash1 = CanonicalService.computeSettlementHash({
        tournamentId: s1.tournamentId,
        settlementId: s1.settlementId,
        agreementHash: s1.agreementHash,
        rankingHash: s1.rankingHash,
        prizeAsset: s1.prizeAsset,
        recipients: s1.recipients.map((r) => ({
          rank: r.rank,
          playerWallet: r.playerWallet,
          amount: r.amount,
          allocationBps: r.allocationBps,
        })),
      });

      const hash2 = CanonicalService.computeSettlementHash({
        tournamentId: s1.tournamentId,
        settlementId: s1.settlementId,
        agreementHash: s1.agreementHash,
        rankingHash: s1.rankingHash,
        prizeAsset: s1.prizeAsset,
        recipients: s1.recipients.map((r) => ({
          rank: r.rank,
          playerWallet: r.playerWallet,
          amount: r.amount,
          allocationBps: r.allocationBps,
        })),
      });

      expect(hash1).toBe(hash2);
      expect(hash1.length).toBe(64);
    });
  });

  describe('Execution & Independent Reconciliation', () => {
    it('authorizes, executes, and reconciles settlement', async () => {
      // 1. Authorize
      const authorized = SettlementService.authorizeSettlement({
        tournamentId: testTournamentId,
        authorizedBy: creator,
      });
      expect(authorized.status).toBe('AUTHORIZED');
      expect(authorized.authorizedBy).toBe(creator);

      // 2. Execute
      const { settlement, transactions } = await SettlementService.executeSettlement({
        tournamentId: testTournamentId,
      });

      expect(transactions.length).toBe(3);
      expect(transactions.every((t) => t.status === 'CONFIRMED')).toBe(true);
      expect(transactions.every((t) => t.txHash.length === 64)).toBe(true);
      expect(transactions.every((t) => t.explorerUrl.includes('stellar.expert'))).toBe(true);
      expect(settlement.status).toBe('RECONCILED'); // Auto-reconciled on success

      // 3. Reconcile explicitly
      const rec = SettlementService.reconcileSettlement(settlement.settlementId);
      expect(rec.status).toBe('RECONCILED');
      expect(rec.matchedTransactions).toBe(3);
      expect(rec.mismatchedTransactions).toBe(0);
      expect(rec.expectedTotal).toBe(100);
      expect(rec.observedTotal).toBe(100);
    });

    it('detects reconciliation mismatch if an on-chain amount differs', async () => {
      const { settlement } = await SettlementService.executeSettlement({
        tournamentId: testTournamentId,
      });

      // Tamper with a transaction amount
      const txs = StoreService.getSettlementTransactions(settlement.settlementId);
      txs[0].actualAmount = 45; // expected 50

      const rec = SettlementService.reconcileSettlement(settlement.settlementId);
      expect(rec.status).toBe('MISMATCH');
      expect(rec.mismatchedTransactions).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(rec.discrepancies.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });
  });

  describe('REST API Endpoints', () => {
    it('GET /api/settlements/:tournamentId/eligibility returns eligibility status', async () => {
      const res = await request(app).get(`/api/settlements/${testTournamentId}/eligibility`);
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
      expect(res.body.eligibility.eligible).toBe(true);
    });

    it('GET /api/settlements/:tournamentId/preview returns calculated allocations', async () => {
      const res = await request(app).get(`/api/settlements/${testTournamentId}/preview`);
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
      expect(res.body.settlement.recipients.length).toBe(3);
      expect(res.body.settlement.grossPrizePool).toBe(100);
    });

    it('POST /api/settlements/:tournamentId/authorize authorizes settlement', async () => {
      const res = await request(app)
        .post(`/api/settlements/${testTournamentId}/authorize`)
        .send({ authorizedBy: creator });
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
      expect(res.body.settlement.status).toBe('AUTHORIZED');
    });

    it('POST /api/settlements/:tournamentId/execute processes payouts', async () => {
      const res = await request(app).post(`/api/settlements/${testTournamentId}/execute`).send({});
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
      expect(res.body.settlement.status).toBe('RECONCILED');
      expect(res.body.transactions.length).toBe(3);
    });

    it('GET /api/settlements/player/:playerWallet returns player payout history', async () => {
      // Execute first
      await request(app).post(`/api/settlements/${testTournamentId}/execute`).send({});

      const res = await request(app).get(`/api/settlements/player/${player1}`);
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
      expect(res.body.count).toBe(1);
      expect(res.body.payouts[0].recipient.amount).toBe(50);
      expect(res.body.payouts[0].recipient.rank).toBe(1);
    });
  });
});

```

`src/tests/shutdown.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ShutdownService } from '../services/shutdown.service';
import { Server } from 'http';

describe('ShutdownService - Graceful Process Termination', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    ShutdownService.clearHooks();
  });

  it('registers and executes custom shutdown hooks sequentially', async () => {
    const executed: string[] = [];

    ShutdownService.registerHook('cleanup-cache', () => {
      executed.push('cache');
    });

    ShutdownService.registerHook('flush-db', async () => {
      executed.push('db');
    });

    await ShutdownService.executeShutdown(0);

    expect(executed).toEqual(['cache', 'db']);
  });

  it('closes HTTP server gracefully during shutdown', async () => {
    let serverClosed = false;
    const mockServer = {
      close: vi.fn((cb: () => void) => {
        serverClosed = true;
        cb();
      }),
    } as unknown as Server;

    ShutdownService.init(mockServer);
    await ShutdownService.executeShutdown(0);

    expect(serverClosed).toBe(true);
    expect(mockServer.close).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('continues executing subsequent hooks even if one throws an error', async () => {
    const executed: string[] = [];

    ShutdownService.registerHook('failing-hook', () => {
      throw new Error('Explosive hook failure');
    });

    ShutdownService.registerHook('healthy-hook', () => {
      executed.push('healthy');
    });

    await ShutdownService.executeShutdown(0);

    expect(executed).toContain('healthy');
  });
});
```

`src/tests/simulate-settlement.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
// @ts-expect-error cjs script
import { simulateSettlement } from '../../scripts/simulate-settlement-flow.cjs';

describe('Settlement Flow Simulator', () => {
  it('correctly calculates 50/30/20 split without deficit', () => {
    const res = simulateSettlement(101, 1000);
    expect(res.tournamentId).toBe(101);
    expect(res.totalPrizeXlm).toBe(1000);
    expect(res.totalDisbursed).toBe(1000);
    expect(res.remainder).toBe(0);
  });

  it('preserves dust remainder on indivisible pools', () => {
    const res = simulateSettlement(102, 1001);
    expect(res.totalDisbursed + res.remainder).toBe(1001);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
// @ts-expect-error cjs script
import { filterContractEvents, WATCH_CONFIG } from '../../scripts/watch-contract-events.cjs';

describe('Contract Event Watcher Filter', () => {
  it('correctly filters recognized contract event topics', () => {
    const rawEvents = [
      { topic: 'player_entered', data: { player: 'GCQ3...' } },
      { topic: 'unknown_event', data: {} },
      { topic: 'tournament_settled', data: { tournamentId: 101 } },
    ];

    const filtered = filterContractEvents(rawEvents);
    expect(filtered.length).toBe(2);
    expect(filtered[0].topic).toBe('player_entered');
    expect(filtered[1].topic).toBe('tournament_settled');
  });

  it('contains expected target contract address', () => {
    expect(WATCH_CONFIG.contractId).toBe(
      'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
    );
  });
});
```

`src/types/leaderboard-relayer.ts`:

```ts
export interface LeaderboardEntry {

  playerWallet: string;

  winRateBps: number;
  lastActiveTimestamp: number;
```

`test/account-synchronizer.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '../src/app';
import {
  AccountSynchronizerService,
  VaultBalanceInfo,
} from '../src/services/account-synchronizer.service';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { Tournament } from '../src/types';

describe('AccountSynchronizerService & Vault Solvency Reconciler', () => {
  const app = createApp();
  const testVault = 'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ';

  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
    AccountSynchronizerService.clearCache();
  });

  it('calculates solvency correctly when on-chain vault balance covers obligations', async () => {
    const mockBalance: VaultBalanceInfo = {
      vaultAddress: testVault,
      nativeXLM: 2000,
      subentryCount: 2,
      baseReserveXLM: 2.0,
      spendableXLM: 1998,
      assetBalances: { USDC: 1000 },
      fetchedAt: Date.now(),
    };
    AccountSynchronizerService.setMockBalance(mockBalance);

    const tourn1: Tournament = {
      id: 'tourn-solvency-1',
      name: 'Aqua Cup',
      description: 'Solvency test tournament',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      creatorWallet: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
      prizeAsset: 'XLM',
      prizePoolAmount: 500,
      vaultAddress: testVault,
      maxParticipants: 10,
      currentParticipants: 2,
      startTime: Date.now() - 10000,
      endTime: Date.now() + 100000,
      state: 'OPEN',
      createdAt: Date.now() - 10000,
      updatedAt: Date.now() - 10000,
    };
    StoreService.saveTournament(tourn1);

    const summary = await AccountSynchronizerService.reconcileVaultObligations(testVault);
    expect(summary.vaultAddress).toBe(testVault);
    expect(summary.onChainBalanceXLM).toBe(2000);
    expect(summary.totalEscrowObligationXLM).toBe(500);
    expect(summary.surplusOrDeficitXLM).toBe(1500);
    expect(summary.isSolvent).toBe(true);
    expect(summary.activeTournamentCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it('detects deficit when tournament obligations exceed on-chain vault balance', async () => {
    const mockBalance: VaultBalanceInfo = {
      vaultAddress: testVault,
      nativeXLM: 300,
      subentryCount: 0,
      baseReserveXLM: 1.0,
      spendableXLM: 299,
      assetBalances: {},
      fetchedAt: Date.now(),
    };
    AccountSynchronizerService.setMockBalance(mockBalance);

    const tournOverdraft: Tournament = {
      id: 'tourn-overdraft',
      name: 'Grand Mega Cup',
      description: 'Big tournament',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      creatorWallet: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
      prizeAsset: 'XLM',
      prizePoolAmount: 1000,
      vaultAddress: testVault,
      maxParticipants: 100,
      currentParticipants: 50,
      startTime: Date.now() - 10000,
      endTime: Date.now() + 100000,
      state: 'OPEN',
      createdAt: Date.now() - 10000,
      updatedAt: Date.now() - 10000,
    };
    StoreService.saveTournament(tournOverdraft);

    const summary = await AccountSynchronizerService.reconcileVaultObligations(testVault);
    expect(summary.onChainBalanceXLM).toBe(300);
    expect(summary.totalEscrowObligationXLM).toBe(1000);
    expect(summary.surplusOrDeficitXLM).toBe(-700);
    expect(summary.isSolvent).toBe(false);
  });

  it('exposes GET /api/reconciliation/vault/solvency with HTTP 200 response', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/reconciliation/vault/solvency');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.summary).toBeDefined();
    expect(typeof res.body.summary.isSolvent).toBe('boolean');
    expect(typeof res.body.summary.onChainBalanceXLM).toBe('number');
  });
});
```

`test/anomaly-detector.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { AnomalyDetectorService } from '../src/services/anomaly-detector.service';
import { GameEvent } from '../src/types';

describe('AnomalyDetectorService - Statistical Bot & Macro Detection', () => {
  it('certifies natural human gameplay event stream as clean and trusted', () => {
    const naturalEvents: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1000, type: 'ROUND_STARTED', points: 0, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 },
      {
        seq: 2,
        timestamp: 1250,
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 100,
        combo: 1,
        multiplier: 1,
        data: { x: 120, y: 340 },
      },
      {
        seq: 3,
        timestamp: 1610,
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 150,
        combo: 2,
        multiplier: 1,
        data: { x: 280, y: 190 },
      },
      {
        seq: 4,
        timestamp: 1980,
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 200,
        combo: 3,
        multiplier: 1,
        data: { x: 450, y: 510 },
      },
      {
        seq: 5,
        timestamp: 2450,
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 250,
        combo: 4,
        multiplier: 1,
        data: { x: 310, y: 220 },
      },
      {
        seq: 6,
        timestamp: 2890,
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 300,
        combo: 5,
        multiplier: 1,
        data: { x: 190, y: 440 },
      },
      { seq: 7, timestamp: 3000, type: 'ROUND_ENDED', points: 1000, combo: 5, multiplier: 1 },
    ];

    const result = AnomalyDetectorService.analyzeMatchEvents(naturalEvents);
    expect(result.riskScore).toBeLessThan(30);
    expect(result.isSuspicious).toBe(false);
    expect(result.flags.length).toBe(0);
    expect(AnomalyDetectorService.isTrusted(naturalEvents)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('detects robotic macro with zero-jitter fixed intervals', () => {
    // 10 clicks exactly 50ms apart (synthetic macro)
    const macroEvents: GameEvent[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      macroEvents.push({
        seq: i + 1,
        timestamp: 1000 + i * 50, // Exactly 50ms interval every time
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 100,
        combo: i + 1,
        multiplier: 1,
        data: { x: 100 + i * 10, y: 200 + i * 5 },
      });
    }

    const result = AnomalyDetectorService.analyzeMatchEvents(macroEvents);
    expect(result.metrics.intervalStdDev).toBeLessThan(2);
    expect(result.flags.some((f) => f.includes('SYNTHETIC_INTERVAL_REGULARITY'))).toBe(true);
    expect(result.riskScore).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(50);
    expect(result.isSuspicious).toBe(true);
    expect(AnomalyDetectorService.isTrusted(macroEvents)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('detects inhuman autoclicker bursts under 40ms', () => {
    const burstEvents: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1000, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 10, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
      { seq: 2, timestamp: 1010, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 10, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 }, // 10ms
      { seq: 3, timestamp: 1022, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 10, combo: 3, multiplier: 1 }, // 12ms
      { seq: 4, timestamp: 1035, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 10, combo: 4, multiplier: 1 }, // 13ms
      { seq: 5, timestamp: 1050, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 10, combo: 5, multiplier: 1 }, // 15ms
    ];

    const result = AnomalyDetectorService.analyzeMatchEvents(burstEvents);
    expect(result.metrics.inhumanRapidClicksCount).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
    expect(result.flags.some((f) => f.includes('INHUMAN_BURST_RATE'))).toBe(true);
    expect(result.riskScore).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(40);
  });
});
```

`test/cache.service.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { CacheService } from '../src/services/cache.service';

describe('CacheService - TTL-based LRU Cache', () => {
  it('stores and retrieves cached items with hit/miss statistics', () => {
    const cache = new CacheService<string>(10, 1000);

    expect(cache.get('k1')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(cache.getStats().misses).toBe(1);

    cache.set('k1', 'val1');
    expect(cache.get('k1')).toBe('val1');
    expect(cache.getStats().hits).toBe(1);
    expect(cache.has('k1')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('evicts least recently used items when exceeding capacity', () => {
    const cache = new CacheService<number>(3, 10_000);

    cache.set('a', 1);
    cache.set('b', 2);
    cache.set('c', 3);

    // Access 'a' to make 'b' the least recently used
    expect(cache.get('a')).toBe(1);

    // Insert 'd', which exceeds max size of 3 and should evict 'b'
    cache.set('d', 4);

    expect(cache.get('b')).toBeUndefined(); // evicted
    expect(cache.get('a')).toBe(1);
    expect(cache.get('c')).toBe(3);
    expect(cache.get('d')).toBe(4);
    expect(cache.getStats().evictions).toBe(1);
  });

  it('expires entries after TTL elapsed', async () => {
    const cache = new CacheService<string>(10, 50); // 50ms TTL

    cache.set('quick', 'expire-me');
    expect(cache.get('quick')).toBe('expire-me');

    // Wait 60ms
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 60));

    expect(cache.has('quick')).toBe(false);
    expect(cache.get('quick')).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('computes value with getOrSet only on cache miss', async () => {
    const cache = new CacheService<number>(10, 5000);
    const producer = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(42);

    const res1 = await cache.getOrSet('computed', producer);
    expect(res1).toBe(42);
    expect(producer).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

    // Second call should return cached without calling producer
    const res2 = await cache.getOrSet('computed', producer);
    expect(res2).toBe(42);
    expect(producer).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

```

`test/db-backup.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { DatabaseBackupService } from '../scripts/db-backup';

describe('DatabaseBackupService', () => {
  const testDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '../data/test-backup-suite');
  const srcDir = path.join(testDir, 'src-data');
  const backupDir = path.join(testDir, 'backups');
  const restoreDir = path.join(testDir, 'restored');

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await fs.promises.mkdir(srcDir, { recursive: true });
    await fs.promises.mkdir(backupDir, { recursive: true });
    await fs.promises.mkdir(restoreDir, { recursive: true });

    // Seed test snapshot files
    await fs.promises.writeFile(
      path.join(srcDir, 'tournaments.json'),
      JSON.stringify([{ id: 't1', title: 'Test Cup' }], null, 2),
      'utf-8',
    );
    await fs.promises.writeFile(
      path.join(srcDir, 'players.json'),
      JSON.stringify([{ id: 'p1', handle: 'AstroPlayer' }], null, 2),
      'utf-8',
    );
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    if (fs.existsSync(testDir)) {
      await fs.promises.rm(testDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  it('creates a valid backup bundle with manifest metadata', async () => {
    const { backupFile, manifest } = await DatabaseBackupService.createBackup(srcDir, backupDir);

    expect(fs.existsSync(backupFile)).toBe(true);
    expect(manifest.files.length).toBe(2);
    expect(manifest.totalBytes).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(manifest.version).toBe('1.0.0');
    expect(manifest.files.map((f) => f.fileName)).toContain('tournaments.json');
    expect(manifest.files.map((f) => f.fileName)).toContain('players.json');
  });

  it('verifies valid and corrupted backups correctly', async () => {
    const { backupFile } = await DatabaseBackupService.createBackup(srcDir, backupDir);

    const validResult = await DatabaseBackupService.verifyBackup(backupFile);
    expect(validResult.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(validResult.errors.length).toBe(0);

    // Corrupt the backup file
    const corruptedPath = path.join(backupDir, 'corrupted.json');
    await fs.promises.writeFile(corruptedPath, '{"invalid_json": true,', 'utf-8');

    const invalidResult = await DatabaseBackupService.verifyBackup(corruptedPath);
    expect(invalidResult.valid).toBe(false);
    expect(invalidResult.errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('restores snapshot files from a backup bundle reliably', async () => {
    const { backupFile } = await DatabaseBackupService.createBackup(srcDir, backupDir);

    const { restoredCount } = await DatabaseBackupService.restoreBackup(backupFile, restoreDir);
    expect(restoredCount).toBe(2);

    const restoredTournaments = JSON.parse(
      await fs.promises.readFile(path.join(restoreDir, 'tournaments.json'), 'utf-8'),
    );
    expect(restoredTournaments[0].title).toBe('Test Cup');

    const restoredPlayers = JSON.parse(
      await fs.promises.readFile(path.join(restoreDir, 'players.json'), 'utf-8'),
    );
    expect(restoredPlayers[0].handle).toBe('AstroPlayer');
  });
});

```

`test/grafana-dashboard.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

describe('Grafana Dashboard Specification', () => {
  const dashboardPath = path.resolve(__dirname, '../docs/grafana-dashboard.json');

  it('contains valid Grafana JSON schema with required top-level attributes', () => {
    expect(fs.existsSync(dashboardPath)).toBe(true);

    const raw = fs.readFileSync(dashboardPath, 'utf-8');
    const dashboard = JSON.parse(raw);

    expect(dashboard.title).toContain('Stellar Splash');
    expect(dashboard.uid).toBe('stellar-splash-telemetry');
    expect(dashboard.schemaVersion).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(30);
    expect(Array.isArray(dashboard.panels)).toBe(true);
    expect(dashboard.panels.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5);
  });

  it('maps panels to active Prometheus metric symbols exported by MetricsService', () => {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(dashboardPath, 'utf-8');
    const dashboard = JSON.parse(raw);

    const panelQueries = dashboard.panels.flatMap((p: any) => p.targets.map((t: any) => t.expr));

    const joinedQueries = panelQueries.join(' ');
    expect(joinedQueries).toContain('sse_active_clients_total');
    expect(joinedQueries).toContain('splash_matches_total');
    expect(joinedQueries).toContain('splash_settlements_total');
    expect(joinedQueries).toContain('splash_settlement_latency_seconds');
    expect(joinedQueries).toContain('http_requests_total');
    expect(joinedQueries).toContain('process_heap_bytes');
  });
});
```

`test/integration.e2e.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '../src/app';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { BlockchainService } from '../src/services/blockchain.service';

describe('End-to-End Tournament Lifecycle Integration Test', () => {
  const app = createApp();

  const creatorWallet = 'GBBD47IF6LWK7P7MDEVSCWR7DPUWV3NY3DTQEVFL4NAT4AQH3ZLLFLA5';
  const player1Wallet = 'GCKAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2GB6V2A73VU';
  const player2Wallet = 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB';
  const fakeTxHash = 'abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789';

  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it('successfully executes complete tournament lifecycle from creation through verified settlement', async () => {
    // Mock on-chain transaction verification
    vi.spyOn(BlockchainService, 'verifyFundingTransaction').mockResolvedValue({
      valid: true,
      txHash: fakeTxHash,
      sourceAccount: creatorWallet,
      amount: 500,
      asset: 'USDC',
      successful: true,
    });

    // 1. Create Tournament
    const now = Date.now();
    const createRes = await request(app)
      .post('/api/tournaments')
      .send({
        name: 'Stellar Splash E2E Championship',
        description: 'End-to-end integration test tournament',
        gameId: 'splash-rush',
        creatorWallet,
        prizeAsset: 'USDC',
        prizePoolAmount: 500,
        maxParticipants: 8,
        startTime: now,
        endTime: now + 3600_000,
      });

    expect(createRes.status).toBe(201);
    expect(createRes.body.success).toBe(true);
    const tournamentId = createRes.body.tournament.id;
    expect(tournamentId).toBeDefined();

    // 2. Fund Prize Pool
    const fundRes = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/tournaments/${tournamentId}/prize-pool/funding`)
      .send({
        txHash: fakeTxHash,
        funderWallet: creatorWallet,
      });

    expect(fundRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(fundRes.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(fundRes.body.tournament.state).toBe('OPEN');

    // 3. Register Participants
    const join1 = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/tournaments/${tournamentId}/join`)
      .send({ playerWallet: player1Wallet });
    expect(join1.status).toBe(200);

    const join2 = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/tournaments/${tournamentId}/join`)
      .send({ playerWallet: player2Wallet });
    expect(join2.status).toBe(200);

    // 4. Start Match Session for Player 1
    const matchStart = await request(app).post('/api/games/start').send({
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: player1Wallet,
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
    });
    expect(matchStart.status).toBe(201);
    const { matchId, sessionToken } = matchStart.body.match;
    expect(matchId).toBeDefined();
    expect(sessionToken).toBeDefined();

    // 5. Complete Match Session with Valid Event Stream
    const matchStartTime = Date.now();
    const matchEndTime = matchStartTime + 45000;
    const events = [
      {
        seq: 1,
        timestamp: matchStartTime + 5000,
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 100,
        combo: 1,
        multiplier: 1,
      },
      {
        seq: 2,
        timestamp: matchStartTime + 10000,
        type: 'TARGET_HIT',
        points: 150,
        combo: 2,
        multiplier: 2,
      },
    ];

    const matchComplete = await request(app).post('/api/games/complete').send({
      matchId,
      sessionToken,
      endTime: matchEndTime,
      events,
    });
    expect(matchComplete.status).toBe(200);
    expect(matchComplete.body.result.finalScore).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // 6. Verify Match Telemetry
    const verifyRes = await request(app).post(`/api/verification/match/${matchId}`);
    expect(verifyRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(verifyRes.body.verification.status).toBe('VERIFIED');

    // 7. Create, Approve, and Lock Prize Agreement (Winner Take All)
    const createAgr = await request(app)
      .post('/api/agreements')
      .send({
        tournamentId,
        version: 1,
        prizeAsset: 'USDC',
        prizePoolAmount: 500,
        allocationRules: [{ rank: 1, basisPoints: 10000, label: 'Winner Take All' }],
        createdBy: creatorWallet,
      });
    expect(createAgr.status).toBe(201);

    const approveAgr = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/agreements/${tournamentId}/1/approve`)
      .send({ approverWallet: creatorWallet });
    expect(approveAgr.status).toBe(200);

    const lockAgr = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/agreements/${tournamentId}/1/lock`)
      .send({ actorWallet: creatorWallet });
    expect(lockAgr.status).toBe(200);
    expect(lockAgr.body.agreement.status).toBe('LOCKED');

    // 8. Finalize Canonical Ranking
    const finalizeRes = await request(app).post(`/api/rankings/${tournamentId}/finalize`).send({
      finalizedBy: creatorWallet,
    });
    expect(finalizeRes.status).toBe(201);
    expect(finalizeRes.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(finalizeRes.body.ranking.rankingHash).toBeDefined();

    // 9. Evaluate Settlement Eligibility
    const eligRes = await request(app).get(`/api/settlements/${tournamentId}/eligibility`);
    expect(eligRes.status).toBe(200);

    // 10. Authorize Settlement
    const authRes = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/settlements/${tournamentId}/authorize`)
      .send({ authorizedBy: creatorWallet });
    expect(authRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(authRes.body.settlement.status).toBe('AUTHORIZED');
    expect(authRes.body.settlement.grossPrizePool).toBe(500);
  });
});
```

`test/logger.service.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { LoggerService } from '../src/services/logger.service';

describe('LoggerService - Structured Logging Abstraction', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    LoggerService.clearHistory();
    LoggerService.setMinLevel('DEBUG');
  });

  it('records debug, info, warn, and error log entries with ISO timestamps', () => {
    LoggerService.debug('Debugging database connection', { pool: 'master' });
    LoggerService.info('Tournament created', { tournamentId: 't-123' });
    LoggerService.warn('High memory usage detected', { usagePercent: 88 });
    LoggerService.error('Transaction simulation failed', new Error('RPC_TIMEOUT'), {
      txHash: '0xabc',
    });

    const history = LoggerService.getHistory();
    expect(history).toHaveLength(4);

    expect(history[0].level).toBe('DEBUG');
    expect(history[0].message).toBe('Debugging database connection');
    expect(history[0].context?.pool).toBe('master');

    expect(history[1].level).toBe('INFO');
    expect(history[1].context?.tournamentId).toBe('t-123');

    expect(history[2].level).toBe('WARN');

    expect(history[3].level).toBe('ERROR');
    expect(history[3].error?.message).toBe('RPC_TIMEOUT');
    expect(history[3].context?.txHash).toBe('0xabc');
  });

  it('filters out messages below the minimum configured log level', () => {
    LoggerService.setMinLevel('WARN');

    LoggerService.debug('Hidden debug message');
    LoggerService.info('Hidden info message');
    LoggerService.warn('Visible warn message');
    LoggerService.error('Visible error message');

    const history = LoggerService.getHistory();
    // Buffer records, but verify level priority check
    expect(history.filter((e) => e.level === 'WARN' || e.level === 'ERROR')).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('scoped logger seamlessly appends parent context to all messages', () => {
    const scoped = LoggerService.withContext({
      requestId: 'req-uuid-1234',
      service: 'settlement',
    });

    scoped.info('Calculating payouts', { recipientCount: 3 });

    const history = LoggerService.getHistory();
    expect(history).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(history[0].context?.requestId).toBe('req-uuid-1234');
    expect(history[0].context?.service).toBe('settlement');
    expect(history[0].context?.recipientCount).toBe(3);
  });
});
```

`test/match-seed.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { ReplayService } from '../src/services/replay.service';
import { PrngService } from '../src/services/prng.service';

describe('Match Seed Derivation & Anti-Precomputation Verification', () => {
  const tournamentId = 'tourn-seed-test-001';
  const matchId = 'match-seed-session-123';
  const playerWallet = 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB';

  it('generates deterministic 64-character hexadecimal SHA-256 seed', () => {
    const seed1 = ReplayService.deriveCanonicalSeed(tournamentId, matchId, playerWallet);
    const seed2 = ReplayService.deriveCanonicalSeed(tournamentId, matchId, playerWallet);

    expect(seed1).toHaveLength(64);
    expect(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(seed1)).toBe(true);
    expect(seed1).toBe(seed2);

    // Altering any input must alter the output hash
    const differentWalletSeed = ReplayService.deriveCanonicalSeed(
      tournamentId,
      matchId,
      'GBOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2GB6V2A73VUX6KAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V',
    );
    expect(differentWalletSeed).not.toBe(seed1);
  });

  it('validates matching claimed seed and computes valid 32-bit numeric PRNG seed', () => {
    const canonical = ReplayService.deriveCanonicalSeed(tournamentId, matchId, playerWallet);
    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatchSeed(
      tournamentId,
      matchId,
      playerWallet,
      canonical,
    );

    expect(verification.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(verification.canonicalSeed).toBe(canonical);
    expect(typeof verification.numericSeed).toBe('number');
    expect(verification.numericSeed).toBe(PrngService.hashStringToSeed(canonical));
    expect(verification.error).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('rejects tampered or mismatched claimed seed with descriptive error', () => {
    const fraudulentSeed = '0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef';
    const verification = ReplayService.verifyMatchSeed(
      tournamentId,
      matchId,
      playerWallet,
      fraudulentSeed,
    );

    expect(verification.valid).toBe(false);
    expect(verification.error).toContain('SEED_MISMATCH');
    expect(verification.canonicalSeed).not.toBe(fraudulentSeed);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { MetricsService } from '../src/services/metrics.service';

describe('MetricsService & Prometheus Endpoint', () => {

    MetricsService.reset();
  });

  it('tracks matches and settlements accurately in memory', () => {
    MetricsService.recordMatch('verified');
    MetricsService.recordMatch('verified');
    MetricsService.recordMatch('failed');

    MetricsService.recordSettlement('success', 250);
    MetricsService.recordSettlement('failed', 450);

    const m = MetricsService.getMetrics();

    expect(m.matchesPlayed.failed).toBe(1);
    expect(m.settlements.success).toBe(1);
    expect(m.settlements.failed).toBe(1);

    MetricsService.recordMatch('verified');
    MetricsService.recordSettlement('success', 1200);

    expect(raw).toContain('# TYPE splash_matches_total counter');
```

`test/replay-diff.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { ReplayDiffService } from '../src/services/replay-diff.service';
import { GameEvent } from '../src/types';

describe('ReplayDiffService - Match Replay Diff & Determinism Analysis', () => {
  const baseStream: GameEvent[] = [
    { seq: 1, timestamp: 1000, type: 'ROUND_STARTED', points: 0, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 },
    { seq: 2, timestamp: 1200, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
    { seq: 3, timestamp: 1500, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 200, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 },
    { seq: 4, timestamp: 2000, type: 'ROUND_ENDED', points: 200, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 },
  ];

  it('reports two identical streams as identical and compatible', () => {
    const streamClone = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(baseStream));
    const result = ReplayDiffService.compareReplays(baseStream, streamClone);

    expect(result.isIdentical).toBe(true);
    expect(result.isCompatible).toBe(true);
    expect(result.divergenceIndex).toBeNull();
    expect(result.maxTimestampDriftMs).toBe(0);
    expect(result.scoreDelta).toBe(0);
  });

  it('detects acceptable sub-threshold timestamp jitter without divergence', () => {
    const jitteredStream: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1005, type: 'ROUND_STARTED', points: 0, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 }, // 5ms jitter
      { seq: 2, timestamp: 1210, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 }, // 10ms jitter
      { seq: 3, timestamp: 1500, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 200, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 }, // 0ms jitter
      { seq: 4, timestamp: 2015, type: 'ROUND_ENDED', points: 200, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 }, // 15ms jitter
    ];

    const result = ReplayDiffService.compareReplays(baseStream, jitteredStream, {
      maxAllowableDriftMs: 50,
    });

    expect(result.isIdentical).toBe(false); // timestamps differed slightly
    expect(result.isCompatible).toBe(true); // within 50ms tolerance
    expect(result.maxTimestampDriftMs).toBe(15);
    expect(result.scoreDelta).toBe(0);
  });

  it('detects score divergence and pinpoint index', () => {
    const divergentStream: GameEvent[] = [
      { seq: 1, timestamp: 1000, type: 'ROUND_STARTED', points: 0, combo: 0, multiplier: 1 },
      { seq: 2, timestamp: 1200, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
      { seq: 3, timestamp: 1500, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 999, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 }, // Score mismatch!
      { seq: 4, timestamp: 2000, type: 'ROUND_ENDED', points: 999, combo: 2, multiplier: 1 },
    ];

    const result = ReplayDiffService.compareReplays(baseStream, divergentStream);

    expect(result.isCompatible).toBe(false);
    expect(result.divergenceIndex).toBe(2);
    expect(result.divergenceReason).toContain('Score mismatch at index 2');
  });

  it('rejects streams with mismatched event lengths', () => {
    const truncatedStream = baseStream.slice(0, 2);
    const result = ReplayDiffService.compareReplays(baseStream, truncatedStream);

    expect(result.isCompatible).toBe(false);
    expect(result.divergenceReason).toContain('Event count mismatch');
  });
});
```

`test/replay-export.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '../src/app';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { MatchSession, GameResult } from '../src/types';

describe('Match Replay Snapshot Exporter & Audit Verification', () => {
  const validWallet = 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB';
  const app = createApp();

  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
  });

  it('returns HTTP 404 for non-existent match replay export', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/games/matches/non-existent-match-id/export');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('exports full match replay with cryptographic archive digest and audit headers', async () => {
    const matchId = 'match-audit-export-101';
    const tournamentId = 'tourn-export-101';
    const now = Date.now();

    const mockSession: MatchSession = {
      matchId,
      sessionToken: 'token-export-101',
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: validWallet,
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      startTime: now - 30000,
      endTime: now,
      status: 'COMPLETED',
      events: [
        {
          seq: 1,
          timestamp: now - 28000,
          type: 'TARGET_HIT',
          points: 100,
          combo: 1,
          multiplier: 1,
        },
        {
          seq: 2,
          timestamp: now - 25000,
          type: 'TARGET_HIT',
          points: 100,
          combo: 2,
          multiplier: 1,
        },
      ],
    };

    const mockResult: GameResult = {
      matchId,
      tournamentId,
      playerWallet: validWallet,
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      startTime: now - 30000,
      endTime: now,
      durationMs: 30000,
      baseScore: 200,
      comboBonus: 0,
      hazardPenalties: 0,
      finalScore: 200,
      maxCombo: 2,
      accuracy: 100,
      targetHits: 2,
      hazardHits: 0,
      misses: 0,
      eventsCount: 2,
      resultHash: '0x1234567890abcdef1234567890abcdef1234567890abcdef1234567890abcdef',
      verified: true,
      createdAt: now,
    };

    StoreService.createMatchSession(mockSession);
    StoreService.saveGameResult(mockResult);

    const res = await request(app).get(`/api/games/matches/${matchId}/export`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.matchId).toBe(matchId);
    expect(res.body.tournamentId).toBe(tournamentId);
    expect(res.body.playerWallet).toBe(validWallet);
    expect(res.body.eventsCount).toBe(2);
    expect(res.body.result.finalScore).toBe(200);

    // Cryptographic archive digest validation
    expect(res.body.archiveDigest).toBeDefined();
    expect(res.body.archiveDigest).toHaveLength(64);

    // Verify response audit headers
    expect(res.headers['x-audit-match-id']).toBe(matchId);
    expect(res.headers['x-audit-result-hash']).toBe(mockResult.resultHash);
    expect(res.headers['x-audit-archive-digest']).toBe(res.body.archiveDigest);
    expect(res.headers['x-audit-exported-at']).toBeDefined();
  });
});
```

`test/tournament-expiration-worker.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { TournamentExpirationWorker } from '../src/services/expiration.worker';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { EventBusService } from '../src/services/event-bus.service';
import { Tournament } from '../src/types';

describe('TournamentExpirationWorker', () => {
  const baseTournament = (id: string, endTime: number, state: Tournament['state']): Tournament => ({
    id,
    name: `Tournament ${id}`,
    description: 'Test tournament description',
    gameId: 'splash-rush',
    gameVersion: '1.0.0',
    creatorWallet: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
    prizeAsset: 'XLM',
    prizePoolAmount: 500,
    maxParticipants: 16,
    currentParticipants: 4,
    startTime: endTime - 3600_000,
    endTime,
    state,
    createdAt: endTime - 3600_000,
    updatedAt: endTime - 3600_000,
  });

  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
    TournamentExpirationWorker.stop();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    TournamentExpirationWorker.stop();
  });

  it('leaves active tournaments with future end times untouched', () => {
    const now = 1_700_000_000_000;
    const futureTourn = baseTournament('tourn-future', now + 60_000, 'OPEN');
    StoreService.saveTournament(futureTourn);

    const finalized = TournamentExpirationWorker.checkExpiredTournaments(now);
    expect(finalized).toHaveLength(0);

    const reloaded = StoreService.getTournamentById('tourn-future');
    expect(reloaded?.state).toBe('OPEN');
  });

  it('automatically transitions overdue active tournaments to FINALIZED', () => {
    const now = 1_700_000_000_000;
    const expiredOpen = baseTournament('tourn-expired-1', now - 1000, 'OPEN');
    const expiredInProgress = baseTournament('tourn-expired-2', now - 5000, 'IN_PROGRESS');
    const futureOpen = baseTournament('tourn-active-future', now + 100_000, 'OPEN');

    StoreService.saveTournament(expiredOpen);
    StoreService.saveTournament(expiredInProgress);
    StoreService.saveTournament(futureOpen);

    const broadcastSpy = vi.spyOn(EventBusService, 'broadcast');

    const finalized = TournamentExpirationWorker.checkExpiredTournaments(now);
    expect(finalized).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(finalized.map((t) => t.id)).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining(['tourn-expired-1', 'tourn-expired-2']),
    );

    // Verify state updated in StoreService
    expect(StoreService.getTournamentById('tourn-expired-1')?.state).toBe('FINALIZED');
    expect(StoreService.getTournamentById('tourn-expired-2')?.state).toBe('FINALIZED');
    expect(StoreService.getTournamentById('tourn-active-future')?.state).toBe('OPEN');

    // Verify broadcast event was dispatched
    expect(broadcastSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      'TOURNAMENT_FINALIZED',
      expect.objectContaining({
        tournamentId: 'tourn-expired-1',
        reason: 'CUTOFF_TIME_ELAPSED',
      }),
    );
  });

  it('does not re-finalize tournaments that are already SETTLED or CANCELLED', () => {
    const now = 1_700_000_000_000;
    const settled = baseTournament('tourn-settled', now - 10_000, 'SETTLED');
    const cancelled = baseTournament('tourn-cancelled', now - 10_000, 'CANCELLED');

    StoreService.saveTournament(settled);
    StoreService.saveTournament(cancelled);

    const finalized = TournamentExpirationWorker.checkExpiredTournaments(now);
    expect(finalized).toHaveLength(0);

    expect(StoreService.getTournamentById('tourn-settled')?.state).toBe('SETTLED');
    expect(StoreService.getTournamentById('tourn-cancelled')?.state).toBe('CANCELLED');
  });

  it('starts and stops background interval timer cleanly', () => {
    expect(TournamentExpirationWorker.isRunning()).toBe(false);

    TournamentExpirationWorker.start(500);
    expect(TournamentExpirationWorker.isRunning()).toBe(true);

    TournamentExpirationWorker.stop();
    expect(TournamentExpirationWorker.isRunning()).toBe(false);
  });
});

```

`test/tournament-pagination.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createApp } from '../src/app';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { Tournament } from '../src/types';

describe('Tournament Pagination & Query Filters', () => {
  const app = createApp();

  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();

    // Populate multiple mock tournaments
    for (let i = 1; i <= 25; i++) {
      const tourn: Tournament = {
        id: `t-${i.toString().padStart(3, '0')}`,
        name: `Tournament #${i}`,
        description: `Description for tournament ${i}`,
        gameId: 'splash-rush',
        gameVersion: '1.0.0',
        creatorWallet: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
        prizeAsset: 'USDC',
        prizePoolAmount: 100 * i,
        vaultAddress: 'GB6V2A73VUX6KAZD66N6E64B44J7K3XW6P2W3V7QOXWCV7T4KQL3ZPL2',
        maxParticipants: 16,
        currentParticipants: 2,
        startTime: 1000 + i * 10,
        endTime: 2000 + i * 10,
        state: i % 2 === 0 ? 'OPEN' : 'COMPLETED',
        createdAt: 1000 + i * 10,
        updatedAt: 1000 + i * 10,
      };
      StoreService.saveTournament(tourn);
    }
  });

  it('paginates results with page and limit parameters', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/tournaments?page=1&limit=10');

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.total).toBe(25);
    expect(res.body.totalPages).toBe(3);
    expect(res.body.count).toBe(10);
    expect(res.body.tournaments).toHaveLength(10);

    // Verify page 2 has next 10
    const res2 = await request(app).get('/api/tournaments?page=2&limit=10');
    expect(res2.body.count).toBe(10);
    expect(res2.body.page).toBe(2);

    // Verify page 3 has remaining 5
    const res3 = await request(app).get('/api/tournaments?page=3&limit=10');
    expect(res3.body.count).toBe(5);
    expect(res3.body.page).toBe(3);
  });

  it('filters tournaments by state', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/tournaments?status=OPEN');

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.total).toBe(12); // Even numbers between 1 and 25
    expect(res.body.tournaments.every((t: Tournament) => t.state === 'OPEN')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('sorts tournaments in ascending and descending order', async () => {
    const resDesc = await request(app).get('/api/tournaments?sort=desc&limit=5');
    expect(resDesc.body.tournaments[0].id).toBe('t-025');

    const resAsc = await request(app).get('/api/tournaments?sort=asc&limit=5');
    expect(resAsc.body.tournaments[0].id).toBe('t-001');
  });
});
```

`test/tournament-refund.worker.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { TournamentRefundWorker } from '../src/services/tournament-refund.worker';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { EventBusService } from '../src/services/event-bus.service';
import { Tournament } from '../src/types';

describe('TournamentRefundWorker', () => {
  const makeTournament = (
    id: string,
    state: Tournament['state'],
    participants: number,
    prize: number,
  ): Tournament => ({
    id,
    name: `Tournament ${id}`,
    description: 'Refund worker test',
    gameId: 'splash-rush',
    gameVersion: '1.0.0',
    creatorWallet: 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB',
    prizeAsset: 'XLM',
    prizePoolAmount: prize,
    maxParticipants: 16,
    currentParticipants: participants,
    startTime: Date.now() - 3600_000,
    endTime: Date.now() - 1000,
    state,
    createdAt: Date.now() - 3600_000,
    updatedAt: Date.now() - 1000,
  });

  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
    TournamentRefundWorker.reset();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    TournamentRefundWorker.stop();
  });

  it('processes refund for cancelled tournament with escrow balance', () => {
    const cancelled = makeTournament('tourn-cancelled', 'CANCELLED', 2, 750);
    StoreService.saveTournament(cancelled);

    const broadcastSpy = vi.spyOn(EventBusService, 'broadcast');

    const refunds = TournamentRefundWorker.processPendingRefunds();
    expect(refunds).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(refunds[0].tournamentId).toBe('tourn-cancelled');
    expect(refunds[0].refundAmount).toBe(750);
    expect(refunds[0].reason).toBe('TOURNAMENT_CANCELLED');

    expect(broadcastSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      'TOURNAMENT_FINALIZED',
      expect.objectContaining({
        action: 'REFUND_DISPATCHED',
        refundAmount: 750,
      }),
    );

    // Second pass should not duplicate refund
    const secondPass = TournamentRefundWorker.processPendingRefunds();
    expect(secondPass).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('processes refund for finalized tournament with zero participants', () => {
    const uncontested = makeTournament('tourn-empty', 'FINALIZED', 0, 500);
    StoreService.saveTournament(uncontested);

    const refunds = TournamentRefundWorker.processPendingRefunds();
    expect(refunds).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(refunds[0].tournamentId).toBe('tourn-empty');
    expect(refunds[0].reason).toBe('ZERO_PARTICIPANTS_UNCONTESTED');
  });

  it('ignores active or contested finalized tournaments', () => {
    const active = makeTournament('tourn-active', 'OPEN', 5, 500);
    const contested = makeTournament('tourn-contested', 'FINALIZED', 8, 1000);
    StoreService.saveTournament(active);
    StoreService.saveTournament(contested);

    const refunds = TournamentRefundWorker.processPendingRefunds();
    expect(refunds).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
```

`test/webhook.service.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { WebhookService } from '../src/services/webhook.service';

describe('WebhookService - HMAC-SHA256 Signed Event Dispatcher', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    WebhookService.clear();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    WebhookService.clear();
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it('generates and verifies HMAC-SHA256 payload signatures', () => {
    const payload = JSON.stringify({ event: 'TOURNAMENT_SETTLED', tournamentId: '101' });
    const secret = 'super-secret-key-123';

    const signature = WebhookService.generateSignature(payload, secret);
    expect(signature).toHaveLength(64); // SHA-256 hex string

    // Correct signature passes verification
    const isValid = WebhookService.verifySignature(payload, secret, signature);
    expect(isValid).toBe(true);

    // With sha256= prefix
    const isValidPrefix = WebhookService.verifySignature(payload, secret, `sha256=${signature}`);
    expect(isValidPrefix).toBe(true);

    // Tampered payload fails
    const tamperedPayload = JSON.stringify({ event: 'TOURNAMENT_SETTLED', tournamentId: '999' });
    const isTamperedValid = WebhookService.verifySignature(tamperedPayload, secret, signature);
    expect(isTamperedValid).toBe(false);

    // Incorrect secret fails
    const isWrongSecretValid = WebhookService.verifySignature(payload, 'wrong-secret', signature);
    expect(isWrongSecretValid).toBe(false);
  });

  it('registers and removes webhooks by ID', () => {
    const hook = WebhookService.registerWebhook({
      url: 'https://example.com/webhook',
      secret: 'sec-1',
      events: ['TOURNAMENT_FUNDED'],
      active: true,
    });

    expect(hook.id).toBeDefined();
    expect(WebhookService.getWebhooks()).toHaveLength(1);

    const removed = WebhookService.removeWebhook(hook.id);
    expect(removed).toBe(true);
    expect(WebhookService.getWebhooks()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('dispatches payload with HMAC signature headers to subscribed webhooks', async () => {
    const mockFetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      status: 200,
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', mockFetch);

    WebhookService.registerWebhook({
      url: 'https://discord-bot.stellar/hook',
      secret: 'discord-sec',
      events: ['TOURNAMENT_SETTLED'],
      active: true,
    });

    // Webhook subscribed to different event should not receive this dispatch
    WebhookService.registerWebhook({
      url: 'https://other-service.stellar/hook',
      secret: 'other-sec',
      events: ['UNRELATED_EVENT'],
      active: true,
    });

    const reports = await WebhookService.dispatch('TOURNAMENT_SETTLED', {
      tournamentId: 501,
      prizeAmount: 100000000,
    });

    expect(reports).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(reports[0].success).toBe(true);
    expect(reports[0].statusCode).toBe(200);

    expect(mockFetch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const [callUrl, callOptions] = mockFetch.mock.calls[0];
    expect(callUrl).toBe('https://discord-bot.stellar/hook');
    expect(callOptions.headers['X-StellarSplash-Event']).toBe('TOURNAMENT_SETTLED');
    expect(callOptions.headers['X-StellarSplash-Signature']).toMatch(/^sha256=[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
  });
});
```
