`tests/admin-auth.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { adminAuthMiddleware } from '../src/middleware/admin-auth.middleware';

describe('adminAuthMiddleware', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
  });

  it('allows access when valid x-admin-key header is provided', () => {
    const req = {
      headers: { 'x-admin-key': 'test-secret-123' },
      path: '/api/admin/maintenance',
      method: 'POST',
    } as unknown as Request;

    const res = {
      status: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(),
      json: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as Response;

    const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

    const middleware = adminAuthMiddleware({ customSecret: 'test-secret-123' });
    middleware(req, res, next);

    expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(res.status).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('allows access when Bearer token matches admin secret', () => {
    const req = {
      headers: { authorization: 'Bearer bearer-secret-456' },
      path: '/api/admin/disputes/resolve',
      method: 'POST',
    } as unknown as Request;

    const res = {
      status: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(),
      json: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as Response;

    const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

    const middleware = adminAuthMiddleware({ customSecret: 'bearer-secret-456' });
    middleware(req, res, next);

    expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('allows access when x-admin-wallet matches authorized wallet list', () => {
    const req = {
      headers: { 'x-admin-wallet': 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB' },
      path: '/api/admin/upgrade',
      method: 'POST',
    } as unknown as Request;

    const res = {
      status: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(),
      json: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as Response;

    const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

    const middleware = adminAuthMiddleware();
    middleware(req, res, next);

    expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('rejects unauthorized requests with HTTP 403 and records audit log', () => {
    const req = {
      headers: {},
      path: '/api/admin/reconcile',
      method: 'POST',
      ip: '192.168.1.1',
    } as unknown as Request;

    const jsonMock = vi.fn();
    const res = {
      status: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ json: jsonMock }),
    } as unknown as Response;

    const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

    const middleware = adminAuthMiddleware({ customSecret: 'my-super-secret' });
    middleware(req, res, next);

    expect(next).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(403);
    expect(jsonMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({
        success: false,
        error: expect.objectContaining({ code: 'ADMIN_UNAUTHORIZED' }),
      })
    );

    const auditLogs = StoreService.getAuditLogs();
    expect(auditLogs.some((l) => l.eventType === 'ADMIN_AUTH_FAILED')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects unauthenticated x-admin-wallet header spoofing in production mode', () => {
    const originalEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;
    try {
      process.env.NODE_ENV = 'production';
      const req = {
        headers: { 'x-admin-wallet': 'GA2C5RFPE6GCKMY3US5PAB6UZLKIGAHWKXX2GAKVOOUMVQHGASVMOROB' },
        path: '/api/admin/upgrade',
        method: 'POST',
      } as unknown as Request;

      const jsonMock = vi.fn();
      const res = {
        status: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ json: jsonMock }),
      } as unknown as Response;

      const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;
      const middleware = adminAuthMiddleware();
      middleware(req, res, next);

      expect(next).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(403);
    } finally {
      process.env.NODE_ENV = originalEnv;
    }
  });
});
```

`tests/anti-cheat-entropy.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { AntiCheatEntropyService } from '../src/services/anti-cheat-entropy.service';
import { PlayerInputTelemetry } from '../src/types/anti-cheat-entropy';

describe('AntiCheatEntropyService', () => {
  it('detects high linearity bot trajectory', () => {
    // 25 points along an exact straight line y = 2x
    const movements = Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, i) => ({
      x: i * 10,
      y: i * 20,
      timestamp: Date.now() + i * 16,
    }));

    const linearity = AntiCheatEntropyService.calculateLinearity(movements);
    expect(linearity).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.99);

    const telemetry: PlayerInputTelemetry = {
      playerId: 'BOT_PLAYER_1',
      matchId: 'MATCH_999',
      gameDurationMs: 5000,
      mouseMovements: movements,
      clicks: [],
      keyPressIntervalsMs: [120, 115, 130],
    };

    const report = AntiCheatEntropyService.evaluate(telemetry);
    expect(report.isFlaggedSuspicious).toBe(true);
    expect(report.reasons.some((r) => r.includes('linearity'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('permits natural human curves with tremors', () => {
    // Points with sinusoidal natural curve
    const movements = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => ({
      x: i * 10,
      y: 100 + Math.sin(i * 0.4) * 40 + (i % 2 === 0 ? 2 : -2),
      timestamp: Date.now() + i * 16,
    }));

    const linearity = AntiCheatEntropyService.calculateLinearity(movements);
    expect(linearity).toBeLessThan(0.95);

    const telemetry: PlayerInputTelemetry = {
      playerId: 'HUMAN_PLAYER_1',
      matchId: 'MATCH_998',
      gameDurationMs: 10000,
      mouseMovements: movements,
      clicks: [
        { x: 10, y: 20, timestamp: 1000 },
        { x: 15, y: 22, timestamp: 1250 },
        { x: 20, y: 25, timestamp: 1600 },
      ],
      keyPressIntervalsMs: [120, 185, 95, 210, 140, 160],
    };

    const report = AntiCheatEntropyService.evaluate(telemetry);
    expect(report.isFlaggedSuspicious).toBe(false);
    expect(report.reasons).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('flags superhuman click bursts under 25ms', () => {
    const telemetry: PlayerInputTelemetry = {
      playerId: 'AUTOCLICKER_1',
      matchId: 'MATCH_997',
      gameDurationMs: 5000,
      mouseMovements: [],
      clicks: [
        { x: 50, y: 50, timestamp: 1000 },
        { x: 50, y: 50, timestamp: 1010 }, // 10ms
        { x: 50, y: 50, timestamp: 1022 }, // 12ms
        { x: 50, y: 50, timestamp: 1035 }, // 13ms
      ],
      keyPressIntervalsMs: [],
    };

    const report = AntiCheatEntropyService.evaluate(telemetry);
    expect(report.isFlaggedSuspicious).toBe(true);
    expect(report.reasons.some((r) => r.includes('Superhuman click'))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`tests/audit-export.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { AuditExportService } from '../src/services/audit-export.service';
import { Tournament } from '../src/types';

describe('AuditExportService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
  });

  it('generates a tamper-evident audit report with SHA-256 integrity hash', () => {
    const tournament: Tournament = {
      id: 'tourn-aud-1',
      name: 'Audit Cup 2026',
      description: 'Audit test tournament',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      creatorWallet: 'GA_ADMIN',
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 1000,
      vaultAddress: 'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
      maxParticipants: 16,
      currentParticipants: 8,
      startTime: 1000,
      endTime: 2000,
      state: 'COMPLETED',
      createdAt: 500,
      updatedAt: 2000,
    };

    StoreService.saveTournament(tournament);
    StoreService.logAudit('TEST_EVENT', 'GA_ADMIN', { tournamentId: 'tourn-aud-1' }, 1500);

    const report = AuditExportService.generateReport('tourn-aud-1', 5000);

    expect(report.tournamentId).toBe('tourn-aud-1');
    expect(report.tournament?.name).toBe('Audit Cup 2026');
    expect(report.auditTrail.length).toBe(2);
    expect(report.integrityHash).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);

    // Verify integrity
    expect(AuditExportService.verifyReportIntegrity(report)).toBe(true);

    // Tampering invalidates verification
    const tampered = { ...report, tournamentId: 'tourn-tampered' };
    expect(AuditExportService.verifyReportIntegrity(tampered)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`tests/channel-account.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { ChannelAccountManagerService } from '../src/services/channel-account.service';

describe('ChannelAccountManagerService', () => {
  let manager: ChannelAccountManagerService;

  beforeEach(() => {
    manager = new ChannelAccountManagerService();
  });

  it('registers channel accounts properly', () => {
    manager.registerChannel('GACCOUNT1', 'SSECRET1', '50000000');
    const metrics = manager.getMetrics();

    expect(metrics.totalChannels).toBe(1);
    expect(metrics.availableChannels).toBe(1);
    expect(metrics.busyChannels).toBe(0);
    expect(metrics.queueDepth).toBe(0);
  });

  it('acquires and locks available channels', () => {
    manager.registerChannel('GACCOUNT1', 'SSECRET1');
    const ch = manager.acquireChannel();

    expect(ch).not.toBeNull();
    expect(ch?.publicKey).toBe('GACCOUNT1');
    expect(ch?.isLocked).toBe(true);

    // Second acquire should return null since all are locked
    const ch2 = manager.acquireChannel();
    expect(ch2).toBeNull();

    const metrics = manager.getMetrics();
    expect(metrics.availableChannels).toBe(0);
    expect(metrics.busyChannels).toBe(1);
  });

  it('releases locked channels with updated sequence number', () => {
    manager.registerChannel('GACCOUNT1', 'SSECRET1');
    const ch = manager.acquireChannel();
    expect(ch).not.toBeNull();

    manager.releaseChannel('GACCOUNT1', '123456789');

    const metrics = manager.getMetrics();
    expect(metrics.availableChannels).toBe(1);
    expect(metrics.busyChannels).toBe(0);

    const reacquired = manager.acquireChannel();
    expect(reacquired?.publicKey).toBe('GACCOUNT1');
    expect(reacquired?.lastSequenceNumber).toBe('123456789');
  });

  it('enqueues and processes submission queue items', () => {
    const item1 = manager.enqueueSubmission({
      tournamentId: 101,
      transactionXdr: 'AAAA1111',
      action: 'settle',
    });
    const item2 = manager.enqueueSubmission({
      tournamentId: 102,
      transactionXdr: 'BBBB2222',
      action: 'register',
    });

    expect(item1.id).toBeDefined();
    expect(item1.status).toBe('pending');
    expect(manager.getMetrics().queueDepth).toBe(2);

    const next = manager.getNextPendingSubmission();
    expect(next?.id).toBe(item1.id);

    manager.updateSubmissionStatus(item1.id, 'confirmed', 'tx_hash_1');
    expect(manager.getSubmission(item1.id)?.status).toBe('confirmed');

    const next2 = manager.getNextPendingSubmission();
    expect(next2?.id).toBe(item2.id);

    manager.updateSubmissionStatus(item2.id, 'failed', undefined, 'insufficient fee');
    expect(manager.getSubmission(item2.id)?.status).toBe('failed');
  });
});
```

`tests/circuit-breaker.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { CircuitBreakerService } from '../src/services/circuit-breaker.service';

describe('CircuitBreakerService', () => {
  let breaker: CircuitBreakerService;

  beforeEach(() => {
    breaker = new CircuitBreakerService(
      ['http://rpc-node-1.test', 'http://rpc-node-2.test'],
      { failureThreshold: 2, recoveryTimeMs: 100 }
    );
  });

  it('starts in CLOSED state with primary node active', () => {
    const status = breaker.getStatus();
    expect(status.state).toBe('CLOSED');
    expect(status.activeEndpoint).toBe('http://rpc-node-1.test');
    expect(status.totalTrips).toBe(0);
  });

  it('records successful calls and tracks latency', () => {
    breaker.recordSuccess(45);
    const status = breaker.getStatus();
    expect(status.nodes[0].lastLatencyMs).toBe(45);
    expect(status.nodes[0].isHealthy).toBe(true);
    expect(status.nodes[0].consecutiveFailures).toBe(0);
  });

  it('trips circuit to OPEN after reaching failure threshold and fails over', () => {
    breaker.recordFailure();
    expect(breaker.getStatus().state).toBe('CLOSED'); // First failure

    breaker.recordFailure(); // Second failure (threshold = 2)
    const status = breaker.getStatus();
    expect(status.state).toBe('OPEN');
    expect(status.totalTrips).toBe(1);
    expect(status.activeEndpoint).toBe('http://rpc-node-2.test'); // Failed over to node 2
  });

  it('transitions to HALF_OPEN after recovery time and restores to CLOSED on success', async () => {
    breaker.recordFailure();
    breaker.recordFailure();
    expect(breaker.getStatus().state).toBe('OPEN');

    // Wait for recovery time (100ms)
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 120));

    expect(breaker.getStatus().state).toBe('HALF_OPEN');

    breaker.recordSuccess(30);
    expect(breaker.getStatus().state).toBe('CLOSED');
  });
});
```

`tests/db-compaction.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { DatabaseCompactionService } from '../src/services/db-compaction.service';
import { MatchSession } from '../src/types';

describe('DatabaseCompactionService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
  });

  it('prunes expired audit logs while keeping fresh entries', () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const tenDaysAgo = now - 10 * 86400 * 1000;
    const fortyDaysAgo = now - 40 * 86400 * 1000;

    StoreService.logAudit('RECENT_ACTION', 'GA_USER_1', { data: 1 }, tenDaysAgo);
    StoreService.logAudit('OLD_ACTION', 'GA_USER_2', { data: 2 }, fortyDaysAgo);

    const report = DatabaseCompactionService.compact(
      { auditLogRetentionMs: 30 * 86400 * 1000 },
      now
    );

    expect(report.prunedAuditLogs).toBe(1);
    const currentLogs = StoreService.getAuditLogs();
    expect(currentLogs.some((l) => l.eventType === 'OLD_ACTION')).toBe(false);
    expect(currentLogs.some((l) => l.eventType === 'RECENT_ACTION')).toBe(true);
    expect(currentLogs.some((l) => l.eventType === 'DB_COMPACTION')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('prunes abandoned draft matches while preserving completed matches', () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const twoDaysAgo = now - 2 * 86400 * 1000;

    const staleDraft: MatchSession = {
      matchId: 'stale-match-1',
      tournamentId: 't-1',
      playerWallet: 'GA_1',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      sessionToken: 'token-1',
      status: 'PLAYING',
      startTime: twoDaysAgo,
    };

    const completedMatch: MatchSession = {
      matchId: 'completed-match-2',
      tournamentId: 't-1',
      playerWallet: 'GA_2',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      sessionToken: 'token-2',
      status: 'COMPLETED',
      startTime: twoDaysAgo,
      endTime: twoDaysAgo + 60000,
    };

    StoreService.createMatchSession(staleDraft);
    StoreService.createMatchSession(completedMatch);

    const report = DatabaseCompactionService.compact(
      { staleDraftMatchRetentionMs: 86400 * 1000 },
      now
    );

    expect(report.prunedStaleMatches).toBe(1);
    expect(StoreService.getMatchSession('stale-match-1')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(StoreService.getMatchSession('completed-match-2')).toBeDefined();
  });
});
```

`tests/dispute-resolution.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { DisputeResolutionService } from '../src/services/dispute-resolution.service';

describe('DisputeResolutionService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    DisputeResolutionService.reset();
  });

  it('files dispute and tracks active status', () => {
    const dispute = DisputeResolutionService.fileDispute(
      'tourney-1',
      'GBBD...1',
      101,
      'Tick sequence mismatch',
      'hash_evidence_1'
    );

    expect(dispute.status).toBe('ACTIVE');
    expect(dispute.tournamentId).toBe('tourney-1');
    expect(DisputeResolutionService.isTournamentDisputed('tourney-1')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects duplicate active dispute filing on same tournament', () => {
    DisputeResolutionService.fileDispute('tourney-2', 'GBBD...1', 101, 'First dispute');

    expect(() =>
      DisputeResolutionService.fileDispute('tourney-2', 'GBBD...2', 102, 'Second dispute')
    ).toThrowError(/Active dispute already exists/);
  });

  it('resolves dispute and records admin resolution notes', () => {
    DisputeResolutionService.fileDispute('tourney-3', 'GBBD...1', 103, 'Challenge');

    const resolved = DisputeResolutionService.resolveDispute(
      'tourney-3',
      'GA_ADMIN',
      'Confirmed tick manipulation; scores adjusted'
    );

    expect(resolved?.status).toBe('RESOLVED');
    expect(resolved?.resolvedBy).toBe('GA_ADMIN');
    expect(DisputeResolutionService.isTournamentDisputed('tourney-3')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('dismisses unsubstantiated challenges cleanly', () => {
    DisputeResolutionService.fileDispute('tourney-4', 'GBBD...1', 104, 'Spurious challenge');

    const dismissed = DisputeResolutionService.dismissDispute(
      'tourney-4',
      'GA_ADMIN',
      'Replay verified 100% deterministic'
    );

    expect(dismissed?.status).toBe('DISMISSED');
    expect(DisputeResolutionService.isTournamentDisputed('tourney-4')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`tests/docker-healthcheck.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

function parseHealthPayload(jsonString: string): { status: string; isHealthy: boolean } {
  try {
    const data = JSON.parse(jsonString);
    return {
      status: data.status || 'unknown',
      isHealthy: data.status === 'ok',
    };
  } catch {
    return { status: 'error', isHealthy: false };
  }
}

describe('Docker Healthcheck Response Parser', () => {
  it('parses valid ok health check json', () => {
    const raw = JSON.stringify({
      status: 'ok',
      service: 'stellar-splash-engine',
      version: '1.0.0',
      network: 'TESTNET',
      uptimeSeconds: 120,
    });

    const parsed = parseHealthPayload(raw);
    expect(parsed.isHealthy).toBe(true);
    expect(parsed.status).toBe('ok');
  });

  it('flags unparseable or error health payload', () => {
    const parsed = parseHealthPayload('Internal Server Error 500');
    expect(parsed.isHealthy).toBe(false);
    expect(parsed.status).toBe('error');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { EntryFeeReconciliationService } from '../src/services/entry-fee-reconciliation.service';

describe('EntryFeeReconciliationService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    EntryFeeReconciliationService.reset();
  });

  it('records entry fee payments and checks status', () => {
    const payment = EntryFeeReconciliationService.recordPayment(
      'tourney-1',
      'GBBD...1',
      5_000_0000000n,
      'tx_hash_1'
    );

    expect(payment.tournamentId).toBe('tourney-1');
    expect(payment.playerAddress).toBe('GBBD...1');
    expect(payment.amount).toBe(5_000_0000000n);

    expect(EntryFeeReconciliationService.hasPlayerPaid('tourney-1', 'GBBD...1')).toBe(true);
    expect(EntryFeeReconciliationService.hasPlayerPaid('tourney-1', 'GBBD...2')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('reconciles balanced tournaments accurately', () => {
    EntryFeeReconciliationService.recordPayment('tourney-2', 'GBBD...1', 10_000_0000000n, 'tx_1');
    EntryFeeReconciliationService.recordPayment('tourney-2', 'GBBD...2', 10_000_0000000n, 'tx_2');

    const report = EntryFeeReconciliationService.getReconciliationSummary(
      'tourney-2',
      10_000_0000000n,
      2
    );

    expect(report.isBalanced).toBe(true);
    expect(report.totalCollected).toBe(20_000_0000000n);
    expect(report.expectedTotal).toBe(20_000_0000000n);
    expect(report.difference).toBe(0n);
    expect(report.collectedCount).toBe(2);
  });

  it('detects underfunded or overfunded states', () => {
    EntryFeeReconciliationService.recordPayment('tourney-3', 'GBBD...1', 5_000_0000000n, 'tx_1');

    const report = EntryFeeReconciliationService.getReconciliationSummary(
      'tourney-3',
      5_000_0000000n,
      2
    );

    expect(report.isBalanced).toBe(false);
    expect(report.collectedCount).toBe(1);
    expect(report.totalCollected).toBe(5_000_0000000n);
    expect(report.expectedTotal).toBe(10_000_0000000n);
    expect(report.difference).toBe(-5_000_0000000n);
  });

  it('clears tournament payments when requested', () => {
    EntryFeeReconciliationService.recordPayment('tourney-4', 'GBBD...1', 5_000_0000000n, 'tx_1');
    expect(EntryFeeReconciliationService.getPayments('tourney-4').length).toBe(1);

    EntryFeeReconciliationService.clearTournament('tourney-4');
    expect(EntryFeeReconciliationService.getPayments('tourney-4').length).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`tests/env-config-validator.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

function validateEnvParams(params: { contractId: string; rpcUrl: string; horizonUrl: string }): boolean {
  if (!params.contractId.startsWith('C') || params.contractId.length !== 56) return false;
  if (!params.rpcUrl.startsWith('http://') && !params.rpcUrl.startsWith('https://')) return false;
  if (!params.horizonUrl.startsWith('http://') && !params.horizonUrl.startsWith('https://')) return false;
  return true;
}

describe('Environment Config Validator', () => {
  it('accepts valid Soroban contract ID and network URLs', () => {
    const valid = validateEnvParams({
      contractId: 'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
      rpcUrl: 'https://soroban-testnet.stellar.org',
      horizonUrl: 'https://horizon-testnet.stellar.org',
    });
    expect(valid).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects invalid contract address prefix or length', () => {
    const invalid = validateEnvParams({
      contractId: 'GACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ', // G-address instead of C
      rpcUrl: 'https://soroban-testnet.stellar.org',
      horizonUrl: 'https://horizon-testnet.stellar.org',
    });
    expect(invalid).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects malformed HTTP protocols', () => {
    const invalid = validateEnvParams({
      contractId: 'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
      rpcUrl: 'ftp://soroban-testnet.stellar.org',
      horizonUrl: 'https://horizon-testnet.stellar.org',
    });
    expect(invalid).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`tests/forensic-packager.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { ForensicPackagerService } from '../src/services/forensic-packager.service';

describe('ForensicPackagerService', () => {
  it('assembles and signs forensic replay package', () => {
    const pkg = ForensicPackagerService.assemblePackage({
      matchId: 'MATCH_TEST_101',
      tournamentId: 99,
      playerWallets: ['GPLAYER_1', 'GPLAYER_2'],
      gameTitle: 'Aqua Dash',
      seed: 424242,
      tickCount: 3600,
      finalScores: { GPLAYER_1: 1500, GPLAYER_2: 1200 },
      isFlagged: false,
      antiCheatReasons: [],
    });

    expect(pkg.matchId).toBe('MATCH_TEST_101');
    expect(pkg.verificationHash).toBeDefined();
    expect(pkg.serverAttestationSignature).toBeDefined();
    expect(pkg.packageVersion).toBe('1.0.0');

    // Verification should pass
    const isValid = ForensicPackagerService.verifyPackageIntegrity(pkg);
    expect(isValid).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects tampered forensic package', () => {
    const pkg = ForensicPackagerService.assemblePackage({
      matchId: 'MATCH_TEST_102',
      tournamentId: 99,
      playerWallets: ['GPLAYER_1', 'GPLAYER_2'],
      gameTitle: 'Aqua Dash',
      seed: 12345,
      tickCount: 1800,
      finalScores: { GPLAYER_1: 800, GPLAYER_2: 950 },
      isFlagged: false,
      antiCheatReasons: [],
    });

    // Tamper with final scores
    const tampered = {
      ...pkg,
      finalScores: { GPLAYER_1: 10000, GPLAYER_2: 950 },
    };

    const isValid = ForensicPackagerService.verifyPackageIntegrity(tampered);
    expect(isValid).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`tests/friendbot-wallet.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { FriendbotService } from '../src/services/friendbot.service';
import { WalletBalanceTrackerService } from '../src/services/wallet-balance-tracker.service';
import { TransactionStatusPollerService } from '../src/services/transaction-poller.service';

describe('Friendbot and Wallet Services', () => {
  describe('FriendbotService', () => {
    it('validates stellar G-address format', async () => {
      const friendbot = new FriendbotService();
      const invalidRes = await friendbot.fundAccount({ destination: 'invalid_address' });

      expect(invalidRes.success).toBe(false);
      expect(invalidRes.error).toContain('Invalid Stellar public key format');
    });

    it('funds valid address using friendbot API', async () => {
      const friendbot = new FriendbotService();
      const validAddress = 'GBZXN7PIRZGNMHGA7MUUUF4GWPY5AYPV6LY4UV2GL6VJGIQRXFDNMADI';

      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        json: async () => ({ hash: 'tx_fb_hash_123' }),
      } as any);

      try {
        const res = await friendbot.fundAccount({ destination: validAddress });
        expect(res.success).toBe(true);
        expect(res.txHash).toBe('tx_fb_hash_123');
        expect(res.destination).toBe(validAddress);
      } finally {
        global.fetch = originalFetch;
      }
    });
  });

  describe('WalletBalanceTrackerService', () => {
    it('returns empty balance for non-existent accounts', async () => {
      const tracker = new WalletBalanceTrackerService('http://localhost:8000');
      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 404,
      } as any);

      try {
        const res = await tracker.getBalances('G_NON_EXISTENT_ACCOUNT');
        expect(res.exists).toBe(false);
        expect(res.nativeBalanceXlm).toBe('0');
        expect(res.balances).toHaveLength(0);
      } finally {
        global.fetch = originalFetch;
      }
    });

    it('parses native and custom asset balances from Horizon', async () => {
      const tracker = new WalletBalanceTrackerService('http://localhost:8000');
      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        json: async () => ({
          balances: [
            { asset_type: 'native', balance: '125.5000000' },
            { asset_type: 'credit_alphanum4', asset_code: 'USDC', balance: '50.0000000', asset_issuer: 'GISSUER' },
          ],
        }),
      } as any);

      try {
        const res = await tracker.getBalances('G_VALID_ACCOUNT');
        expect(res.exists).toBe(true);
        expect(res.nativeBalanceXlm).toBe('125.5000000');
        expect(res.balances).toHaveLength(2);
        expect(res.balances[0].isNative).toBe(true);
        expect(res.balances[1].assetCode).toBe('USDC');
      } finally {
        global.fetch = originalFetch;
      }
    });
  });

  describe('TransactionStatusPollerService', () => {
    it('returns not found status when transaction is missing', async () => {
      const poller = new TransactionStatusPollerService('http://localhost:8000');
      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 404,
      } as any);

      try {
        const res = await poller.getStatus('hash_not_found');
        expect(res.status).toBe('NOT_FOUND');
      } finally {
        global.fetch = originalFetch;
      }
    });

    it('returns success status for confirmed transaction', async () => {
      const poller = new TransactionStatusPollerService('http://localhost:8000');
      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        json: async () => ({
          result: {
            status: 'SUCCESS',
            latestLedger: 10452,
            createdAt: '2026-10-29T08:00:00Z',
          },
        }),
      } as any);

      try {
        const res = await poller.getStatus('valid_hash_123');
        expect(res.status).toBe('SUCCESS');
        expect(res.ledger).toBe(10452);
      } finally {
        global.fetch = originalFetch;
      }
    });
  });
});
```

`tests/gas-price-cache.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { GasPriceCacheService } from '../src/services/gas-price-cache.service';

describe('GasPriceCacheService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    GasPriceCacheService.reset();
  });

  it('populates cache on first call and records a cache miss', () => {
    const now = 1000000;
    const est = GasPriceCacheService.getEstimate('FUND_PRIZE_POOL', 60000, now);

    expect(est.operation).toBe('FUND_PRIZE_POOL');
    expect(est.estimatedFeeStroops).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    const stats = GasPriceCacheService.getStats();
    expect(stats.misses).toBe(1);
    expect(stats.hits).toBe(0);
    expect(stats.entriesCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it('serves subsequent calls from cache within TTL recording cache hits', () => {
    const now = 1000000;
    GasPriceCacheService.getEstimate('PAY_ENTRY_FEE', 60000, now);

    // Call 30 seconds later (within 60s TTL)
    const cachedEst = GasPriceCacheService.getEstimate('PAY_ENTRY_FEE', 60000, now + 30000);
    expect(cachedEst.operation).toBe('PAY_ENTRY_FEE');

    const stats = GasPriceCacheService.getStats();
    expect(stats.hits).toBe(1);
    expect(stats.misses).toBe(1);
  });

  it('refreshes expired entries past TTL', () => {
    const now = 1000000;
    GasPriceCacheService.getEstimate('EXECUTE_SETTLEMENT', 60000, now);

    // Call 70 seconds later (expired)
    GasPriceCacheService.getEstimate('EXECUTE_SETTLEMENT', 60000, now + 70000);

    const stats = GasPriceCacheService.getStats();
    expect(stats.misses).toBe(2);
    expect(stats.hits).toBe(0);
  });

  it('invalidates cache entries on demand', () => {
    GasPriceCacheService.getEstimate('CREATE_TOURNAMENT');
    expect(GasPriceCacheService.getStats().entriesCount).toBe(1);

    GasPriceCacheService.invalidate('CREATE_TOURNAMENT');
    expect(GasPriceCacheService.getStats().entriesCount).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`tests/gas-price-estimator.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { GasPriceEstimatorService } from '../src/services/gas-price-estimator.service';

describe('GasPriceEstimatorService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    GasPriceEstimatorService.setCongestionLevel('NORMAL');
  });

  it('estimates fee accurately for normal congestion', () => {
    const est = GasPriceEstimatorService.estimateFee('FUND_PRIZE_POOL');
    // Base 120,000 * 1.2 = 144,000 Stroops
    expect(est.estimatedFeeStroops).toBe(144_000);
    expect(est.estimatedFeeXlm).toBe(0.0144);
    expect(est.cpuInstructions).toBe(1_500_000);
    expect(est.inclusionPriority).toBe('Standard');
  });

  it('scales fee dynamically during extreme congestion', () => {
    const est = GasPriceEstimatorService.estimateFee('EXECUTE_SETTLEMENT', 'EXTREME');
    // Base 250,000 * 2.5 = 625,000 Stroops
    expect(est.estimatedFeeStroops).toBe(625_000);
    expect(est.inclusionPriority).toBe('Critical');
  });

  it('maintains global congestion state', () => {
    GasPriceEstimatorService.setCongestionLevel('HIGH');
    expect(GasPriceEstimatorService.getCongestionLevel()).toBe('HIGH');

    const est = GasPriceEstimatorService.estimateFee('REGISTER_PARTICIPANT');
    // Base 75,000 * 1.8 = 135,000 Stroops
    expect(est.estimatedFeeStroops).toBe(135_000);
    expect(est.inclusionPriority).toBe('Expedited');
  });
});
```

`tests/health-probe.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { HealthProbeService } from '../src/services/health-probe.service';

describe('HealthProbeService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    HealthProbeService.setReady(true);
    HealthProbeService.setHorizonReachable(true);
  });

  it('reports healthy liveness probe with status UP and 200', () => {
    const liveness = HealthProbeService.checkLiveness();
    expect(liveness.status).toBe('UP');
    expect(liveness.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(liveness.checks.find((c) => c.name === 'process')?.status).toBe('UP');
    expect(liveness.uptimeSeconds).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
  });

  it('reports healthy readiness probe by default', () => {
    const readiness = HealthProbeService.checkReadiness();
    expect(readiness.status).toBe('UP');
    expect(readiness.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(readiness.checks.length).toBe(2);
  });

  it('returns status DOWN and 503 when readiness is toggled off', () => {
    HealthProbeService.setReady(false);

    const readiness = HealthProbeService.checkReadiness();
    expect(readiness.status).toBe('DOWN');
    expect(readiness.statusCode).toBe(503);
    expect(readiness.checks.find((c) => c.name === 'engine_state')?.status).toBe('DOWN');
  });

  it('returns status DOWN and 503 when Horizon is unreachable', () => {
    HealthProbeService.setHorizonReachable(false);

    const readiness = HealthProbeService.checkReadiness();
    expect(readiness.status).toBe('DOWN');
    expect(readiness.statusCode).toBe(503);
    expect(readiness.checks.find((c) => c.name === 'horizon_connectivity')?.status).toBe('DOWN');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  LeaderboardExportService,
  LeaderboardExportRow,
} from '../src/services/leaderboard-export.service';

describe('LeaderboardExportService', () => {
  const mockEntries: LeaderboardExportRow[] = [
    {
      rank: 1,
      playerWallet: 'GBBD47IF6LWK7P7MDEVSCWR7DPUWV3NY3DTQEVFL4NAT4AQH3ZLLFLA5',
      score: 1500,
      prizeShareBps: 6000,
      prizeAmount: 60.0,
      asset: 'XLM',
      status: 'SETTLED',
    },
    {
      rank: 2,
      playerWallet: 'GA7QYNF7SOWQ3GLR2BGMZEHXAVIRZA4KVWLTJJFC7MGXUA74P7UJVWAC',
      score: 1100,
      prizeShareBps: 4000,
      prizeAmount: 40.0,
      asset: 'XLM',
      status: 'SETTLED',
    },
  ];

    const csv = LeaderboardExportService.exportCsv('tourney-1', mockEntries);

    expect(csv).toContain('Rank,Player Wallet,Score,Prize Share (Bps),Prize Amount,Asset,Status');
    expect(csv).toContain('1,"GBBD47IF6LWK7P7MDEVSCWR7DPUWV3NY3DTQEVFL4NAT4AQH3ZLLFLA5",1500,6000,60.0000,"XLM","SETTLED"');

    const parsed = LeaderboardExportService.parseCsv(csv);
    expect(parsed.length).toBe(2);
    expect(parsed[0].rank).toBe(1);
    expect(parsed[0].score).toBe(1500);
    expect(parsed[0].prizeAmount).toBe(60.0);
  });

  it('exports structured JSON with metadata', () => {
    const jsonStr = LeaderboardExportService.exportJson(
      'tourney-1',
      'Weekend Splash',
      'XLM',
      100,
      mockEntries,
      1700000000
    );

    const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonStr);
    expect(parsed.tournamentId).toBe('tourney-1');
    expect(parsed.title).toBe('Weekend Splash');
    expect(parsed.totalPrizePool).toBe(100);
    expect(parsed.exportedAt).toBe(1700000000);
    expect(parsed.recordCount).toBe(2);
    expect(parsed.rankings[0].playerWallet).toBe('GBBD47IF6LWK7P7MDEVSCWR7DPUWV3NY3DTQEVFL4NAT4AQH3ZLLFLA5');
  });
});
```

`tests/ledger-sync.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { LedgerSequenceSyncService } from '../src/services/ledger-sync.service';

describe('LedgerSequenceSyncService', () => {
  it('detects synchronized ledgers when lag is within tolerance', async () => {
    const service = new LedgerSequenceSyncService('http://horizon.test', 'http://soroban.test');

    vi.spyOn(service, 'fetchHorizonLedger').mockResolvedValue(10050);
    vi.spyOn(service, 'fetchSorobanLedger').mockResolvedValue(10049);

    const status = await service.checkSync();
    expect(status.isSynced).toBe(true);
    expect(status.sequenceLag).toBe(1);
    expect(status.warning).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('flags desynchronization when lag exceeds threshold', async () => {
    const service = new LedgerSequenceSyncService('http://horizon.test', 'http://soroban.test');

    vi.spyOn(service, 'fetchHorizonLedger').mockResolvedValue(10050);
    vi.spyOn(service, 'fetchSorobanLedger').mockResolvedValue(10040); // 10 ledgers behind

    const status = await service.checkSync();
    expect(status.isSynced).toBe(false);
    expect(status.sequenceLag).toBe(10);
    expect(status.warning).toContain('exceeds maximum allowed lag');
  });

  it('handles unreachable ledger providers gracefully', async () => {
    const service = new LedgerSequenceSyncService('http://horizon.test', 'http://soroban.test');

    vi.spyOn(service, 'fetchHorizonLedger').mockResolvedValue(0);
    vi.spyOn(service, 'fetchSorobanLedger').mockResolvedValue(10050);

    const status = await service.checkSync();
    expect(status.isSynced).toBe(false);
    expect(status.warning).toContain('Failed to fetch ledger sequence');
  });
});
```

`tests/match-attestation.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { MatchAttestationService } from '../src/services/match-attestation.service';

describe('MatchAttestationService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    MatchAttestationService.reset();
  });

  it('creates pending attestation with deterministic digest', () => {
    const attest = MatchAttestationService.createAttestation(
      'match-1',
      'tourney-1',
      'GBBD...1',
      1200,
      'hash_123'
    );

    expect(attest.status).toBe('PENDING');
    expect(attest.attestationId).toMatch(/^attest-[0-9a-f]{16}$/);
    expect(attest.attestationDigest).toHaveLength(64);
  });

  it('transitions through ATTESTED, DISPUTED, and SETTLED states', () => {
    const attest = MatchAttestationService.createAttestation(
      'match-2',
      'tourney-2',
      'GBBD...2',
      950,
      'hash_456'
    );

    const attested = MatchAttestationService.markAttested(attest.attestationId, 'tx_abc', 104523);
    expect(attested?.status).toBe('ATTESTED');
    expect(attested?.txHash).toBe('tx_abc');
    expect(attested?.ledger).toBe(104523);

    const disputed = MatchAttestationService.markDisputed(attest.attestationId, 'Suspicious tick anomaly');
    expect(disputed?.status).toBe('DISPUTED');
    expect(disputed?.disputeReason).toBe('Suspicious tick anomaly');

    const settled = MatchAttestationService.markSettled(attest.attestationId);
    expect(settled?.status).toBe('SETTLED');
  });

  it('retrieves all attestations for a specific tournament', () => {
    MatchAttestationService.createAttestation('m-1', 'tourn-99', 'P1', 100, 'h1');
    MatchAttestationService.createAttestation('m-2', 'tourn-99', 'P2', 200, 'h2');
    MatchAttestationService.createAttestation('m-3', 'other-tourn', 'P3', 300, 'h3');

    const list = MatchAttestationService.getAttestationsForTournament('tourn-99');
    expect(list.length).toBe(2);
  });
});
```

`tests/match-replay-audit.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { MatchReplayAuditService } from '../src/services/match-replay-audit.service';
import { MatchSession } from '../src/types';

describe('MatchReplayAuditService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
  });

  it('generates a valid diagnostic audit package for a match', () => {
    const session: MatchSession = {
      matchId: 'match-audit-101',
      tournamentId: 'tourn-aud-1',
      playerWallet: 'GA_AUDIT_PLAYER',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      sessionToken: 'token-aud-1',
      startTime: 1000,
      endTime: 2000,
      status: 'COMPLETED',
      events: [
        { seq: 1, timestamp: 1100, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 100, combo: 1, multiplier: 1 },
        { seq: 2, timestamp: 1250, type: 'TARGET_HIT', points: 200, combo: 2, multiplier: 1.5 },
      ],
    };

    StoreService.createMatchSession(session);

    const pkg = MatchReplayAuditService.generatePackage('match-audit-101', 5000);

    expect(pkg.matchId).toBe('match-audit-101');
    expect(pkg.tournamentId).toBe('tourn-aud-1');
    expect(pkg.anomalyAnalysis.hasAnomalies).toBe(false);
    expect(pkg.packageChecksum).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);

    expect(MatchReplayAuditService.verifyPackageChecksum(pkg)).toBe(true);

    // Tampered payload fails checksum
    const tampered = { ...pkg, tournamentId: 'tourn-tampered' };
    expect(MatchReplayAuditService.verifyPackageChecksum(tampered)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`tests/match-tick-anomaly.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  MatchTickAnomalyService,
  MatchTickItem,
} from '../src/services/match-tick-anomaly.service';

describe('MatchTickAnomalyService', () => {
  it('reports no anomalies for clean sequential ticks', () => {
    const cleanTicks: MatchTickItem[] = [
      { tickIndex: 0, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE', payload: { x: 10, y: 10 }, timestamp: 100 },
      { tickIndex: 1, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE', payload: { x: 20, y: 15 }, timestamp: 200 },
      { tickIndex: 2, playerIndex: 0, action: 'FIRE_WATER_BALLOON', timestamp: 400 },
    ];

    const report = MatchTickAnomalyService.scanTicks(cleanTicks, 1000);
    expect(report.hasAnomalies).toBe(false);
    expect(report.anomalyCount).toBe(0);
  });

  it('detects rapid fire violations', () => {
    const rapidTicks: MatchTickItem[] = [
      { tickIndex: 0, playerIndex: 0, action: 'FIRE_WATER_BALLOON', timestamp: 100 },
      { tickIndex: 1, playerIndex: 0, action: 'FIRE_WATER_BALLOON', timestamp: 150 }, // only 50ms gap
    ];

    const report = MatchTickAnomalyService.scanTicks(rapidTicks, 1000);
    expect(report.hasAnomalies).toBe(true);
    expect(report.anomalies.find((a) => a.rule === 'RAPID_FIRE')).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('detects velocity spikes (teleportation)', () => {
    const teleportTicks: MatchTickItem[] = [
      { tickIndex: 0, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE', payload: { x: 10, y: 10 }, timestamp: 100 },
      { tickIndex: 1, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE', payload: { x: 500, y: 500 }, timestamp: 150 },
    ];

    const report = MatchTickAnomalyService.scanTicks(teleportTicks, 1000);
    expect(report.hasAnomalies).toBe(true);
    expect(report.anomalies.find((a) => a.rule === 'VELOCITY_SPIKE')).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('detects non-sequential tick indices', () => {
    const outOfOrderTicks: MatchTickItem[] = [
      { tickIndex: 5, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE', timestamp: 100 },
      { tickIndex: 2, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE', timestamp: 150 },
    ];

    const report = MatchTickAnomalyService.scanTicks(outOfOrderTicks, 1000);
    expect(report.hasAnomalies).toBe(true);
    expect(report.anomalies.find((a) => a.rule === 'INVALID_TICK_ORDER')).toBeDefined();
  });
});
```

`tests/player-leaderboard-cache.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import {
  PlayerLeaderboardCacheService,
  CachedLeaderboardEntry,
} from '../src/services/player-leaderboard-cache.service';

describe('PlayerLeaderboardCacheService', () => {
  const entries: CachedLeaderboardEntry[] = [
    { rank: 1, playerWallet: 'GBBD...1', score: 1000, updatedAt: 100 },
    { rank: 2, playerWallet: 'GBBD...2', score: 850, updatedAt: 100 },
  ];

  beforeEach(() => {
    PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.clearAll();
  });

  it('sets and retrieves cached leaderboard entries within TTL', () => {
    PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.setLeaderboard('tourney-1', entries, 10_000, 1000);

    const cached = PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.getLeaderboard('tourney-1', 5000);
    expect(cached).toBeDefined();
    expect(cached?.length).toBe(2);
    expect(cached?.[0].playerWallet).toBe('GBBD...1');
  });

  it('expires cached leaderboard when TTL elapses', () => {
    PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.setLeaderboard('tourney-2', entries, 5_000, 1000);

    const expired = PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.getLeaderboard('tourney-2', 7000);
    expect(expired).toBeNull();
  });

  it('invalidates cache on explicit demand', () => {
    PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.setLeaderboard('tourney-3', entries, 10_000, 1000);
    expect(PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.getLeaderboard('tourney-3', 2000)).not.toBeNull();

    const invalidated = PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.invalidate('tourney-3');
    expect(invalidated).toBe(true);
    expect(PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.getLeaderboard('tourney-3', 2000)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('prunes expired entries accurately', () => {
    PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.setLeaderboard('t-exp', entries, 2_000, 1000);
    const pruned = PlayerLeaderboardCacheService.pruneExpired(4000);
    expect(pruned).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { PlayerProfileService } from '../src/services/player-profile.service';

describe('PlayerProfileService', () => {

    PlayerProfileService.reset();

    expect(profile.lastActiveAt).toBe(2000);

    PlayerProfileService.recordMatchResult(wallet, 't-2', 200, 3, 10, 2000);
    PlayerProfileService.recordMatchResult(wallet, 't-3', 300, 1, 100, 3000);

    expect(history[1].tournamentId).toBe('t-2');
```

`tests/preflight.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { SorobanPreflightService } from '../src/services/preflight.service';

describe('SorobanPreflightService', () => {
  it('rejects invalid or empty transaction XDR', async () => {
    const service = new SorobanPreflightService('http://localhost:8000');
    const result = await service.simulate({ transactionXdr: '' });

    expect(result.success).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error).toContain('Invalid or empty transaction XDR');
  });

  it('handles successful simulation responses', async () => {
    const service = new SorobanPreflightService('http://localhost:8000');
    // Mock global fetch
    const originalFetch = global.fetch;
    global.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      json: async () => ({
        result: {
          minResourceFee: '150000',
          cost: {
            cpuInsns: '1200000',
            memBytes: '64000',
          },
          events: [{}, {}],
        },
      }),
    } as any);

    try {
      const result = await service.simulate({ transactionXdr: 'AAAA_VALID_XDR' });
      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.minResourceFeeStroops).toBe('150000');
      expect(result.cpuInstructions).toBe(1200000);
      expect(result.memoryBytes).toBe(64000);
      expect(result.eventsCount).toBe(2);
      expect(result.costEstimateXlm).toBe('0.0150000');
    } finally {
      global.fetch = originalFetch;
    }
  });

  it('handles RPC simulation failure errors', async () => {
    const service = new SorobanPreflightService('http://localhost:8000');
    const originalFetch = global.fetch;
    global.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      json: async () => ({
        result: {
          error: 'HostError: Error(Contract, #12)',
        },
      }),
    } as any);

    try {
      const result = await service.simulate({ transactionXdr: 'AAAA_ERROR_XDR' });
      expect(result.success).toBe(false);
      expect(result.error).toBe('HostError: Error(Contract, #12)');
    } finally {
      global.fetch = originalFetch;
    }
  });
});
```

`tests/rate-limit-bypass.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import { rateLimitBypassMiddleware } from '../src/middleware/rate-limit-bypass.middleware';

describe('rateLimitBypassMiddleware', () => {
  it('sets isRateLimitBypassed = true when valid token is supplied', () => {
    const req = {
      headers: { 'x-rate-limit-bypass-token': 'valid-secret-token-xyz' },
    } as unknown as Request;

    const res = {} as Response;
    const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

    const middleware = rateLimitBypassMiddleware({
      bypassTokens: ['valid-secret-token-xyz'],
    });

    middleware(req, res, next);

    expect(req.isRateLimitBypassed).toBe(true);
    expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('sets isRateLimitBypassed = false when token is invalid or missing', () => {
    const reqMissing = { headers: {} } as unknown as Request;
    const reqInvalid = {
      headers: { 'x-rate-limit-bypass-token': 'wrong-token' },
    } as unknown as Request;

    const res = {} as Response;
    const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

    const middleware = rateLimitBypassMiddleware({
      bypassTokens: ['valid-secret-token-xyz'],
    });

    middleware(reqMissing, res, next);
    expect(reqMissing.isRateLimitBypassed).toBe(false);

    middleware(reqInvalid, res, next);
    expect(reqInvalid.isRateLimitBypassed).toBe(false);
  });

  it('does not allow hardcoded dev bypass token in production mode', () => {
    const originalEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;
    try {
      process.env.NODE_ENV = 'production';
      delete process.env.RATE_LIMIT_BYPASS_TOKEN;

      const req = {
        headers: { 'x-rate-limit-bypass-token': 'relay-daemon-bypass-token-stellar-splash-2026' },
      } as unknown as Request;

      const res = {} as Response;
      const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

      const middleware = rateLimitBypassMiddleware();
      middleware(req, res, next);

      expect(req.isRateLimitBypassed).toBe(false);
      expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    } finally {
      process.env.NODE_ENV = originalEnv;
    }
  });
});
```

`tests/rate-limit-tier.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { RateLimitTierService } from '../src/services/rate-limit-tier.service';

describe('RateLimitTierService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    RateLimitTierService.resetAll();
  });

  it('allows requests within anonymous tier threshold and tracks remaining', () => {
    const id = '192.168.1.1';
    const now = 1_000_000;

    const res1 = RateLimitTierService.recordRequest(id, 'ANONYMOUS', now);
    expect(res1.allowed).toBe(true);
    expect(res1.remaining).toBe(29);
    expect(res1.limit).toBe(30);

    const check = RateLimitTierService.checkLimit(id, 'ANONYMOUS', now);
    expect(check.remaining).toBe(29);
  });

  it('rejects requests when rate limit is exceeded', () => {
    const id = '192.168.1.2';
    const now = 1_000_000;

    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
      RateLimitTierService.recordRequest(id, 'ANONYMOUS', now + i * 10);
    }

    const blocked = RateLimitTierService.recordRequest(id, 'ANONYMOUS', now + 500);
    expect(blocked.allowed).toBe(false);
    expect(blocked.remaining).toBe(0);
    expect(blocked.resetAfterMs).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('supports higher limits for player and creator tiers', () => {
    const player = 'GBBD...PLAYER';
    const now = 2_000_000;

    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
      RateLimitTierService.recordRequest(player, 'PLAYER', now + i * 10);
    }

    const check = RateLimitTierService.checkLimit(player, 'PLAYER', now + 600);
    expect(check.allowed).toBe(true);
    expect(check.remaining).toBe(70); // 120 - 50 = 70
  });

  it('slides window forward after windowMs elapsed', () => {
    const id = '192.168.1.3';
    const t0 = 100_000;

    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
      RateLimitTierService.recordRequest(id, 'ANONYMOUS', t0);
    }

    // Immediately blocked
    expect(RateLimitTierService.checkLimit(id, 'ANONYMOUS', t0).allowed).toBe(false);

    // 61 seconds later
    const t1 = t0 + 61_000;
    const afterWindow = RateLimitTierService.recordRequest(id, 'ANONYMOUS', t1);
    expect(afterWindow.allowed).toBe(true);
    expect(afterWindow.remaining).toBe(29);
  });

  it('prunes old window records', () => {
    const id = '192.168.1.4';
    RateLimitTierService.recordRequest(id, 'ANONYMOUS', 1000);

    const pruned = RateLimitTierService.pruneOldWindows(70_000);
    expect(pruned).toBe(1);
  });
});

```

`tests/replay-bundle-validation.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  ReplayBundleValidationService,
  ReplayBundle,
  ReplayTick,
} from '../src/services/replay-bundle-validation.service';

describe('ReplayBundleValidationService', () => {
  const matchId = 'match-101';
  const tournamentId = 'tourney-42';
  const players = ['GBBD...1', 'GBBD...2'];
  const ticks: ReplayTick[] = [
    { tickIndex: 0, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE_UP', timestamp: 100 },
    { tickIndex: 1, playerIndex: 1, action: 'FIRE_WATER_BALLOON', timestamp: 110 },
  ];

  it('validates a correct bundle with computed hash', () => {
    const stateRoot = ReplayBundleValidationService.computeStateRoot(matchId, tournamentId, players, ticks);
    const bundle: ReplayBundle = {
      version: '1.0.0',
      matchId,
      tournamentId,
      players,
      finalScores: { 'GBBD...1': 100, 'GBBD...2': 80 },
      stateRootSha256: stateRoot,
      ticks,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };

    const res = ReplayBundleValidationService.validate(bundle, true);
    expect(res.isValid).toBe(true);
    expect(res.bundle).toBeDefined();
    expect(res.error).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('fails validation on hash mismatch', () => {
    const bundle: ReplayBundle = {
      version: '1.0.0',
      matchId,
      tournamentId,
      players,
      finalScores: { 'GBBD...1': 100 },
      stateRootSha256: '0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef',
      ticks,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };

    const res = ReplayBundleValidationService.validate(bundle, true);
    expect(res.isValid).toBe(false);
    expect(res.error).toContain('State root hash mismatch');
  });

  it('rejects non-sequential tick indexes', () => {
    const invalidTicks: ReplayTick[] = [
      { tickIndex: 0, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE', timestamp: 100 },
      { tickIndex: 5, playerIndex: 0, action: 'MOVE', timestamp: 110 },
    ];

    const bundle = {
      version: '1.0.0',
      matchId,
      tournamentId,
      players,
      finalScores: {},
      stateRootSha256: '0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef',
      ticks: invalidTicks,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };

    const res = ReplayBundleValidationService.validate(bundle, false);
    expect(res.isValid).toBe(false);
    expect(res.error).toContain('Invalid tick index at position 1');
  });

  it('rejects invalid hex hash format', () => {
    const bundle = {
      version: '1.0.0',
      matchId,
      tournamentId,
      players,
      finalScores: {},
      stateRootSha256: 'not-a-valid-hex-hash',
      ticks,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };

    const res = ReplayBundleValidationService.validate(bundle, false);
    expect(res.isValid).toBe(false);
    expect(res.error).toContain('64-character hexadecimal SHA-256 hash');
  });
});
```

`tests/security-headers.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import { securityHeadersMiddleware } from '../src/middleware/security-headers.middleware';

describe('securityHeadersMiddleware', () => {
  it('sets standard OWASP security headers on outbound responses', () => {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    const req = {} as Request;
    const res = {
      setHeader: vi.fn((key: string, val: string) => {
        headers[key.toLowerCase()] = val;
      }),
    } as unknown as Response;
    const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

    const middleware = securityHeadersMiddleware();
    middleware(req, res, next);

    expect(headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(headers['x-frame-options']).toBe('DENY');
    expect(headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('strict-origin-when-cross-origin');
    expect(headers['strict-transport-security']).toContain('max-age=31536000');
    expect(headers['content-security-policy']).toBe("default-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none';");
    expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('respects configuration flags for disabling specific headers', () => {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    const req = {} as Request;
    const res = {
      setHeader: vi.fn((key: string, val: string) => {
        headers[key.toLowerCase()] = val;
      }),
    } as unknown as Response;
    const next = vi.fn() as NextFunction;

    const middleware = securityHeadersMiddleware({
      enableHsts: false,
      enableCsp: false,
      enableFrameGuard: false,
    });
    middleware(req, res, next);

    expect(headers['x-frame-options']).toBeUndefined();
    expect(headers['strict-transport-security']).toBeUndefined();
    expect(headers['content-security-policy']).toBeUndefined();
    expect(headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(next).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});
```

`tests/session-token.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { SessionTokenService } from '../src/services/session-token.service';

describe('SessionTokenService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    SessionTokenService.reset();
  });

  it('issues valid single-use tokens', () => {
    const session = SessionTokenService.issueToken('m-1', 't-1', 'GBBD...1');
    expect(session.token).toHaveLength(48); // 24 bytes hex
    expect(session.consumed).toBe(false);

    expect(SessionTokenService.isTokenValid(session.token)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('consumes token atomically and prevents reuse', () => {
    const session = SessionTokenService.issueToken('m-2', 't-2', 'GBBD...2');

    // First consumption succeeds
    const ok = SessionTokenService.verifyAndConsumeToken(session.token, 'm-2', 'GBBD...2');
    expect(ok).toBe(true);
    expect(SessionTokenService.isTokenValid(session.token)).toBe(false);

    // Second consumption rejected
    const replay = SessionTokenService.verifyAndConsumeToken(session.token, 'm-2', 'GBBD...2');
    expect(replay).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects consumption with mismatched matchId or playerWallet', () => {
    const session = SessionTokenService.issueToken('m-3', 't-3', 'GBBD...3');

    const wrongPlayer = SessionTokenService.verifyAndConsumeToken(
      session.token,
      'm-3',
      'WRONG_WALLET'
    );
    expect(wrongPlayer).toBe(false);

    const wrongMatch = SessionTokenService.verifyAndConsumeToken(
      session.token,
      'WRONG_MATCH',
      'GBBD...3'
    );
    expect(wrongMatch).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects expired tokens and prunes them', () => {
    const session = SessionTokenService.issueToken('m-4', 't-4', 'GBBD...4', 5_000, 1000);

    expect(SessionTokenService.isTokenValid(session.token, 7000)).toBe(false);

    const pruned = SessionTokenService.pruneExpired(7000);
    expect(pruned).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

`tests/settlement-daemon.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { SettlementDaemonService } from '../src/services/settlement-daemon.service';

describe('SettlementDaemonService', () => {
  let daemon: SettlementDaemonService;

  beforeEach(() => {
    daemon = new SettlementDaemonService();
  });

  it('registers completed tournament with dispute window', () => {
    const job = daemon.registerCompletedTournament(42, 1000, 3600_000);
    expect(job.tournamentId).toBe(42);
    expect(job.status).toBe('PENDING_DISPUTE');
    expect(job.disputeWindowExpiresAt).toBe(1000 + 3600_000);

    const stats = daemon.getStats();
    expect(stats.activeJobsCount).toBe(1);
    expect(stats.settledCount).toBe(0);
  });

  it('executes settlement when dispute window has elapsed', async () => {
    // Window ended 1000ms ago
    const endedAt = Date.now() - 5000;
    const windowDuration = 1000;
    daemon.registerCompletedTournament(43, endedAt, windowDuration);

    const settled = await daemon.evaluatePendingJobs();
    expect(settled).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(settled[0].status).toBe('SETTLED');
    expect(settled[0].txHash).toBeDefined();

    const stats = daemon.getStats();
    expect(stats.settledCount).toBe(1);
    expect(stats.activeJobsCount).toBe(0);
  });

  it('halts settlement when tournament is disputed', async () => {
    daemon.registerCompletedTournament(44, Date.now() - 5000, 1000);
    daemon.markDisputed(44, 'Contested winner rank');

    const job = daemon.getJob(44);
    expect(job?.status).toBe('DISPUTED');
    expect(job?.error).toContain('Contested winner');

    const settled = await daemon.evaluatePendingJobs();
    expect(settled).toHaveLength(0);

    const stats = daemon.getStats();
    expect(stats.disputedCount).toBe(1);
    expect(stats.settledCount).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`tests/solvency-monitor.worker.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { SolvencyMonitorWorker } from '../src/services/solvency-monitor.worker';
import { Tournament } from '../src/types';

describe('SolvencyMonitorWorker', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.reset();
    SolvencyMonitorWorker.stop();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    SolvencyMonitorWorker.stop();
  });

  it('evaluates fully solvent vault when balance meets or exceeds obligations', () => {
    const t: Tournament = {
      id: 'tourn-solv-1',
      name: 'Solvency Test Cup',
      description: 'Testing solvency',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      creatorWallet: 'GA_CREATOR',
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 500,
      vaultAddress: 'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
      maxParticipants: 16,
      currentParticipants: 4,
      startTime: 1000,
      endTime: 10000,
      state: 'OPEN',
      createdAt: 500,
      updatedAt: 500,
    };

    StoreService.saveTournament(t);

    const result = SolvencyMonitorWorker.checkSolvency(600);

    expect(result.isSolvent).toBe(true);
    expect(result.vaultBalance).toBe(600);
    expect(result.totalObligations).toBe(500);
    expect(result.ratioPct).toBe(120);
    expect(result.deficitAmount).toBe(0);
  });

  it('detects vault deficit and logs audit event when balance is insufficient', () => {
    const t: Tournament = {
      id: 'tourn-solv-2',
      name: 'Deficit Cup',
      description: 'Testing deficit',
      gameId: 'splash-rush',
      gameVersion: '1.0.0',
      creatorWallet: 'GA_CREATOR',
      prizeAsset: 'USDC',
      prizePoolAmount: 1000,
      vaultAddress: 'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
      maxParticipants: 16,
      currentParticipants: 8,
      startTime: 1000,
      endTime: 10000,
      state: 'OPEN',
      createdAt: 500,
      updatedAt: 500,
    };

    StoreService.saveTournament(t);

    // Simulated balance is only 800 USDC (deficit of 200 USDC)
    const result = SolvencyMonitorWorker.checkSolvency(800);

    expect(result.isSolvent).toBe(false);
    expect(result.deficitAmount).toBe(200);
    expect(result.ratioPct).toBe(80);

    const auditLogs = StoreService.getAuditLogs();
    expect(auditLogs.some((l) => l.eventType === 'VAULT_DEFICIT_DETECTED')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('starts and stops worker timer without error', () => {
    SolvencyMonitorWorker.start(10000);
    SolvencyMonitorWorker.stop();
  });
});
```

`tests/sse-heartbeat.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Response } from 'express';
import { SseHeartbeatService } from '../src/services/sse-heartbeat.service';

describe('SseHeartbeatService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    SseHeartbeatService.reset();
  });

  it('registers and unregisters SSE client connections', () => {
    const mockRes = {
      writableEnded: false,
      closed: false,
      write: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as Response;

    const session = SseHeartbeatService.registerClient('conn-1', mockRes, '127.0.0.1');
    expect(session.id).toBe('conn-1');
    expect(session.pingsSent).toBe(0);

    let metrics = SseHeartbeatService.getMetrics();
    expect(metrics.activeConnections).toBe(1);

    const unregistered = SseHeartbeatService.unregisterClient('conn-1');
    expect(unregistered).toBe(true);

    metrics = SseHeartbeatService.getMetrics();
    expect(metrics.activeConnections).toBe(0);
  });

  it('dispatches keepalive comments to active clients', () => {
    const writeMock = vi.fn();
    const mockRes = {
      writableEnded: false,
      closed: false,
      write: writeMock,
    } as unknown as Response;

    SseHeartbeatService.registerClient('conn-1', mockRes);
    SseHeartbeatService.registerClient('conn-2', mockRes);

    const sent = SseHeartbeatService.dispatchHeartbeat();
    expect(sent).toBe(2);
    expect(writeMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);

    const metrics = SseHeartbeatService.getMetrics();
    expect(metrics.totalPingsSent).toBe(2);
  });

  it('prunes stale or closed client sockets during dispatch', () => {
    const activeWrite = vi.fn();
    const activeRes = {
      writableEnded: false,
      closed: false,
      write: activeWrite,
    } as unknown as Response;

    const closedRes = {
      writableEnded: true,
      closed: true,
      write: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as Response;

    SseHeartbeatService.registerClient('active-conn', activeRes);
    SseHeartbeatService.registerClient('closed-conn', closedRes);

    const sent = SseHeartbeatService.dispatchHeartbeat();
    expect(sent).toBe(1);

    const metrics = SseHeartbeatService.getMetrics();
    expect(metrics.activeConnections).toBe(1);
    expect(metrics.staleConnectionsPruned).toBe(1);
  });
});

```

`tests/synthetic-load.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

interface SyntheticPlayer {
  playerId: string;
  wallet: string;
  score: number;
  mouseMovementsCount: number;
  clicksCount: number;
}

function generateSyntheticPlayer(idx: number): SyntheticPlayer {
  return {
    playerId: `SYNTH_PLAYER_${idx.toString().padStart(3, '0')}`,
    wallet: `GBZX${idx.toString().padStart(48, '0')}TEST`,
    score: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5000) + 500,
    mouseMovementsCount: Math.floor(Math.random() * 80) + 40,
    clicksCount: Math.floor(Math.random() * 30) + 10,
  };
}

describe('Synthetic Load Generator', () => {
  it('generates 100 correctly formatted player telemetry instances', () => {
    const players = Array.from({ length: 100 }, (_, i) => generateSyntheticPlayer(i + 1));
    expect(players).toHaveLength(100);

    const first = players[0];
    expect(first.playerId).toBe('SYNTH_PLAYER_001');
    expect(first.wallet.startsWith('GBZX')).toBe(true);
    expect(first.wallet).toHaveLength(56);
    expect(first.score).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(500);
    expect(first.mouseMovementsCount).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(40);
  });
});
```

`tests/token-allowlist-cache.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { TokenAllowlistCacheService } from '../src/services/token-allowlist-cache.service';

describe('TokenAllowlistCacheService', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    TokenAllowlistCacheService.reset();
  });

  it('allows default seeded tokens like XLM and USDC', () => {
    const isXlmAllowed = TokenAllowlistCacheService.isTokenAllowed(
      'CAS3J7GYLGXMF6TDJBBYYSE3HQ6BBSMLNUQ34T6TZMYMW2EVH34XOWMA'
    );
    expect(isXlmAllowed).toBe(true);

    const xlmMeta = TokenAllowlistCacheService.getTokenMetadata(
      'CAS3J7GYLGXMF6TDJBBYYSE3HQ6BBSMLNUQ34T6TZMYMW2EVH34XOWMA'
    );
    expect(xlmMeta?.symbol).toBe('XLM');
    expect(xlmMeta?.decimals).toBe(7);
  });

  it('registers custom tokens with TTL', () => {
    const customToken = 'CDLZFC3SYJYDZT7K67VZ75HPJVIEUVNIXF47ZG2FB2RMQQVU2HHGCYSC';
    const now = 1_000_000;

    TokenAllowlistCacheService.registerToken(
      customToken,
      'SPLASH',
      7,
      'GAEXAMPLE',
      60_000,
      now
    );

    expect(TokenAllowlistCacheService.isTokenAllowed(customToken, now + 10_000)).toBe(true);
    expect(TokenAllowlistCacheService.isTokenAllowed(customToken, now + 70_000)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('removes tokens upon explicit request', () => {
    const usdc = 'CAQCFVLOBK5GIULPNZCGATSTPCHLNBHHCEYLZTG2NOX7I2L46MDQH2XU';
    expect(TokenAllowlistCacheService.isTokenAllowed(usdc)).toBe(true);

    const removed = TokenAllowlistCacheService.removeToken(usdc);
    expect(removed).toBe(true);
    expect(TokenAllowlistCacheService.isTokenAllowed(usdc)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('clears expired tokens correctly', () => {
    const token1 = 'TOKEN_EXPIRED';
    const now = 2_000_000;
    TokenAllowlistCacheService.registerToken(token1, 'EXP', 7, undefined, 5_000, now);

    const pruned = TokenAllowlistCacheService.clearExpired(now + 10_000);
    expect(pruned).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(TokenAllowlistCacheService.isTokenAllowed(token1, now + 10_000)).toBe(false);
  });
});

```

`tests/tournament-cloning.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { TournamentCloningService } from '../src/services/tournament-cloning.service';
import { StoreService } from '../src/services/store.service';
import { Tournament } from '../src/types';

describe('TournamentCloningService', () => {
  const creator = 'GBBD47IF6LWK7P7MDEVSCWR7DPUWV3NY3DTQEVFL4NAT4AQH3ZLLFLA5';
  const sourceTournament: Tournament = {
    id: 'source-100',
    name: 'Weekly Championship #001',
    description: 'Weekly tournament #001',
    gameId: 'splash-rush',
    gameVersion: '1.0.0',
    creatorWallet: creator,
    prizePoolAmount: 500,
    prizeAsset: 'XLM',
    vaultAddress: 'CACHWJEZY6JN36VFACWRQ7FP4T5EPRRRNBVLOSFHXTJHETZY36JD7QYJ',
    maxParticipants: 32,
    currentParticipants: 10,
    startTime: 1000,
    endTime: 10000,
    state: 'FINALIZED',
    createdAt: 500,
    updatedAt: 10000,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    StoreService.saveTournament(sourceTournament);
  });

  it('clones a tournament template successfully with reset state', () => {
    const cloned = TournamentCloningService.cloneTournament({
      sourceTournamentId: 'source-100',
      newTournamentId: 'clone-101',
      title: 'Weekly Championship #002',
      startTime: 20000,
      endTime: 90000,
      creatorWallet: creator,
      prizePoolAmount: 750,
    });

    expect(cloned.id).toBe('clone-101');
    expect(cloned.name).toBe('Weekly Championship #002');
    expect(cloned.state).toBe('DRAFT');
    expect(cloned.currentParticipants).toBe(0);
    expect(cloned.prizePoolAmount).toBe(750);
    expect(cloned.maxParticipants).toBe(32);
    expect(cloned.gameId).toBe('splash-rush');
  });

  it('rejects unauthorized cloning by non-creator', () => {
    expect(() =>
      TournamentCloningService.cloneTournament({
        sourceTournamentId: 'source-100',
        newTournamentId: 'clone-102',
        title: 'Unauthorized Clone',
        startTime: 20000,
        endTime: 90000,
        creatorWallet: 'OTHER_WALLET',
      })
    ).toThrowError(/Unauthorized/);
  });

  it('rejects invalid duration timebounds', () => {
    expect(() =>
      TournamentCloningService.cloneTournament({
        sourceTournamentId: 'source-100',
        newTournamentId: 'clone-103',
        title: 'Too Short Event',
        startTime: 20000,
        endTime: 20010, // Only 10s
        creatorWallet: creator,
      })
    ).toThrowError(/Invalid timebounds/);
  });
});

```

`tests/webhook-dispatcher.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { WebhookDispatcherService } from '../src/services/webhook-dispatcher.service';

describe('WebhookDispatcherService', () => {
  let service: WebhookDispatcherService;

  beforeEach(() => {
    service = new WebhookDispatcherService();
  });

  it('registers and retrieves webhooks', () => {
    const reg = service.registerWebhook('http://listener.test/webhook', ['TOURNAMENT_SETTLED']);
    expect(reg.id).toBeDefined();
    expect(reg.secret).toBeDefined();
    expect(reg.events).toContain('TOURNAMENT_SETTLED');

    const list = service.getWebhooks();
    expect(list).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(list[0].id).toBe(reg.id);
  });

  it('generates reproducible HMAC-SHA256 signature', () => {
    const secret = 'MY_TEST_SECRET';
    const payload = JSON.stringify({ message: 'hello' });
    const sig1 = service.createSignature(payload, secret);
    const sig2 = service.createSignature(payload, secret);

    expect(sig1).toBe(sig2);
    expect(sig1).toHaveLength(64); // SHA-256 hex
  });

  it('dispatches events only to subscribed webhooks and tracks history', async () => {
    service.registerWebhook('http://listener.test/settled', ['TOURNAMENT_SETTLED'], 'secret_1');
    service.registerWebhook('http://listener.test/created', ['TOURNAMENT_CREATED'], 'secret_2');

    const originalFetch = global.fetch;
    global.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      status: 200,
    } as any);

    try {
      const attempts = await service.dispatchEvent('TOURNAMENT_SETTLED', { tournamentId: 101 });
      expect(attempts).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(attempts[0].success).toBe(true);
      expect(attempts[0].statusCode).toBe(200);

      const history = service.getHistory();
      expect(history).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(history[0].eventId).toBeDefined();
    } finally {
      global.fetch = originalFetch;
    }
  });
});
```
